This search revealed that contour weighting dominates registration accuracy for tissue types with large lateral deformation, while the algorithm operates over a broad range of parameter combinations.

中文摘要翻译: 全球每年有89万新发病例，头颈鳞状细胞癌是实体恶性肿瘤中复发率最高的癌症之一。尽管冷冻切片分析是术中切缘评估的标准方法，但由于切除标本与其切除床之间的对准不精确，加之切除后黏膜组织收缩，准确地在切除床上定位检测到的阳性切缘仍具挑战性。我们提出了一种基于力学驱动的可变形配准框架，通过纠正切除后组织变形来提供术中引导。该方法基于正则化Kelvinlet基函数的可变形配准方法，将3D标本网格注册到术中切除床点云上。该配准过程同时匹配表面点云、基准标记物以及边界轮廓约束，其中边界约束直接惩罚标本与切除床边界之间的垂直距离偏差。在来自皮肤、颊黏膜和舌部位的9个标本上，刚性配准的整体平均目标配准误差为11.11±4.07mm，使用无轮廓约束的可变形配准后降至8.20±2.68mm（降低26.19%），而所提出的轮廓约束可变形配准则进一步将误差降至5.62±2.28mm，较刚性配准降低了49.41%。在最具临床挑战性的舌标本上观察到了最大改善。我们还进行了系统的两阶段参数搜索，以表征表面配准、基准点对应、轮廓约束和应变能正则化的相对重要性。该搜索揭示，对于侧向变形较大的组织类型，轮廓权重主导配准精度，而算法在广泛的参数组合范围内均能稳定运行。

</details>


### [117] [SIMBA: ABidirectional Retrieval Forward Simulation Framework for Modeling FY-4A GIIRS Hyperspectral Infrared Radiances Toward NWP Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19943)
*Jingdong Shen,Fu Wang*,Qifeng Lu,Hao Huang,Chunqiang Wu,Chi Yang,Xiaofang Liu*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出SIMBA框架，联合进行大气廓线反演与高光谱红外辐射模拟，通过双向Mamba模块和循环一致性约束，在FY-4A GIIRS数据上优于多个深度学习基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有深度学习方法主要关注从辐射到大气廓线的单向反演，忽视了反向辐射模拟过程以及大气状态空间与辐射观测空间之间的一致性。

Method: 提出SIMBA，一种统一的双向反演-前向模拟框架，联合进行大气廓线反演与辐射重建，引入循环一致性约束加强两个过程的耦合，采用双向Mamba状态空间模块沿气压层捕获长程依赖关系。

Result: 在温度反演、比湿反演、长波辐射重建和中波辐射重建四个任务上，SIMBA优于多个代表性深度学习基线；消融实验验证了双向设计和循环一致性机制的贡献。

Conclusion: 所提框架能有效联合大气廓线反演与高光谱红外辐射建模，在未来雅可比矩阵相关分析和面向数值天气预报的扩展方面具有潜力。

Original Abstract: Hyperspectral infrared observations are an important data source for numerical weather prediction (NWP) because they provide rich information on the vertical structure of atmospheric temperature and humidity. However, most existing deep learning methods mainly focus on one-way retrieval from radiances to atmospheric profiles, while the reverse radiance simulation process and the consistency between atmospheric state space and radiance observation space are insufficiently considered. In this study, we propose SIMBA, a unified bidirectional retrieval-forward simulation framework for FY-4A GIIRS hyperspectral infrared radiance modeling toward NWP applications. The framework jointly performs atmospheric profile retrieval and radiance reconstruction, introduces a cycle-consistency constraint to strengthen the coupling between the two processes, and employs a bidirectional Mamba state-space module to capture long-range dependencies along pressure levels. Using collocated FY-4A GIIRS observations and ERA5 reanalysis data, the proposed method is evaluated for temperature retrieval, specific humidity retrieval, long-wave radiance reconstruction, and medium-wave radiance reconstruction. Experimental results show that SIMBA outperforms several representative deep learning baselines across both retrieval and reconstruction tasks, while ablation experiments confirm the contribution of the bidirectional design and cycle-consistency mechanism. These results demonstrate that the proposed framework is effective for joint atmospheric profile retrieval and hyperspectral infrared radiance modeling, and suggest potential for future Jacobian-related analysis and NWP-oriented extensions.

中文摘要翻译: 高光谱红外观测因其能提供大气温度和湿度垂直结构的丰富信息，成为数值天气预报的重要数据源。然而，现有深度学习方法主要关注从辐射亮温到大气廓线的单向反演，而反向辐射模拟过程以及大气状态空间与辐射观测空间之间的一致性未得到充分考虑。本研究提出SIMBA，一种面向FY-4A GIIRS高光谱红外辐射建模的统一双向反演-前向模拟框架，旨在服务数值天气预报应用。该框架联合执行大气廓线反演与辐射重建，引入循环一致性约束以加强两个过程之间的耦合，并采用双向Mamba状态空间模块沿气压层捕获长程依赖关系。利用FY-4A GIIRS观测与ERA5再分析资料的配对数据，在温度反演、比湿反演、长波辐射重建和中波辐射重建任务上对所提方法进行了评估。实验结果表明，SIMBA在反演和重建任务上均优于多个代表性深度学习基线，消融实验也证实了双向设计与循环一致性机制的贡献。这些结果证明所提框架能有效实现联合大气廓线反演与高光谱红外辐射建模，并显示出未来在雅可比矩阵相关分析以及面向数值天气预报扩展方面的潜力。

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [Computational Identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19361)
*Lucius E. J. Bynum,Rajesh Ranganath,Kyunghyun Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“计算可识别性”框架，将传统理论可识别性从无限数据假设扩展到有限计算资源下的实际可识别性，通过有限搜索程序寻找经验估计器，并在小样本、模糊图标准则、混合观测-干预数据等场景中验证其有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统因果识别理论依赖于渐近性质和无限数据等理想化条件，但在实际应用中（如小样本、模糊图结构、混合数据类型）这些假设难以满足。论文旨在弥合理论可识别性与实际计算可行性之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出“计算可识别性”框架，定义有限计算搜索程序：在指定先验分布和搜索过程条件下，通过有限搜索为经验估计器寻找可行解；若能在期望误差容限内找到估计器，则视为满足可识别性。

Result: 通过多项实验展示了该框架在细粒度实际识别问题上的有效性，包括：小有限样本下的识别、模糊图标准则下的识别、混合观测-干预数据的识别，以及反事实数据和估计量的识别。

Conclusion: 计算可识别性框架能够回答传统理论可识别性无法处理的实用性问题，为在有限数据和计算资源条件下的因果识别提供了可行的新途径。

Original Abstract: Identification conditions describe the computability of a target query or parameter of interest as a function of the type and amount of information available. In causal identification, this information is often expressed in the form of a causal graph, and data are observed or collected for some subset of variables in the graph. Target queries may be for a single effect alone or for a class of effects in a given model. The derivation of an identification algorithm then defines mathematically the process by which the desired causal effect(s) can be uniquely determined, theoretically, in expectation. Identifiability in expectation, or 'theoretical identifiability,' generally assumes asymptotic properties, infinite data, or other mathematically idealized conditions. In this paper, we explore a fundamental distinction between this theoretical, idealized notion of identifiability and a proposed alternative that is computation-bound. The framework we propose - 'computational identifiability' - is to instead define a finite computational search procedure for an empirical estimator. If this process finds an estimator empirically, within a desired error tolerance, then identifiability is satisfied, conditional on the specified assumptions of the search (i.e., a prior distribution over the parameters) and conditional on the search procedure itself. Through several experiments, we demonstrate how this framework allows us to answer fine-grained, practical identification questions, such as identification with small finite samples, with ambiguous graphical criteria, with mixed observational-interventional data, and across counterfactual data and estimands. Code is available at https://github.com/lbynum/metadentify.

中文摘要翻译: 识别条件描述了目标查询或感兴趣参数作为可用信息类型和数量的函数的可计算性。在因果识别中，这些信息通常以因果图的形式表达，并且数据是从图中部分变量观测或收集而来。目标查询可以是单一效应，也可以是给定模型中的一类效应。识别算法的推导在数学上定义了预期中理论上唯一确定所需因果效应的过程。期望意义上的可识别性，即"理论可识别性"，通常假设渐近性质、无限数据或其他数学理想化条件。本文探讨了这种理论理想化的可识别性概念与一种受限于计算的替代方案之间的根本区别。我们提出的框架——"计算可识别性"——将可识别性重新定义为针对经验估计器的有限计算搜索过程。如果该过程在期望误差容限内找到了一个经验估计器，则在所指定的搜索假设（即参数上的先验分布）和搜索过程本身条件下，可识别性得以满足。通过多项实验，我们展示了该框架如何回答细粒度、实用性的识别问题，例如：小有限样本下的识别、模糊图标准则下的识别、混合观测-干预数据下的识别，以及跨反事实数据和估计量的识别。代码可在 https://github.com/lbynum/metadentify 获取。

</details>


### [119] [When to Trust, How to Distill: Multi-Foundation Model Guidance for Lightweight, Robust Scientific Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19363)
*Rupasree Dey,Abdul Matin,Nathan Orwick,Yao Zhang,Shrideep Pallickara,Sangmi Lee Pallickara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Guard框架，通过实例级自适应多教师蒸馏，利用领域不匹配的大模型（教师）来训练轻量级专用预测器，在四个气候关键领域显著降低RMSE，适用于边缘计算部署。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）在物理科学部署中存在两难困境：零样本时存在严重分布偏移，且计算成本过高无法用于边缘传感网络。需要一种方法从这些领域不匹配的基础模型中提取潜在结构知识，训练轻量级专用预测器。

Method: 提出Guard（Gated Uncertainty-Aware Routing for Distillation）框架，将多教师蒸馏重构为实例级决策过程，包含两个自适应机制：(1) 上下文路由（Contextual Router）基于局部输入统计动态选择最相关的教师模型，利用不同基础模型的互补性；(2) 不确定性门控温度（Uncertainty-Gated Temperature）作为"断路器"，当教师置信度偏离领域现实时自动减弱蒸馏强度。

Result: 在气象学、生态系统碳通量、土壤湿度和能源网格四个气候关键领域，Guard相比固定权重的多教师蒸馏基线显著降低RMSE。即使在教师模型零样本精度不佳的情况下，仍能成功蒸馏知识。领域不匹配的教师模型在最困难的28.5%样本上优于全局最优的基础模型。

Conclusion: 领域不匹配的基础模型仍可作为关键的纠正信号，通过Guard框架蒸馏出有用知识，实现适用于资源受限边缘部署的高精度科学预测。

Original Abstract: The deployment of Time-Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) in physical sciences is hindered by a critical trade-off: while these models encode rich, universal temporal dynamics, they suffer from severe distributional misalignment when applied zero-shot to specific scientific domains, and their computational cost prohibits deployment in edge-computing sensor networks. We address a fundamental challenge: How can we extract latent structural knowledge from misaligned foundation models (FM) to train lightweight, specialized forecasters? We propose Gated Uncertainty-Aware Routing for Distillation (Guard), a novel framework that reframes multiteacher distillation as an instance-wise decision process with two adaptive mechanisms: (1) a Contextual Router that dynamically selects the most relevant teacher based on local input statistics, exploiting complementarity across diverse foundation models; and (2) an Uncertainty-Gated Temperature mechanism that acts as a "circuit-breaker," automatically attenuating distillation strength when teacher confidence diverges from domain reality. We evaluate our proposed lightweight framework on four climate-critical domains: meteorology, ecosystem carbon flux, soil moisture, and energy grids. Our method significantly reduces RMSE relative to a fixed-weight multi-teacher distillation baseline, successfully distilling knowledge from pretrained FMs (teachers) even when they exhibit suboptimal zero-shot accuracy due to distribution shift between the original and target data domains. We demonstrate that these domain-misaligned teachers can still serve as critical correctives, outperforming the globally superior FMs on 28.5% of the hardest instances. Ultimately, this enables high-precision scientific forecasting suitable for resource-constrained edge deployment. Code is available at https://github.com/RupasreeDey/GUARD-KDD2026.

中文摘要翻译: 时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）在物理科学中的部署受到一个关键权衡的阻碍：虽然这些模型编码了丰富、通用的时间动态，但当零样本应用于特定科学领域时，它们会遭受严重的分布性错配，而且其计算成本也阻碍了在边缘计算传感器网络中的部署。我们解决一个根本性挑战：如何从错配的基础模型（FM）中提取潜在结构知识，以训练轻量级专用预测器？我们提出Guard（Gated Uncertainty-Aware Routing for Distillation，门控不确定性感知蒸馏路由），这是一个新颖框架，将多教师蒸馏重构为实例级决策过程，包含两种自适应机制：（1）上下文路由器，基于局部输入统计动态选择最相关的教师，利用不同基础模型之间的互补性；（2）不确定性门控温度机制，作为"断路器"，当教师置信度偏离领域现实时自动减弱蒸馏强度。我们在四个气候关键领域评估了我们的轻量级框架：气象学、生态系统碳通量、土壤湿度和能源网格。我们的方法相对于固定权重的多教师蒸馏基线显著降低了RMSE，成功从预训练FM（教师）中蒸馏知识，即使这些教师因原始数据域与目标数据域之间的分布偏移而表现出次优的零样本精度。我们证明，这些领域错配的教师仍然可以充当关键的修正信号，在最困难的28.5%实例上优于全局最优的FM。最终，这实现了适用于资源受限边缘部署的高精度科学预测。代码可在 https://github.com/RupasreeDey/GUARD-KDD2026 获取。

</details>


### [120] [Closing the Social-Semantic Gap: SPSD for Edge-Based Prompt Compression in Cloud LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19364)
*Abhinit Sen,Ajeet Kumar,Manaranjan Pradhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SPSD（情感保留语义蒸馏）方法，利用边缘端的4比特量化小语言模型压缩用户提示词中的社交填充文本，从而减少云端大语言模型的输入token成本，同时保证响应质量在非劣效性范围内。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 大语言模型推理的预填充阶段能耗持续增长，而用户提示中大量包含社交填充文本（如礼貌用语、客套铺垫等），这类文本对人际交流重要但对机器推理信息量低，存在"社会-语义鸿沟"，因此需要通过压缩减少不必要的计算和能源开销。

Method: SPSD是一个基于边缘端的流水线，使用4比特量化的小语言模型（Gemma-2-2B-Instruct Q4_K_M）在用户提示传输到云端大语言模型之前进行压缩，通过情感保留语义蒸馏去除社交填充文本；对安全关键领域采用基于规则的直通路由。

Result: 在248个提示的语料库上，每次蒸馏调用平均节省99.9个输入token，所有146次蒸馏调用均产生正向节省；响应质量通过盲审LLM评判，43%平局、28%蒸馏胜出、29%原始胜出，非劣效于原始路径；余弦相似度均值0.682，中位数0.712；每次调用净节能估计为70-270微瓦时。

Conclusion: 设备端提示蒸馏可以在实际非劣效性范围内减少云端大语言模型的输入token成本，同时保持响应质量。

Original Abstract: The prefill stage of Large Language Model (LLM) inference is a growing contributor to cloud-scale energy cost. Many consumer-support and conversational prompts contain social scaffolding: politeness markers, apologetic preamble, repetition, and rapport-building language that is important for human communication but carries low marginal information for machine reasoning. We call this discrepancy the Social-Semantic Gap. We present SPSD (Sentiment Preserving Semantic Distillation), an edge-based pipeline that compresses user prompts using a 4-bit quantised Small Language Model before transmission to a cloud-deployed LLM. Evaluation on a 248-prompt corpus using Gemma-2-2B-Instruct (Q4_K_M) as the SLM and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct as the cloud evaluation model yields a mean input token saving of 99.9 tokens per distilled call, with all 146 distilled calls yielding positive savings. Response quality, assessed by blind LLM-as-judge scoring across 121 pairs, is non-inferior to the raw path within a pre-specified 1-point margin on a 15-point rubric; the judge awarded 43 percent ties, 28 percent distilled wins, and 29 percent raw wins. Cosine similarity is mixed: mean 0.682, median 0.712, with 54.1 percent of pairs above the 0.70 reference threshold. Safety-critical domains are conservatively routed to passthrough via rule-based gates. Per-call net energy saving is estimated at 70-270 uWh under stated assumptions. SPSD shows that on-device prompt distillation can reduce cloud LLM input-token cost while preserving response quality within a practical non-inferiority margin.

中文摘要翻译: 大语言模型推理的预填充阶段是云规模能源成本日益增长的贡献者。许多面向消费者支持和对话式提示包含社交填充文本：礼貌标记、道歉性开场白、重复表述以及建立融洽关系的语言，这些对人类沟通至关重要，但对机器推理而言携带的低边际信息量较少。我们将这种差异称为"社会-语义鸿沟"。本文提出SPSD（情感保留语义蒸馏），一种基于边缘端的流水线，在传输至云端部署的大语言模型之前，使用4比特量化的小语言模型对用户提示进行压缩。在包含248个提示的语料库上，使用Gemma-2-2B-Instruct（Q4_K_M）作为小语言模型、Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct作为云端评估模型的评估结果表明，每次蒸馏调用平均节省99.9个输入token，所有146次蒸馏调用均产生正向节省。通过盲审LLM评判对121对样本进行的响应质量评估显示，在15分制评分标准的1分预指定非劣效性边界内，蒸馏路径不劣于原始路径；评判结果中43%为平局、28%为蒸馏胜出、29%为原始胜出。余弦相似度结果存在差异：均值0.682，中位数0.712，其中54.1%的配对高于0.70的参考阈值。安全关键领域通过基于规则的网关保守地路由至直通模式。在既定假设下，每次调用的净节能估计为70-270微瓦时。SPSD表明，设备端提示蒸馏可以在保持实际非劣效性边界内的响应质量的同时，减少云端大语言模型的输入token成本。

</details>


### [121] [Performance Analysis and Optimization of 3D Generative Diffusion Models across GPU Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19365)
*Jeeho Ryoo,Yongchan Jung,Muhammad Ali Khaliq,Weidong Zhang,Jiatong Han,Byeong Kil Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对医学扩散模型Med-DDPM在三代NVIDIA架构上进行了全面的内核级性能分析，发现训练主要受cuDNN卷积和隐式GEMM内核主导，存在内存访问模式、张量布局转换和Tensor Core利用率不足等瓶颈。基于这些发现，采用TF32 Tensor Core激活和3D channels-last布局两种优化，在A100上实现了SM周期减少100倍、动态指令减少100倍、Tensor Core利用率提升至9.98倍、IPC提升7%，且不损失合成质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 扩散模型在高质量3D MRI合成中表现出色，但每样本需要数百次U-Net评估，且内核行为高度异构，导致GPU资源需求巨大。当前缺乏对其内核级性能瓶颈的系统分析，亟需深入理解运行时特征以指导优化。

Method: 对Med-DDPM在三代NVIDIA架构上进行详细的性能分析，包括内核级运行时分解、指令混合特征、内存系统利用率、warp级活动和分析器优先级评分估计。基于分析结果，评估两种架构感知优化：TF32 Tensor Core激活和3D channels-last布局。

Result: 训练主要被cuDNN卷积和隐式GEMM内核主导，瓶颈来自内存访问模式、张量布局转换和Tensor Core利用率低。优化后在A100上：SM周期减少100倍，动态指令减少100倍，Tensor Core利用率从1.45提升至9.98倍，IPC提升7%，且合成质量无损。

Conclusion: 通过架构感知优化（TF32 Tensor Core和3D channels-last布局），可以大幅提升医学扩散模型的GPU利用效率，在不牺牲合成质量的前提下实现数量级的性能提升。

Original Abstract: Diffusion models have become essential for high-fidelity 3D MRI synthesis, yet their deployment remains constrained by substantial GPU resource demands arising from hundreds of U-Net evaluations per sample and a highly heterogeneous kernel behavior. This paper performs a comprehensive performance analysis of the state-of-the-art medical diffusion model, Med-DDPM, across three generations of NVIDIA architectures to study kernel-level runtime breakdowns, instruction-mix characteristics, memory system utilization, warp-level activities, and profiler priority-score estimates. We show that training is overwhelmingly dominated by cuDNN convolution and implicit-GEMM kernels, with inefficiencies arising from memory-access patterns, tensor-layout conversions, and limited Tensor Core utilization. Guided by these insights, we evaluate two architecture-aware optimizations TF32 Tensor Core activation and a 3D channels-last layout and demonstrate that they reduce SM cycles by up to 100x, cut dynamic instructions by 100x, raise Tensor Core utilization from 1.45 to 9.98x, and increase IPC by 7% on A100, all without degrading synthesis quality.

中文摘要翻译: 扩散模型已成为高保真3D MRI合成的关键技术，但由于每样本需要数百次U-Net评估且内核行为高度异构，其部署受到巨大的GPU资源需求制约。本文对最先进的医学扩散模型Med-DDPM在三代NVIDIA架构上进行了全面的性能分析，研究了内核级运行时分解、指令混合特征、内存系统利用率、warp级活动以及分析器优先级评分估计。我们发现训练主要受cuDNN卷积和隐式GEMM内核主导，效率低下源于内存访问模式、张量布局转换和有限的Tensor Core利用率。基于这些发现，我们评估了两种架构感知优化——TF32 Tensor Core激活和3D channels-last布局，并证明它们在A100上将SM周期减少了100倍，动态指令削减了100倍，Tensor Core利用率从1.45倍提升至9.98倍，IPC提高了7%，且完全不损害合成质量。

</details>


### [122] [Information Lattice Learning as Probabilistic Graphical Model Structure Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19366)
*Haizi Yu,Lav R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了信息格学习（ILL）与概率图模型之间的联系，证明ILL学习到的概率规则可解释为基于商变量的约束因子图，其L2均匀性提升等价于最大熵原理，从而将ILL定位为可解释约束因子图的结构学习方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 阐明ILL与概率图模型（PGM）之间的形式化联系，为ILL提供严谨的概率图模型理论基础，并探索其与最大熵模型的深层关系。

Method: 通过将ILL中的划分视为确定性商变量（quotient variable），将规则视为商变量的边际分布，将规则集视为可解释抽象上的边际约束集合；将一般提升（general lifting）定义为满足约束的所有联合分布的可行族，特殊提升（special lifting）采用基于L2均匀性原理的最大无知重构；在香农熵提升下，将相同约束转化为以学习到的抽象为索引的对数线性因子图。

Result: 建立了ILL与概率图模型的形式化等价关系：ILL的规则对应于PGM中的边际约束，L2均匀性提升对应于最大熵原理，香农熵提升下的ILL等价于对数线性因子图。同时指出信息格本身不是贝叶斯网络，其边编码的是抽象的细化与粗化关系而非条件依赖关系。

Conclusion: ILL应被视为基于商变量的可解释约束因子图的结构学习方法，这一观点澄清了ILL与图模型及最大熵模型的关系，并为推理、可辨识性及混合符号-概率学习指明了新方向。

Original Abstract: Information lattice learning (ILL) learns interpretable rules of a signal by alternately projecting the signal onto a partition lattice that encodes a hierarchy of abstractions and lifting selected rules back to the signal domain. When the signal is a probability mass function, we show the probabilistic rules learned by ILL admit a natural probabilistic graphical model (PGM) interpretation and develop this interpretation in detail. A partition in ILL induces a deterministic quotient variable, and a rule is the marginal law of that quotient variable. A rule set is therefore a collection of marginal constraints over interpretable abstractions. General lifting is the feasible family of all joint distributions satisfying those constraints, while special lifting chooses a maximum-ignorance reconstruction, implemented in ILL by an L2 uniformity principle closely related to maximum entropy. Under a Shannon-entropy lifting, the same constraints yield a log-linear factor graph whose factors are indexed by learned abstractions. The information lattice itself, however, is not a Bayesian network: its edges encode refinement and coarsening of abstractions, not conditional dependence. Thus ILL is best viewed as structure learning for interpretable constraint-based factor graphs over quotient variables. This view clarifies how ILL relates to graphical models and maximum entropy models, while suggesting new directions for inference, identifiability, and hybrid symbolic-probabilistic learning.

中文摘要翻译: 信息格学习（ILL）通过将信号交替投影到编码抽象层次结构的划分格上，并将选中的规则提升回信号域，来学习信号的可解释规则。当信号为概率质量函数时，我们证明ILL学习到的概率规则具有自然的概率图模型（PGM）解释，并详细阐述了这一解释。ILL中的划分诱导出一个确定性商变量，一条规则即为该商变量的边际分布。因此，规则集是在可解释抽象上的一组边际约束。一般提升是满足这些约束的所有联合分布的可行族，而特殊提升则选择最大无知重建，这通过ILL中与最大熵密切相关的L2均匀性原理实现。在香农熵提升下，相同的约束产生一个对数线性因子图，其因子以学习到的抽象为索引。然而，信息格本身并非贝叶斯网络：其边编码的是抽象的细化与粗化关系，而非条件依赖关系。因此，ILL最好被视为基于商变量的可解释约束因子图的结构学习。这一观点阐明了ILL与图模型及最大熵模型的关系，同时为推理、可辨识性及混合符号-概率学习指明了新方向。

</details>


### [123] [Weibull Weight-Scale Parameter Evolution under AdamW Training Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19367)
*Tiexin Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在双参数Weibull框架下研究AdamW训练中权重分布尺度参数λ(t)先增长后超调再松弛的动力学机制，揭示了三力分解（对齐力、注入力、衰减力）的主导作用，并提出从稀疏检查点恢复对齐力的样条位移方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 理解AdamW优化器训练过程中权重分布尺度参数λ(t)为何呈现增长→超调→松弛的非单调演化模式，揭示其背后的力学驱动机理。

Method: 从AdamW更新中推导出权重的平方范数的三力分解：对齐力（权重与自适应更新方向的相关性）、注入力（自适应步长幅度）和衰减力（解耦权重衰减）。针对真实模型无法获取优化器矩的情况，提出样条位移方法从稀疏检查点恢复对齐力。

Result: 在对齐力主导上升阶段，贡献88-94%的绝对力预算；饱和阶段对齐力与衰减力趋于平衡；样条位移方法以约92-94%的精度恢复对齐力，是朴素两基点基线的约两倍；RMS到Weibull的重构偏移约为5-6%；λ(t)峰值与训练数据一致性有关。

Conclusion: 权重尺度演化由对齐力、注入力和衰减力的动态平衡驱动，其中对齐力是主导因素；所提出的样条位移方法可有效从稀疏检查点分析真实模型；数据一致性影响λ(t)峰值，值得进一步研究。

Original Abstract: Building on a two-parameter Weibull framework for diagnosing transformer weight distributions, we study why the Weibull weight-scale parameter $λ$ grows, overshoots, and then relaxes during AdamW training. We derive a leading-order three-force decomposition of the squared weight norm from the AdamW update: an alignment force measuring the correlation between weights and the adaptive update direction, an injection force from adaptive step magnitude, and a decay force from decoupled weight decay. On self-trained Pythia-70M models with ground-truth optimizer moments, alignment dominates the rise phase, contributing 88-94% of the absolute force budget across four random seeds and remaining robust to super-weight removal. Near saturation, alignment and decay approach balance, explaining the transition from weight-scale growth to relaxation. These force dynamics directly govern the squared-norm component underlying $λ(t)$; the remaining RMS-to-Weibull reconstruction offset is measurable and decomposes into bridge and integration components, totaling approximately 5-6% in densely sampled regions. To extend the analysis to real models where optimizer moments are unavailable, we introduce a spline displacement method that recovers the alignment force from sparse checkpoints with approximately 92-94% accuracy, about twice the naive two-point baseline. We further observe that the peak value of $λ(t)$ varies with training-data coherence in our experiments, suggesting a data-dependent component of weight-scale growth that we leave to a controlled follow-up study. Code and data are available at https://github.com/tiexinding/NPM-Weibull-public.

中文摘要翻译: 基于用于诊断Transformer权重分布的双参数Weibull框架，我们研究了在AdamW训练过程中，Weibull权重尺度参数λ为何会增长、超调然后松弛的原因。我们从AdamW更新中推导出权重平方范数的领头阶三力分解：一个衡量权重与自适应更新方向之间相关性的对齐力，一个来自自适应步长幅度的注入力，以及一个来自解耦权重衰减的衰减力。在具有真实优化器矩的自训练Pythia-70M模型上，对齐力主导了上升阶段，在四个随机种子下贡献了88-94%的绝对力预算，并且对去除超级权重保持鲁棒。在接近饱和时，对齐力与衰减力趋于平衡，这解释了从权重尺度增长到松弛的转变。这些力的动力学直接支配着λ(t)背后的平方范数分量；剩余的RMS到Weibull重构偏移是可测量的，并可分解为桥接分量和积分分量，在密集采样区域总计约5-6%。为了将分析扩展到无法获取优化器矩的真实模型，我们引入了一种样条位移方法，该方法能从稀疏检查点以约92-94%的精度恢复对齐力，其性能约为朴素两基点基线的两倍。我们进一步观察到，在我们的实验中，λ(t)的峰值随训练数据的一致性而变化，这暗示了权重尺度增长存在一个数据依赖的分量，我们将其留待后续受控研究。代码和数据可在https://github.com/tiexinding/NPM-Weibull-public获取。

</details>


### [124] [Zero-Inflated Gaussian Distributions Enable Parameter-Space Sparsity in Estimation-of-Distribution Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19369)
*Andreas Faust,Sven Nitzsche,Juergen Becker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于多元零膨胀高斯分布的分布估计算法（ZIG-EDA），首次将EDA推广到稀疏黑箱优化问题，在不依赖人工设计稀疏算子的情况下，联合优化稀疏模式和活跃参数值，在Lunar Lander基准上取得优于密集高斯EDA和手工稀疏演化算法的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统EDA无法处理稀疏参数空间（即大多数系数恰好为零的问题），现有稀疏黑箱优化器不得不重新引入手工设计的稀疏算子、双层优化方案、阈值截断等操作，这与EDA避免人工设计算子的核心理念相悖。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出多元零膨胀高斯分布作为EDA的采样分布。该分布通过隐高斯模型分别建模指示维度和数值维度，捕获稀疏模式、活跃参数间的相关性以及二者的交互作用，从而无层次地联合优化稀疏模式和活跃参数值。同时引入实用的基于摊销逆推的估计器来估计模型隐参数。

Result: 所提估计器能准确恢复隐相关结构；在Lunar Lander基准上，ZIG-EDA收敛速度更快、最终回报更高，优于密集高斯EDA、手工稀疏演化算法和临时稀疏EDA，且找到的控制器仅需少量参数活跃。

Conclusion: 零膨胀高斯分布可作为EDA的有效采样法则，实现稀疏黑箱优化的免手工设计算子方案，在联合优化稀疏模式与活跃值方面具有显著优势。

Original Abstract: Estimation-of-distribution algorithms (EDAs) are a powerful class of evolutionary methods for black-box optimization, especially when little is known about the structure of the objective. Whereas classical evolutionary algorithms rely on hand-designed mutation and crossover operators, hard to devise for unknown problem structures, and a source of bias, EDAs sidestep operator design entirely: they fit a probability distribution to the best individuals and sample the next generation from it. EDAs are well established on continuous parameter spaces, but they have not previously been generalized to sparse ones, in which most coefficients of a good solution are exactly zero. Existing sparse black-box optimizers therefore reintroduce exactly what EDAs were designed to avoid: hand-crafted sparsity operators, bi-level schemes alternating between support set and active values, zeroing thresholds, and other baked-in assumptions. We close this gap by proposing multivariate zero-inflated Gaussian (ZIG) distributions as EDA sampling laws. A latent Gaussian model with separate indicator and value dimensions represents sparsity patterns, correlations among active parameters, and the interactions between the two, so sparsity patterns and active values are optimized jointly, hierarchy-free. We show that the latent parameters of this model are identifiable from observed samples, unlike in the missing-data settings where related constructions originate, and introduce practical amortized inversion-based estimators for them. The estimators accurately recover latent correlation structures, and on the Lunar Lander benchmark the resulting ZIG-EDA converges faster and reaches higher final returns than a dense Gaussian EDA, a hand-crafted sparse evolutionary algorithm, and an ad-hoc sparse EDA, while finding controllers with only a small fraction of parameters active.

中文摘要翻译: 分布估计算法（EDA）是一类强大的黑箱优化进化方法，尤其适用于对目标结构知之甚少的情形。经典进化算法依赖人工设计的变异和交叉算子，这些算子难以针对未知问题结构设计，且会引入偏差，而EDA完全绕开了算子设计：它根据最优个体拟合概率分布，并据此采样生成下一代个体。EDA在连续参数空间上已得到充分发展，但此前尚未推广到稀疏参数空间——即优秀解中大多数系数恰好为零的场景。现有的稀疏黑箱优化器因此重新引入了EDA本意要避免的内容：手工设计的稀疏算子、在支撑集与活跃值之间交替的双层方案、零阈值等预设假设。本文通过提出多元零膨胀高斯（ZIG）分布作为EDA的采样法则来弥合这一差距。该隐高斯模型分别用独立的指示维度和数值维度来表示稀疏模式、活跃参数间的相关性以及二者的交互作用，从而无需层次结构即可联合优化稀疏模式和活跃参数值。我们证明，与相关构造所源自的缺失数据场景不同，该模型的隐参数可从观测样本中识别，并引入了实用的基于摊销逆推的估计器。这些估计器能准确恢复隐相关结构；在Lunar Lander基准上，所得ZIG-EDA的收敛速度更快、最终回报更高，优于密集高斯EDA、手工稀疏演化算法以及临时稀疏EDA，同时找到的控制器仅有少量参数处于活跃状态。

</details>


### [125] [Human-like autonomy emerges from self-play and a pinch of human data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19370)
*Daphne Cornelisse,Julian Hunt,Zixu Zhang,Waël Doulazmi,Kevin Joseph,Jaime Fernández Fisac,Eugene Vinitsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种结合自对弈强化学习与少量人类演示（仅30分钟）的驾驶策略训练方法，以解决纯自对弈策略与人类驾驶习惯不一致的问题，在单张消费级GPU上15小时内完成训练。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 纯自对弈强化学习训练出的驾驶策略虽然有效，但会学习到与人类驾驶习惯不符的"异类"驾驶行为；现有通过奖励工程和域随机化的缓解方法脆弱且劳动密集。

Method: 在最小安全目标奖励的基础上，将少量人类演示数据（30分钟）作为正则化目标，与自对弈强化学习联合训练，而非完全抛弃人类数据。

Result: 仅需30分钟人类演示（比同类模仿学习方法少2500倍数据），策略能与未见过的真实人类轨迹协同，在单张消费级GPU上15小时内完成训练。

Conclusion: 少量人类数据作为正则化项能有效引导自对弈强化学习策略与人类驾驶习惯对齐，大幅降低对人类数据的依赖，实现高效且行为兼容的驾驶策略训练。

Original Abstract: Self-play reinforcement learning has recently emerged as a way to train driving policies without any human data. It uses cheap, large-scale simulations to substitute expensive, large-scale human driving demonstrations. A key limitation of this approach is that policies trained through pure self-play can learn effective but alien driving conventions incompatible with people. Previous works attempt to mitigate such behavioral misalignments through extensive reward engineering and domain randomization, which are brittle and labor-intensive. Instead of completely discarding human demonstrations, our method treats them as a regularization objective on top of a minimal safe goal-reaching reward. Like the spice in a good stew, we find that a little human data goes a long way: our method uses only 30 minutes of human demonstrations, 2500x fewer than comparable imitation learning approaches. Resulting policies coordinate with held-out human trajectories and complete training in 15 hours on a single consumer-grade GPU. Videos and full source code are available at https://spiced-self-play.com/.

中文摘要翻译: 自对弈强化学习最近成为一种无需任何人类数据即可训练驾驶策略的方法。它利用廉价的大规模仿真来替代昂贵的大规模人类驾驶演示。这种方法的一个关键局限性是，通过纯自对弈训练出的策略可能学会有效但与人兼容性差的异类驾驶习惯。先前的工作试图通过广泛的奖励工程和域随机化来缓解此类行为偏差，但这些方法脆弱且劳动密集。我们的方法没有完全抛弃人类演示，而是将其作为最小安全目标奖励之上的正则化目标。就像好炖菜中的香料一样，我们发现少量人类数据就能发挥巨大作用：我们的方法仅使用30分钟的人类演示，比同类模仿学习方法少2500倍数据。由此产生的策略能与未见过的真实人类轨迹协同，并在单张消费级GPU上15小时内完成训练。视频和完整源代码可在 https://spiced-self-play.com/ 获取。

</details>


### [126] [ProMUSE: Progressive Multi-modal Uncertainty-guided Staged Evidential Alzheimer Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19371)
*Long Doan,Branden Chen,Ethan Litton,Huan Huang,Jiajing Huang,Yixin Xie,Weihua Zhou,Nandakumar Narayanan,Chen Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ProMUSE，一种渐进式多模态不确定性引导的分阶段证据网络，通过自适应判断何时需要额外模态（如MRI/PET）来降低阿尔茨海默病诊断成本，同时保持诊断精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病早期诊断依赖多模态数据（临床评估、MRI、PET），但MRI和PET获取成本高昂且不普及，导致全模态推理在真实临床中不切实际，因此需要一种能自适应决定是否需要昂贵成像模态的方法来降低总体数据采集成本。

Method: ProMUSE首先利用低成本临床数据进行证据分类，通过Dirichlet主观逻辑模型量化不确定性；当不确定性超过学习阈值时，逐步引入MRI或PET特征，并利用Dempster-Shafer理论融合模态层面的信念和不确定性，获得校准的多模态预测。

Result: 在ADNI、AIBL和OASIS数据集上的CN-AD、CN-MCI和MCI-AD任务中，ProMUSE达到与全模态基线相当或更高的准确率，同时将MRI/PET使用量减少50-90%，显著降低成本。

Conclusion: ProMUSE是一种实用、不确定性感知且资源高效的解决方案，适用于真实世界的阿尔茨海默病筛查。

Original Abstract: Alzheimer's disease (AD) is a fatal disorder that destroys memory and cognitive skills in the elderly population. Most treatments for AD are effective in the early stage, leading to an increasing demand for early AD diagnosis. AD diagnosis increasingly relies on multimodal data such as clinical assessments, structural Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI), and Positron Emission Tomography (PET) imaging. However, MRI and PET acquisition remain costly and not universally accessible, making full-modality inference impractical in real-world clinical workflows. We propose ProMUSE, a Progressive Multi-modal Uncertainty Guided Staged Evidential Network that adaptively determines when additional modalities are necessary, helping reduce the overall cost of data acquisition while maintaining accuracy. ProMUSE first performs evidential classification using low-cost clinical data and quantifies uncertainty via a Dirichlet-based subjective logic model. When uncertainty exceeds a learned threshold, ProMUSE progressively incorporates MRI or PET features, fusing modality-wise belief and uncertainty through Dempster-Shafer theory to obtain a calibrated multimodal prediction. This staged acquisition strategy enables accurate diagnosis while minimizing reliance on expensive imaging. Experiments on ADNI, AIBL, and OASIS across CN-AD, CN-MCI, and MCI-AD tasks demonstrate that ProMUSE achieves competitive or superior accuracy compared to full-modality baselines while reducing MRI/PET usage by 50-90%, yielding substantial cost savings. These results highlight ProMUSE as a practical, uncertainty-aware, and resource-efficient solution for real-world AD screening.

中文摘要翻译: 阿尔茨海默病（AD）是一种毁灭性的疾病，会破坏老年人群的记忆和认知能力。大多数AD治疗在早期阶段有效，因此对早期AD诊断的需求日益增长。AD诊断越来越依赖于多模态数据，如临床评估、结构磁共振成像（MRI）和正电子发射断层扫描（PET）成像。然而，MRI和PET的获取仍然昂贵且不易普及，使得全模态推断在实际临床工作流中不切实际。我们提出ProMUSE——一种渐进式多模态不确定性引导的分阶段证据网络，能够自适应地确定何时需要额外模态，从而在保持准确性的同时降低数据采集的总体成本。ProMUSE首先使用低成本的临床数据进行证据分类，并通过基于Dirichlet的主观逻辑模型量化不确定性。当不确定性超过学习阈值时，ProMUSE逐步引入MRI或PET特征，通过Dempster-Shafer理论融合模态层面的信念和不确定性，从而获得校准后的多模态预测。这种分阶段采集策略能够实现准确诊断，同时最小化对昂贵成像的依赖。在ADNI、AIBL和OASIS数据集上的CN-AD、CN-MCI和MCI-AD任务中的实验表明，ProMUSE在实现与全模态基线相当或更高准确率的同时，将MRI/PET的使用量减少了50-90%，带来了显著的成本节约。这些结果突显了ProMUSE作为一种实用、不确定性感知且资源高效的解决方案，适用于真实世界的阿尔茨海默病筛查。

</details>


### [127] [cAPM: Continual AI-Assisted Pace-Mapping with Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19373)
*Dylan O'Hara,Pradeep Bajracharya,Casey Meisenzahl,Karli Gillette,Anton J. Prassl,Gernot Plank,Saman Nazarian,Roderick Tung,John L Sapp,Linwei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出cAPM，一种基于持续学习的AI辅助起搏标测方法，通过在多个室性心动过速（VT）任务间迁移知识，显著减少所需起搏位点数量。在仿真实验中，cAPM以4.5个起搏位点达到81%的定位准确率（5mm容差），远超现有方法（13.7个位点，38%准确率）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有AI辅助起搏标测方法需对每个靶点重新训练，无法在同一个患者或多个患者的多个VT之间迁移知识，导致仍需大量起搏位点。为减少未来靶点VT所需的起搏标测数据量，亟需一种能累积和迁移过往知识的持续学习方法。

Method: cAPM包含三个核心组件：(1) 任务无关的代理神经网络，学习起搏位点到12导联ECG形态的映射；(2) 主动学习策略，为每个靶点选择信息量最大的起搏位点以优化代理模型；(3) 持续学习策略，顺序处理多个任务同时保留先前靶点的知识。

Result: 在包含不同生理条件和心室几何结构的顺序定位任务仿真测试平台上，使用和不使用过往数据回放的cAPM，平均仅需4.5个起搏标测位点即可达到81%的临床容差（5mm精度）定位概率；而现有最先进的主动学习方法需要13.7个起搏位点，仅能达到38%的概率。

Conclusion: cAPM通过持续学习实现了跨VT任务的知识迁移，显著提升了起搏标测效率，为后续向体内临床前和临床研究推进奠定了坚实基础。

Original Abstract: Ventricular tachycardia is a life-threatening rhythm disorder and a major cause of sudden cardiac death. Pace-mapping is a clinical procedure for identifying the intervention target during catheter ablation of VT. It requires clinicians to pace different sites in the ventricles and rapidly interpret the resulting electrocardiograms to determine where to pace next or whether a target site has been identified. Active learning AI models have been proposed to guide clinicians to the next pacing site, showing promise in reducing the number of pacing sites and improving the efficiency of pace-mapping. Existing methods require retraining each target without the ability to transfer knowledge across multiple VTs within the same patient or across patients. We introduce cAPM for continuous AI-assisted pace-mapping to capture and transfer knowledge accumulated from past pace-mapping data to reduce the number of pace-mapping data needed for future target VTs. This is made possible by a task-agnostic surrogate neural network that learns the mapping from pacing sites to 12-lead ECG morphology, an active-learning strategy that refines this surrogate model by selecting the most informative pacing site for each target, and a continual learning strategy to do so sequentially while retaining knowledge from prior targets. Evaluated on an in-silico testbed consisting of sequentially-presented localization tasks across different physiological conditions and ventricular geometries, cAPM with and without replay of past data samples achieved an 81% probability of localizing within clinical tolerance (5 mm accuracy) using 4.5 pace-mapping sites, compared to the state-of-the-art active-learning method achieving 38% probability using 13.7 pacing sites. These results provide a strong basis for preparing cAPM towards in-vivo preclinical and clinical studies where it can be used to guide pace-mapping.

中文摘要翻译: 室性心动过速（VT）是一种危及生命的心律失常，也是心源性猝死的主要原因。起搏标测是导管消融治疗VT过程中识别干预靶点的临床操作，要求临床医生在不同心室位点起搏并快速解读所得心电图，以决定下一步起搏位置或判断是否已找到靶点。主动学习AI模型已被提出用于引导临床医生选择下一个起搏位点，在减少起搏位点数量、提高起搏标测效率方面展现出潜力。然而，现有方法需要对每个靶点重新训练，无法在同一患者或多个患者的多个VT之间迁移知识。我们提出cAPM（持续AI辅助起搏标测），通过捕获并迁移过往起搏标测数据中累积的知识，减少未来靶点VT所需的起搏标测数据量。这一目标通过以下技术实现：一个任务无关的代理神经网络，学习起搏位点到12导联ECG形态的映射；一个主动学习策略，通过为每个靶点选择信息量最大的起搏位点来优化代理模型；以及一个持续学习策略，顺序处理多个任务同时保留先前靶点的知识。在包含不同生理条件和心室几何结构的顺序定位任务仿真测试平台上，使用和不使用过往数据回放的cAPM平均仅需4.5个起搏标测位点即可达到81%的临床容差（5mm精度）定位概率；相比之下，现有最先进的主动学习方法需要13.7个起搏位点，仅能达到38%的概率。这些结果为cAPM向体内临床前和临床研究推进奠定了坚实基础，cAPM有望用于引导起搏标测操作。

</details>


### [128] [Protein Representation Learning with Secondary-Structure and Energy-Filtered Hydrogen-Bond Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19374)
*Mohamed Mouhajir,Limei Wang,El Houcine Bergou,Hajar El Hammouti,Lamiae Azizi,Dongqi Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种二级结构感知的图神经网络（SSProNet），通过引入二级结构标签作为节点特征，并基于能量过滤的氢键构建图边，提升了蛋白质表示学习的性能与可解释性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于图的蛋白质建模方法大多依赖序列邻接或几何邻近性，未能充分反映蛋白质折叠的核心原理。蛋白质的三维构象围绕二级结构元素（α-螺旋和β-折叠）组织，这些元素编码了重复出现的局部基序和稳定的氢键相互作用，现有方法未能有效利用这些结构信息。

Method: 提出二级结构感知的图神经网络（SSProNet）：(1) 残基层面的节点表示中加入二级结构分配标签；(2) 图边基于氢键相互作用构建，并通过能量强度进行过滤。该设计使模型能够同时捕获局部结构上下文和长程耦合信息。

Result: 在常用蛋白质基准上，该方法相较于现有基于图的方法取得了一致的性能提升；同时，所学习的图连接与已知的结构基序对齐，提供了更好的生物学可解释性。

Conclusion: 融合二级结构和能量过滤的氢键拓扑为蛋白质表示学习提供了有效的归纳偏置，表明该方向值得进一步探索。

Original Abstract: Graph-based representations are widely used in protein modeling, yet many existing approaches rely primarily on sequence adjacency or geometric proximity, which only partially reflect the principles governing protein folding. Proteins instead adopt complex three-dimensional conformations organized around secondary structure elements, such as $α$-helices and $β$-sheets, which encode recurring local motifs and stabilizing hydrogen-bond interactions. In this work, we introduce a secondary-structure-aware graph neural network for protein representation learning. Residue-level node representations are augmented with secondary structure assignments, and graph edges are constructed from hydrogen-bond interactions filtered by their energetic strength. This design enables the model to capture both local structural context and long-range couplings that are central to protein stability and function. We evaluate the proposed approach on commonly used protein benchmarks and observe consistent improvements over existing graph-based methods. In addition, the resulting graph representations offer enhanced biological interpretability, as the learned connectivity aligns with established structural motifs. These findings suggest that incorporating secondary structure and energy-filtered hydrogen-bond topology provides an effective inductive bias for protein representation learning. The code is released at https://github.com/mohamedmohamed2021/SSProNet

中文摘要翻译: 基于图的表示在蛋白质建模中被广泛使用，但许多现有方法主要依赖序列邻接性或几何邻近性，这仅部分反映了控制蛋白质折叠的原理。蛋白质实际上采用复杂的三维构象，围绕二级结构元素（如α-螺旋和β-折叠）组织，这些元素编码了重复出现的局部基序和稳定的氢键相互作用。在这项工作中，我们引入了一种二级结构感知的图神经网络用于蛋白质表示学习。残基层面的节点表示增加了二级结构分配信息，图边则基于经能量强度过滤的氢键相互作用构建。这种设计使模型能够同时捕获局部结构上下文和对蛋白质稳定性与功能至关重要的长程耦合。我们在常用的蛋白质基准上评估了所提方法，观察到相较于现有基于图的方法取得了一致的改进。此外，所得到的图表示提供了增强的生物学可解释性，因为学习到的连接性与已知的结构基序对齐。这些发现表明，融入二级结构和能量过滤的氢键拓扑为蛋白质表示学习提供了有效的归纳偏置。代码已发布于 https://github.com/mohamedmohamed2021/SSProNet

</details>


### [129] [Physics-Informed Discovery of Yield Functions in Plasticity via Convex Neural Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19375)
*Hyeonbin Moon,Donghyuk Cho,Jecheon Yu,Jeong Whan Yoon,Seunghwa Ryu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种物理信息驱动的框架，仅从全场位移和反作用力数据中识别各向异性屈服函数，无需应力观测、塑性应变测量或预定义参数形式，通过凸神经网络表示屈服函数并结合可微应力更新与物理信息力平衡损失进行训练。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 各向异性屈服函数的识别面临三大挑战：屈服现象无法直接从全场力学测量中观测、方向性标定需要大量加载方向、选择合适的解析形式非常困难。现有方法依赖应力观测或预先指定的参数形式，限制了其通用性和适用性。

Method: 将屈服函数表示为凸神经网络（强制凸性与一阶正齐次性，并施加拉压对称性），将其嵌入弹塑性应力积分的可微计算流程中，通过多个加载工况下的物理信息力平衡损失进行训练，无需直接的应力空间监督。

Result: 在von Mises、Hill 1948和Yld2000-2d屈服函数的有限元基准测试中验证了框架的有效性，评估了屈服轮廓一致性、位移噪声敏感性、塑性活跃应力状态的可识别性、认知不确定性以及多项式代理部署。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一条力学约束的路径，能够仅从位移和力数据中发现各向异性屈服函数，并将识别出的屈服函数保留在弹塑性应力积分的结构内，具有实际工程应用潜力。

Original Abstract: Identifying anisotropic yield functions remains challenging since yielding is not directly observed in full-field mechanical measurements, directional calibration can require many loading directions, and selecting an appropriate analytical form is nontrivial. This study proposes a physics-informed framework for discovering yield functions from full-field displacement data and reaction force data, without stress observations, plastic strain measurements, direct yield surface data, or a prescribed parametric yield function. The framework identifies the yield function as a mechanically constrained constitutive component inside elastoplastic stress integration, rather than through direct stress-space supervision. The yield function is represented by a convex neural network that enforces convexity and positive homogeneity of degree one while imposing the assumed tension-compression symmetry, and this neural yield function is trained with a differentiable stress update and a physics-informed force equilibrium loss across multiple loading cases. The proposed framework is validated using finite element (FE) benchmark studies with von Mises, Hill 1948, and Yld2000-2d yield functions, assessing yield contour agreement, displacement-noise sensitivity, identifiability through plastically active stress states, epistemic uncertainty, and polynomial-surrogate deployment. This study provides a mechanics-constrained pathway for discovering anisotropic yield functions from displacement and force data while keeping the identified component within the structure of elastoplastic stress integration.

中文摘要翻译: 识别各向异性屈服函数仍然具有挑战性，因为屈服现象无法直接从全场力学测量中观测，方向性标定可能需要大量加载方向，且选择合适的解析形式并非易事。本研究提出了一种物理信息驱动的框架，能够从全场位移数据和反作用力数据中发现屈服函数，无需应力观测、塑性应变测量、直接的屈服面数据或预设的参数化屈服函数。该框架将屈服函数作为力学约束的本构组件嵌入弹塑性应力积分内部进行识别，而非通过直接的应力空间监督。屈服函数由一个凸神经网络表示，该网络强制满足凸性和一阶正齐次性，同时施加假定的拉压对称性；该神经屈服函数通过可微的应力更新和跨多个加载工况的物理信息力平衡损失进行训练。所提出的框架在von Mises、Hill 1948和Yld2000-2d屈服函数的有限元基准研究中得到验证，评估了屈服轮廓一致性、位移噪声敏感性、塑性活跃应力状态的可识别性、认知不确定性以及多项式代理部署。本研究提供了一条力学约束的路径，能够仅从位移和力数据中发现各向异性屈服函数，同时将识别出的组件保留在弹塑性应力积分的结构之内。

</details>


### [130] [Cost-Optimal LLM Routing with Limited User Feedback under User Satisfaction Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19376)
*Herbert Woisetschläger,Arastun Mammadli,Ryan Zhang,Shiqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SLARouter是一种在线路由算法，能从稀疏的单侧用户反馈中学习成本最优策略，在保证严格SLA合规的同时将运营成本降低高达2.2倍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: LLM应用推理成本快速增长，而用户对响应质量有高要求（SLA约束），现有成本感知路由方法依赖完整反馈信号、离线训练、大量调参，且缺乏SLA保证和推理时自适应性。

Method: 提出SLARouter在线路由算法，利用生产系统中稀疏、单侧的用户反馈，学习成本最优策略，并提供了成本最优性和严格SLA合规的理论保证。

Result: 在多种LLM基准测试中，SLARouter无需针对每个基准调参即可满足SLA约束，相比现有基线将运营成本降低高达2.2倍。

Conclusion: SLARouter解决了成本与质量之间的根本矛盾，通过在线学习和理论保证实现了高效、自适应的LLM请求路由。

Original Abstract: Inference costs for large language model (LLM) applications are rapidly growing, driven by surging demand and rising infrastructure cost. Users expect high-quality responses, and in commercial settings this is formally codified in Service Level Agreements (SLAs), creating a fundamental tension between cost and quality. Recent progress on cost-aware LLM request routing has shown potential to resolve this tension, but existing approaches rely on complete feedback signals, offline training, extensive per-workload tuning, and most lack SLA guarantees or inference-time adaptivity. We introduce SLARouter, an online routing algorithm that learns a cost-optimal policy from the sparse, one-sided user feedback available in production systems. SLARouter provides theoretical guarantees for both cost optimality and strict SLA compliance. Experiments across a wide range of LLM benchmarks show that SLARouter satisfies SLA constraints without the need for per-benchmark tuning, reducing operating cost by up to 2.2x over existing baselines.

中文摘要翻译: 大型语言模型（LLM）应用的推理成本正在快速增长，其驱动力来自激增的需求和不断上升的基础设施成本。用户期望获得高质量的响应，在商业环境中这被正式编码为服务等级协议（SLA），从而在成本与质量之间形成了根本性的矛盾。近来，成本感知的LLM请求路由研究展现出解决这一矛盾的潜力，但现有方法依赖完整的反馈信号、离线训练、大量的每工作负载调参，且大多数缺乏SLA保证或推理时的自适应性。我们引入了SLARouter，一种在线路由算法，能够从生产系统中可获得的稀疏、单侧用户反馈中学习成本最优策略。SLARouter为成本最优性和严格的SLA合规性提供了理论保证。在多种LLM基准测试上的实验表明，SLARouter无需针对每个基准进行调参即可满足SLA约束，相比现有基线将运营成本降低高达2.2倍。

</details>


### [131] [Emyx: Fast and efficient all-atom protein generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19377)
*Nicholas J. Williams,Ward Haddadin,Matteo P. Ferla,Constantin Schneider,Nicholas B. Woodall,Ruby Sedgwick,Christian D. Madsen,Andrew L. Hopkins,Edward O. Pyzer-Knapp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Emyx是一个140M参数的基于条件流匹配的酶设计模型，通过轻量条件表示和稀疏连接替代传统复杂架构，在更小模型规模和更低训练成本下，在酶设计基准上超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前全原子生成模型继承自结构预测的昂贵架构，训练成本高且样本多样性有限；而生成模型实际上只需要对稀疏几何约束进行条件化，无需丰富的共进化信号，因此可以大幅简化架构。

Method: 提出Emyx，一个140M参数的condition flow matching模型：1) 用标准Transformer模块集中容量，用轻量条件表示和稀疏连接替代重嵌入堆叠；2) 推导流匹配插值到EDM噪声水平框架的精确重参数化，将流匹配训练效率与扩散模型的最优采样方法桥接，无需重新训练。

Result: Emyx在AME酶设计基准上，在全局折叠恢复和催化几何精度下的成功率、结构新颖性、骨架多样性和几何有效性均超越Proteína-Complexa和RFdiffusion3；训练仅需682 GPU小时，约为RFdiffusion3的1/4。

Conclusion: 生成模型可以大幅简化架构，不需要继承结构预测的复杂设计；通过轻量条件表示和稀疏连接，小模型也能在酶设计任务上取得更优性能，同时显著降低训练成本。

Original Abstract: Computational enzyme design requires generating proteins that scaffold catalytic residues and ligands, a task that demands both geometric accuracy and structural diversity from the underlying generative model. Current all-atom generators inherit expensive architectures from structure prediction, leading to high training costs and limited sample diversity. We argue that much of this complexity is unnecessary for generators, which condition on sparse geometric constraints rather than rich co-evolutionary signals. Emyx is a 140M-parameter conditional flow matching model that concentrates capacity within standard transformer blocks, replacing heavy embedding stacks with lightweight conditional representations and sparse connectivity. We additionally derive an exact reparametrisation of the flow matching interpolant into the EDM noise-level framework, bridging flow matching training efficiency with state-of-the-art sampling methods designed for diffusion models without retraining. Despite being the smallest model, Emyx outperforms both Proteína-Complexa and RFdiffusion3 against the AME enzyme design benchmark across success rate under strict evaluation requiring both global fold recovery and catalytic geometry accuracy, structural novelty, scaffold diversity, and geometric validity, while training in just $682$ GPU-hours, roughly $4\times$ less than RFdiffusion3.

中文摘要翻译: 计算酶设计需要生成能够支撑催化残基和配体的蛋白质，这一任务要求底层生成模型同时具备几何精度和结构多样性。当前的全原子生成模型继承了结构预测中的昂贵架构，导致训练成本高、样本多样性有限。我们认为，生成模型所需的复杂度远低于此——它们仅需对稀疏几何约束进行条件化处理，而非依赖丰富的共进化信号。Emyx是一个1.4亿参数的conditional flow matching模型，将模型容量集中于标准Transformer模块内，用轻量条件表示和稀疏连接替代了厚重的嵌入堆叠。此外，我们推导了流匹配插值到EDM噪声水平框架的精确重参数化方法，在不需重新训练的情况下，将流匹配的训练效率与专为扩散模型设计的最先进采样方法桥接起来。尽管Emyx是最小的模型，它在AME酶设计基准上，在严格要求全局折叠恢复和催化几何精度的成功率、结构新颖性、骨架多样性以及几何有效性方面，均优于Proteína-Complexa和RFdiffusion3，而训练仅需682 GPU小时，约为RFdiffusion3的四分之一。

</details>


### [132] [A Hybrid GNN-FEM Framework for Phase-Field Fracture Simulation. Physics-Preserving Hybridization for Generalizable Surrogate Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19378)
*Hyeonbin Moon,Yongjin Choi,Seunghwa Ryu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出混合GNN-FEM框架，用图神经网络替代相场更新模块，保留FEM位移求解器，实现高效且泛化能力强的相场断裂模拟。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 相场断裂模型虽能鲁棒模拟复杂裂纹演化，但求解耦合非线性历史依赖系统导致计算成本极高，限制了实际应用。

Method: 将图神经网络代理集成到传统交错求解方案中，替代每个载荷增量下的相场更新，同时保留基于FEM的位移求解器以强制执行力学平衡和边界条件；通过无量纲特征设计、基于网格的图结构以及源自相场控制方程的物理信息损失函数实现泛化。

Result: 数值实验表明，与常规FEM相比，混合方法在保持精度的同时降低了计算成本，并在不同几何形状、加载条件、材料属性和离散化下展现出鲁棒的预测性能。

Conclusion: 选择性代理策略通过保留增量求解结构，使框架与历史依赖的断裂演化保持一致，无需代理近似完整解轨迹，是一种物理意义明确且高效的相场断裂模拟方法。

Original Abstract: Scientific machine learning (SciML) has emerged as a promising approach for accelerating simulations of complex physical systems, yet achieving physically consistent and generalizable predictions for nonlinear, history-dependent problems remains a central challenge. In this study, we propose a hybrid GNN--FEM framework for efficient and generalizable phase-field fracture modeling. While phase-field approaches provide a robust variational framework for simulating complex crack evolution, their high computational cost limits practical applications because they require solving coupled, nonlinear, and history-dependent systems within an incremental finite element procedure. To address this challenge, a graph neural network surrogate is integrated into the conventional staggered scheme, replacing the phase-field update at each load increment while retaining the FEM-based displacement solver to enforce mechanical equilibrium and boundary conditions. By preserving the incremental solution structure, the framework remains consistent with history-dependent fracture evolution without requiring the surrogate to approximate the full solution trajectory. This selective surrogate strategy emphasizes the identification of a physically meaningful and incrementally structured learning target, rather than relying on brute-force data generation to learn the full fracture process. The proposed framework achieves strong generalization across varying geometries, loading conditions, material properties, and discretizations through dimensionless feature design, a graph-based formulation on mesh-based domains, and a physics-informed loss derived from the governing phase-field equation. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the hybrid approach reduces computational cost while maintaining accuracy compared with conventional FEM, and exhibits robust predictive performance across diverse problem settings.

中文摘要翻译: 科学机器学习（SciML）已成为加速复杂物理系统仿真的有前景方法，但在非线性、历史依赖问题中实现物理一致且可泛化的预测仍是一个核心挑战。在本研究中，我们提出了一种混合GNN-FEM框架，用于高效且可泛化的相场断裂建模。虽然相场方法为模拟复杂裂纹演化提供了稳健的变分框架，但其高计算成本限制了实际应用，因为需要在增量有限元过程中求解耦合的非线性历史依赖系统。为应对这一挑战，将图神经网络代理集成到传统的交错方案中，在每个载荷增量下替代相场更新，同时保留基于FEM的位移求解器以强制执行力学平衡和边界条件。通过保留增量求解结构，该框架保持与历史依赖的断裂演化一致，而无需代理近似完整的解轨迹。这种选择性代理策略强调识别物理意义明确且增量结构化的学习目标，而非依赖暴力数据生成来学习完整的断裂过程。通过无量纲特征设计、基于网格域的图表示以及源自相场控制方程的物理信息损失函数，所提出框架在变化的几何形状、加载条件、材料属性和离散化下实现了强大的泛化能力。数值实验表明，与常规FEM相比，混合方法在降低计算成本的同时保持了精度，并在多种问题设置下展现出鲁棒的预测性能。

</details>


### [133] [How Linear Is a Transformer Feed-Forward Block? Per-Block Linear Recoverability Is Learned, Not Architectural](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19379)
*Stuart Whipp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出线性可恢复性（R²_lin）指标，用于量化Transformer中FFN层的非线性程度，发现不同层非线性差异巨大且与激活函数无关，并利用该指标指导模型压缩，同时指出线性基线在Transformer上收敛不良的方法论问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: Transformer的FFN层通常被视为非线性计算单元，但训练后的FFN块实际非线性程度从未被精确测量，本文旨在量化这一性质。

Method: 将每个FFN视为位置级输入-输出映射，分解为精确最小二乘线性近似与残差两部分，用线性映射解释的留出方差定义线性可恢复性（R²_lin），该度量不依赖优化器。

Result: 在GPT-2、Pythia-160m和Llama-160m的12个块中，R²_lin高度异质且不随深度单调变化，相邻块从近线性（>0.99）到强非线性（<0.3）；相同宽度和GELU激活的模型R²_lin分布截然不同，说明可恢复性是学习得到的而非架构决定的。低秩双线性探针仅恢复少量R²，增益与残差非线性无关。可恢复块可被大幅压缩（GPT-2早期FFN用8倍少参数仅增加0.77困惑度），低可恢复块则不安全。此外，训练式线性基线在Transformer病态激活上收敛不良。

Conclusion: FFN层的线性可恢复性是训练后习得的属性，可用于指导模型压缩，同时揭示了线性基线在Transformer中的收敛问题，建议使用闭式最小二乘上界。

Original Abstract: Transformer feed-forward networks (FFNs) are often treated as nonlinear stores of computation, yet how nonlinear a trained FFN block actually is has rarely been measured. We treat each FFN as a position-wise input-to-output map and split it into the exact least-squares linear approximation plus a residual. The held-out variance the closed-form linear map explains defines a block's linear recoverability (R^2_lin), an optimiser-free measure of its linearity.
  Across all twelve blocks of GPT-2, Pythia-160m, and llama-160m, R^2_lin is highly heterogeneous and non-monotone with depth, ranging from near-linear (>0.99) to strongly nonlinear (<0.3) between adjacent blocks, and is not set by the activation function: same-width GELU models GPT-2 and Pythia-160m have sharply different profiles, so recoverability is a learned property of individual trained blocks, not an architectural one. A low-rank bilinear probe of the residual recovers only a few points of R^2, with gain uncorrelated with residual nonlinearity: the unrecovered computation is not a single position-wise product but higher-order or distributed structure.
  The measurement also serves as a targeted compression signal: recoverable blocks admit large single-layer replacements (GPT-2's early FFN at 8x fewer parameters for +0.77 perplexity), while low-recoverability blocks flag where this is unsafe. It further exposes a methodological pitfall: trained linear baselines can badly under-converge on ill-conditioned transformer activations, so we report the exact closed-form least-squares ceiling throughout.

中文摘要翻译: Transformer的前馈网络（FFN）通常被视为非线性计算存储单元，但训练后的FFN块实际非线性程度很少被量化。本文将每个FFN视为位置级的输入-输出映射，并将其分解为精确的最小二乘线性近似加上一个残差。用闭式线性映射解释的留出方差定义了一个块的线性可恢复性（R²_lin），这是一个无需优化器的线性度量指标。在GPT-2、Pythia-160m和Llama-160m全部12个块中，R²_lin呈现高度异质性且随深度非单调变化，相邻块之间从近线性（>0.99）到强非线性（<0.3），并且该指标不由激活函数决定：使用相同宽度GELU的GPT-2和Pythia-160m模型具有截然不同的分布，因此可恢复性是单个训练块的学习属性，而非架构属性。对残差进行低秩双线性探针仅恢复少量R²，增益与残差非线性不相关：未被恢复的计算不是单一位置级的乘积，而是高阶或分布式结构。该度量还可作为有针对性的压缩信号：可恢复块可接受大幅单层替换（GPT-2早期FFN用8倍少参数仅增加0.77困惑度），而低可恢复块则标记了不安全的压缩位置。该度量还暴露了一个方法论陷阱：训练式线性基线在Transformer的病态激活上可能严重欠收敛，因此本文在整个研究中报告了精确的闭式最小二乘上界。

</details>


### [134] [VERITAS: Verifier-Guided Proof Search for Zero-Shot Formal Theorem Proving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19399)
*Manish Acharya,Zhenyu Liao,Yueke Zhang,Kevin Leach,Yu Huang,Yifan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VERITAS是一个零样本框架，通过两阶段协议（Best-of-N采样+批评者引导的MCTS）将验证器信号完整反馈到定理证明搜索中，在miniF2F上达到40.6%，并发布了一个55个定理的组合学基准VERITAS-CombiBench，揭示无引导采样在需要从验证器反馈中迭代恢复正确引理名时反而有害。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的基于LLM的形式化证明器通常将丰富的验证器信号（语法错误、类型不匹配、部分目标进展）简化为二元通过/失败信号，丢失了大量对证明搜索有价值的信息。

Method: 提出VERITAS零样本框架，采用两阶段协议：第一阶段进行Best-of-N采样；第二阶段使用批评者引导的MCTS（蒙特卡洛树搜索），将第一阶段的失败案例作为显式负例输入，从而利用验证器反馈进行探索。

Result: 在miniF2F上达到40.6%（独立Best-of-5为36.9%，Portfolio为26.2%）；在发布的55个定理的组合学基准VERITAS-CombiBench上达到7.3%（Best-of-5仅1.8%，Portfolio为3.6%），证明无引导采样在需要从验证器反馈迭代恢复引理名时效果更差。

Conclusion: VERITAS通过将验证器信号完整路由回证明搜索，显著提升了形式化定理证明的性能，特别是在需要从反馈中迭代恢复正确引理名的复杂组合学问题上优势更明显。

Original Abstract: LLM-based formal provers often collapse rich verifier signals (syntax errors, type mismatches, partial goal progress) into a binary pass/fail bit. We present VERITAS, a zero-shot framework that routes every verifier signal back into proof search through a two-phase protocol: Best-of-N sampling first, then a critic-guided MCTS pass that ingests Phase 1 failures as explicit negative examples. The protocol preserves every theorem solved by its own Phase 1 sweep, so Phase 2's additional solves are attributable to feedback-driven exploration. VERITAS reaches 40.6% on miniF2F (vs. an independently run Best-of-5 at 36.9%, Portfolio 26.2%) and 7.3% on VERITAS-CombiBench, a 55-theorem combinatorics benchmark we release on which Best-of-5 (1.8%) falls below Portfolio (3.6%), exposing that unguided sampling hurts when correct lemma names must be recovered iteratively from verifier feedback. Artifacts are available on GitHub.

中文摘要翻译: 基于大语言模型的形式化证明器常常将丰富的验证器信号（如语法错误、类型不匹配、部分目标进展等）压缩为一个二元的通过/失败位。本文提出VERITAS，一个零样本框架，通过两阶段协议将每个验证器信号路由回证明搜索中：首先进行Best-of-N采样，然后进行批评者引导的蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS），将第一阶段失败案例作为显式负例输入。该协议保留了第一阶段自身解决的所有定理，因此第二阶段的额外求解可归因于反馈驱动的探索。VERITAS在miniF2F上达到40.6%（独立运行的Best-of-5为36.9%，Portfolio为26.2%），在VERITAS-CombiBench上达到7.3%，后者是我们发布的包含55个定理的组合学基准，在该基准上Best-of-5（1.8%）低于Portfolio（3.6%），暴露出当需要从验证器反馈中迭代恢复正确的引理名时，无引导采样的效果反而更差。相关代码已发布于GitHub。

</details>


### [135] [Thermodynamic Signatures of Reasoning: Free-Energy and Spectral-Form-Factor Diagnostics for Hallucination Detection in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19404)
*Salim Khazem*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自由能签名（Fes）作为注意力图谱拉普拉斯矩阵的谱描述符，将每个注意力层视为热力学系统并提取配分函数、自由能、谱熵、热容及随机矩阵理论谱形状因子等热力学量，用于检测大语言模型中的幻觉。理论和实验均证明该方法优于现有谱基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于注意力图谱拉普拉斯谱的幻觉检测方法仅使用少量特征值或手工标量，忽略了谱的大部分结构信息，导致检测能力有限。

Method: 将每个注意力层的拉普拉斯矩阵视为哈密顿量，提取其热力学势（配分函数、自由能、谱熵、热容）和随机矩阵理论谱形状因子构成Fes描述符。理论证明了Lipschitz稳定性、表达性增强和有限样本PAC界。实验中使用轻量级探针或RMT偏差分数进行检测。

Result: 在6个开源LLM和6个基准上，基于Fes的轻量级探针取得平均AUROC最优，比LapEig高+6.5点，比GoR-4高+2.4点。无监督RMT偏差分数平均AUROC为0.71。RMT分析显示正确生成呈现Wigner-Dyson谱统计，幻觉呈现Poisson谱统计。

Conclusion: Fes描述符充分利用了注意力图谱拉普拉斯的完整谱结构，在理论和实验上均展现出优越的幻觉检测能力，且无需修改底层LLM。

Original Abstract: Hallucination detection in large language models (LLMs) is deployment-critical, and recent work shows that the spectrum of attention-derived graph Laplacians carries strong signal about reasoning quality. Prior spectral diagnostics, however, summarize the Laplacian spectrum by a handful of eigenvalues or hand-picked scalars, leaving most of its structure unused. We propose Free-Energy Signatures (Fes), a spectral descriptor that treats each layer's attention Laplacian as a Hamiltonian and extracts its thermodynamic potentials partition function, free energy, spectral entropy, heat capacity together with the random-matrix-theory (RMT) spectral form factor. We prove three results: (i)~Lipschitz stability of Fes under attention perturbation; (ii)~an expressiveness result showing that Fes enriches finite spectral summaries and approximates moment-derived spectral functionals under explicit regularity and grid-resolution assumptions; and (iii)~a finite-sample PAC bound on the AUROC of a training-free detector built from Fes. Empirically, across six open-weight LLMs and six benchmarks, a lightweight probe on Fes descriptors achieves the strongest aggregate AUROC among attention-spectral baselines, improving over LapEig by $+6.5$ AUROC points and over GoR-4 by $+2.4$ points on average, while requiring no update to the underlying LLM. In the fully unsupervised setting, an RMT-deviation score achieves mean AUROC $0.71$, providing a label-free but weaker detector. A complementary RMT analysis shows that correct generations exhibit more Wigner-Dyson like spectral statistics, whereas hallucinations exhibit more Poisson-like statistics. The anonymized code and config are provided in the supplementary material.

中文摘要翻译: 大语言模型中的幻觉检测对部署至关重要，近期研究表明注意力图谱拉普拉斯矩阵的谱携带了关于推理质量的强烈信号。然而，现有谱诊断方法仅用少量特征值或手工标量来概括拉普拉斯谱，留下了大部分结构未被利用。我们提出自由能签名（Fes），一种谱描述符，它将每层的注意力拉普拉斯矩阵视为哈密顿量，并提取其热力学势——配分函数、自由能、谱熵、热容——以及随机矩阵理论（RMT）谱形状因子。我们证明了三个结论：(i) Fes在注意力扰动下具有Lipschitz稳定性；(ii) 一个表达性结果，表明Fes丰富了有限谱摘要，并在明确的规则性和网格分辨率假设下逼近基于矩的谱泛函；(iii) 基于Fes构建的无训练检测器AUROC的有限样本PAC界。实验上，在六个开源权重LLM和六个基准上，基于Fes描述符的轻量级探针在注意力谱基线中取得了最强的聚合AUROC，平均比LapEig提高+6.5个AUROC点，比GoR-4提高+2.4个点，且无需更新底层LLM。在完全无监督设置下，RMT偏差分数达到了平均AUROC 0.71，提供了一个无标签但较弱的检测器。补充的RMT分析表明，正确生成表现出更多的Wigner-Dyson型谱统计，而幻觉则表现出更多的Poisson型谱统计。匿名代码和配置已在补充材料中提供。

</details>


### [136] [FlexLAM: Resolving the Bottleneck Trade-off in Latent Action Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19408)
*Takanori Yoshimoto,Yang Hu,Naruya Kondo,Tatsuya Matsushima*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlexLAM提出用可变长度潜动作替代固定容量瓶颈，通过嵌套dropout训练生成前缀有效编码，在相同token预算下匹配或超越固定容量LAM，且支持推理时动态调整token预算，实现了对现有潜动作模型的无架构零成本升级。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有潜动作模型（LAM）使用固定容量瓶颈，面临两难：过于紧凑的编码会丢弃动作对齐所需的过渡线索，而过于宽松的编码会保留额外过渡变异，在标注稀缺时难以解析。作者希望在不引入新架构或损失函数的情况下解决这一问题。

Method: FlexLAM采用嵌套dropout（nested dropout）训练可变长度潜动作，生成前缀有效（prefix-valid）编码，先捕获紧凑的过渡结构，仅在需要时添加细节，无需新架构或新损失函数。

Result: 单一FlexLAM在标准稀缺标注监督和低回报单任务对齐压力测试下，在每个评估的token预算上匹配或超越单独训练的固定容量LAM；支持推理时调整token预算无需重新训练；改善了Ego4D过渡重建效果。

Conclusion: 可变长度潜动作是对固定容量瓶颈的无架构、即插即用升级，可应用于潜动作模型、潜动作世界模型和视频预训练动作接口。

Original Abstract: Latent actions provide a compact interface between action-free video and downstream decision-making, yet existing Latent Action Models (LAMs) force every transition through a fixed-capacity bottleneck. We identify a bottleneck trade-off: overly tight codes can discard transition cues needed for action alignment, while overly loose codes preserve additional transition variation that must be resolved when alignment labels are scarce or narrowly distributed. FlexLAM replaces this fixed capacity with variable-length latent actions trained by nested dropout, yielding prefix-valid codes that capture compact transition structure first and add detail only when needed, without new architectures or losses. A single FlexLAM matches or surpasses separately trained fixed-capacity LAMs at every evaluated token budget under standard scarce-label supervision and under a low-return single-task alignment stress test, indicating that FlexLAM is not merely adjustable at inference time but learns a better latent-action interface at the same token budgets. The same model supports inference-time token-budget adjustment without retraining, and FlexLAM improves Ego4D transition reconstruction. These results suggest that variable-length latent actions are an architecture-free, drop-in upgrade to the fixed-capacity bottleneck in latent action models, latent-action world models, and video-pretrained action interfaces.

中文摘要翻译: 潜动作在无动作视频与下游决策之间提供了紧凑的接口，但现有的潜动作模型（LAM）强制每一个过渡都经过固定容量的瓶颈。我们识别出一个瓶颈权衡问题：过于紧凑的编码可能丢弃动作对齐所需的过渡线索，而过于宽松的编码则保留额外的过渡变异，在动作对齐标签稀缺或分布狭窄时难以解析。FlexLAM用由嵌套dropout训练的可变长度潜动作替代固定容量瓶颈，生成前缀有效编码，先捕获紧凑的过渡结构，仅在需要时添加细节，且无需引入新架构或新损失函数。在标准稀缺标签监督下以及低回报单任务对齐压力测试中，单一的FlexLAM在每一个评估的token预算上均匹配或超越了单独训练的固定容量LAM，这表明FlexLAM不仅能在推理时调整，还能在相同token预算下学到更好的潜动作接口。同一模型支持推理时调整token预算而无需重新训练，且FlexLAM改善了Ego4D过渡重建效果。这些结果表明，可变长度潜动作是对潜动作模型、潜动作世界模型以及视频预训练动作接口中固定容量瓶颈的一种无架构依赖、即插即用的升级方案。

</details>


### [137] [Spectral DPPs via NEPv: A Scalable Continuous Relaxation of Determinantal MAP for Diversity-Aware Data Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19411)
*Richard Yi Da Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对DPP的MAP子集选择问题（NP难），将其重新表述为Stiefel流形上的连续优化，推导出新型非线性特征值问题（NEPv），并提出基于自洽场（SCF）迭代的求解算法，复杂度近乎线性于候选集规模n，适用于大规模数据筛选场景。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在大规模候选集中选择小且多样化的高质量子集是数据筛选、核心集选取、主动学习、检索多样化等任务的核心，而DPP的MAP目标（最大化logdet(L_S)）是NP难的，现有贪心算法和采样算法在n达到百万/十亿级别时开销过大。因此需要一种在数据为中心的范式中可扩展、近乎线性复杂度的高效求解器。

Method: 将DPP-MAP重新表述为Stiefel流形上的连续优化问题，证明其一阶最优性条件构成一种具有特征向量依赖的新型非线性特征值问题（NEPv）；针对该NEPv，设计基于谱间隙（spectral gap）局部收缩保证的自洽场（SCF）迭代，仅需矩阵-向量乘积，整体复杂度为O((ndk+nk^2)t)，其中t为迭代次数（小常数）。

Result: 论文聚焦于理论层面的松弛构造、求解器设计与复杂度分析，给出了SCF迭代的局部收缩保证（基于谱间隙）。复杂度分析表明算法在n上近乎线性，且可直接适配低秩和特征图核。完整的现实数据基准测试留待后续实证研究。

Conclusion: 本文通过连续优化重铸和新NEPv框架，为DPP-MAP问题提供了一个理论上可控、可扩展的迭代求解方案，开启了将DPP应用于超大规模数据筛选的新途径。

Original Abstract: Selecting a small, diverse, high-quality subset from a massive pool of candidates is a recurring primitive in modern machine learning -- data curation and coreset selection for training and fine-tuning large models, active-learning batch acquisition, prompt and exemplar selection for in-context learning, retrieval diversification, and experimental design. Determinantal Point Processes (\DPP s) give a principled, well-calibrated notion of diversity for this task, but their \emph{MAP} objective -- pick a size-$k$ subset $S$ maximizing $\logdet(L_S)$ -- is NP-hard, and the standard greedy and sampling algorithms scale superlinearly in the ground-set size $n$. This cost is prohibitive precisely in the data-centric regime where diversity matters most, where $n$ ranges over millions to billions of candidate examples, features, or embeddings. We recast \DPP-MAP as a continuous optimization problem over the Stiefel manifold, and show that its first-order optimality conditions form a \emph{Nonlinear Eigenvalue Problem with eigenvector dependency} (\NEPv) of a previously unstudied form. This \NEPv\ admits a self-consistent field (\SCF) iteration with a spectral-gap-based local contraction guarantee, giving a principled iterative solver where the diversity objective drives an eigenvector-dependent operator. The resulting algorithm, \OurMethod, requires only matrix-vector products with the kernel and runs in time $O\!\big((ndk+nk^2)\,t\big)$ for a small number of iterations $t$, scaling near-linearly in $n$ and integrating directly with low-rank and feature-map kernels common in ML. This paper focuses on the relaxation, solver, and scaling analysis; full real-data benchmarking is left to a planned empirical study.

中文摘要翻译: 从海量候选集中选择一个小型、多样且高质量的子集，是现代机器学习中反复出现的基础操作——包括训练和微调大模型时的数据筛选与核心集选取、主动学习的批次获取、上下文学习中的提示与示例选择、检索多样化以及实验设计。行列式点过程（DPP）为此任务提供了原则性的、校准良好的多样性度量，但其MAP目标——选取一个大小为k的子集S最大化logdet(L_S)——是NP难的，且标准的贪心和采样算法在候选集规模n上呈超线性扩展。这一开销在多样性至关重要的数据为中心场景中恰恰是不可接受的，此时n的范围从数百万到数十亿个候选示例、特征或嵌入不等。本文将DPP-MAP重新表述为Stiefel流形上的连续优化问题，并证明其一阶最优性条件构成一个此前未被研究过的、具有特征向量依赖的非线性特征值问题（NEPv）。该NEPv允许采用具有基于谱间隙的局部收缩保证的自洽场（SCF）迭代，从而提供了一种原则性的迭代求解器，其中多样性目标驱动一个依赖于特征向量的算子。由此产生的算法OurMethod仅需与核进行矩阵-向量乘积，运行时间为O((ndk+nk^2)t)，其中t为少量迭代次数，在n上近乎线性扩展，并且可直接适配机器学习中常见的低秩核和特征图核。本文聚焦于松弛构造、求解器设计和扩展性分析；完整的真实数据基准测试留待计划中的实证研究。

</details>


### [138] [Spectral Retrieval-Augmented Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19412)
*Huu Hiep Nguyen,Minh Hoang Nguyen,Dung Nguyen,Hung Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SpecReTF，一种通过频域表示和指数加权方案来改进时间序列检索增强预测的方法，解决现有方法在频域信息缺失和时序重要性差异上的不足。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有检索增强的时间序列预测方法存在两个根本缺陷：频域盲区（忽略时序列的周期性频域特征）和时间近因偏差（平等对待所有历史数据，未强调近期更相关的模式）。

Method: 将时间序列转换为窗口化频域表示，使用结合振幅和相位信息的联合度量计算相似度，并采用指数移动平均加权方案平衡近期与历史上下文的重要性。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，SpecReTF在预测精度上优于传统的时域检索方法，尤其对复杂非平稳时间序列表现更佳。

Conclusion: SpecReTF通过引入频域相似度量和指数加权机制，有效提升了非平稳时间序列的检索增强预测性能。

Original Abstract: Time series forecasting leverages historical patterns to predict future values, but traditional methods face challenges when dealing with complex, non-stationary patterns that are difficult to memorize during training. Retrieval-augmented approaches have emerged as promising solutions by retrieving similar historical patterns to enhance predictions. However, existing retrieval methods suffer from two fundamental limitations: spectral blindness, which overlooks critical frequency-domain characteristics that capture underlying periodic structures, and temporal recency, which treats all historical data equally without emphasizing recent, more relevant patterns. In this paper, we propose SpecReTF, a novel retrieval method that addresses these issues by converting time series into windowed frequency representations, measuring similarity with a combined metric that captures both amplitude and phase information. To balance recency and historical context, we apply an exponential moving average weighting scheme that emphasizes recent windows. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate that SpecReTF outperforms time-domain retrieval methods, achieving superior forecasting accuracy across diverse, non-stationary time series.

中文摘要翻译: 时间序列预测利用历史模式来预测未来值，但传统方法在处理复杂的、非平稳的模式时面临挑战，这些模式在训练期间难以记忆。检索增强方法通过检索相似的历史模式来增强预测，已成为有前景的解决方案。然而，现有的检索方法存在两个根本性局限：频域盲区（忽略了捕捉底层周期性结构的关键频域特征）和时间近因偏差（平等对待所有历史数据，而未强调更相关的近期模式）。在本文中，我们提出SpecReTF，一种新颖的检索方法，通过将时间序列转换为窗口化频域表示，并利用结合了振幅和相位信息的联合度量来测量相似性，从而解决这些问题。为了平衡近因性与历史上下文，我们应用了一种指数移动平均加权方案，以强调最近的窗口。在基准数据集上的大量实验表明，SpecReTF优于时域检索方法，在各种非平稳时间序列上实现了卓越的预测精度。

</details>


### [139] [Does Text Actually Help? Uncovering and Resolving Text Collapse in Multimodal Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19413)
*Huu Hiep Nguyen,Minh Hoang Nguyen,Dung Nguyen,Hung Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现多模态时间序列预测中文本分支存在的“文本崩溃”问题（文本分支退化为与输入无关的变换），并提出REST-TS方法，利用残差专属监督迫使文本分支从输入中提取有效信息，在多个真实场景中取得了最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有多模态时间序列预测框架中，文本分支存在“文本崩溃”问题，即文本分支收敛为内容无关的变换，无法提供有效的判别信号。根本原因是数值输入与输出之间存在强自相关性，使得数值骨干天然占优，文本分支虽携带互补信息却被系统性利用不足。

Method: 提出REST-TS（Residual-Exclusive Supervision for Text in Time Series）方法，将不对称性转化为设计原则：数值骨干独立生成数值预测，文本分支则专门被监督去预测残差（即数值无法解释的预测缺口）的结构化成分。由于数值通路无法减少这部分损失，文本分支必须从输入描述中提取真实内容。

Result: 在多种真实世界领域和骨干架构上，REST-TS取得了最优性能，并且相比现有框架，文本分支的利用率持续更高，实证表明对残差进行监督能迫使文本分支提取输入中的真实内容。

Conclusion: 通过将文本分支的监督目标从整体预测转向数值残差，可以有效解决文本崩溃问题，使文本分支真正利用输入文本中的语义信息，提升多模态时间序列预测的整体性能。

Original Abstract: Multimodal time series forecasting, which pairs numerical sequences with domain-relevant textual reports, promises to inject world knowledge into forecasting pipelines. However, we uncover a critical failure mode in existing frameworks that we term text collapse: the text branch converges to a content-independent transformation, contributing negligible discriminative signal regardless of the input description. We argue that text collapse is a consequence of a fundamental asymmetry in time series forecasting: the numerical input is strongly autocorrelated with the output, making the numerical backbone inherently dominant, while the text branch, despite carrying complementary and often critical information, is insufficiently utilized, leading to its systematic underexploitation. To address this, we propose \textbf{REST-TS} (\textbf{R}esidual-\textbf{E}xclusive \textbf{S}upervision for \textbf{T}ext in \textbf{T}ime \textbf{S}eries), which turns the asymmetry into a design principle: the numerical backbone produces its own independent numerical forecast, and the text branch is exclusively supervised to predict the structured components of the residual, the prediction gap that numbers cannot explain. Because no numerical pathway can reduce these losses, the text branch must extract genuine content from the input description. Evaluated across diverse real-world domains and backbone architectures, REST-TS achieves state-of-the-art performance and consistently demonstrates greater text-branch utilization than existing frameworks, providing strong empirical evidence that supervising the text branch on the residual compels it to extract genuine content from the input.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态时间序列预测将数值序列与领域相关的文本报告配对，有望将世界知识注入预测流程。然而，我们揭示了现有框架中的一个关键失败模式，我们称之为“文本崩溃”：文本分支收敛到一种与输入内容无关的变换，无论输入描述如何，都贡献可忽略的判别信号。我们认为，文本崩溃是时间序列预测中一个基本不对称性的结果：数值输入与输出之间存在强自相关性，使得数值骨干天然占优，而文本分支尽管携带互补且往往关键的信息，却被利用不足，导致其系统性的未被充分利用。为解决此问题，我们提出REST-TS（时间序列中文本的残差专属监督方法），将这种不对称性转化为设计原则：数值骨干独立生成其自身的数值预测，而文本分支被专门监督以预测残差（即数值无法解释的预测缺口）的结构化成分。由于任何数值通路都无法减少这些损失，文本分支必须从输入描述中提取真实内容。在多种真实世界领域和骨干架构上的评估表明，REST-TS取得了最优性能，并且相比现有框架，文本分支的利用率持续更高，提供了强有力的实证证据：对残差进行文本分支监督能迫使它从输入中提取真实内容。

</details>


### [140] [MortarBench: Evaluating Mortgage Loan Origination Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19416)
*Matthew Toles,Yunan Lu,Manav Munjal,Bojun Liu,Yuanhao Deng,Stephanie Selig,Derek Rindner,Cheng Li,Zhou Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了MortarBench，一个贷款发起智能体基准测试，用于评估LLM在贷款发起任务中的表现。发现最先进LLM表现不佳（最高77.1%准确率），存在系统性偏见，并提出了CRIT置信度校准框架，将准确率提升至80.5%，同时改善了风险管理并减少了偏见。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 贷款发起过程对评估申请人资格和风险水平至关重要，企业已开始使用贷款智能体辅助人工信贷员，但缺乏公开基准来评估这类系统的性能。

Method: 1) 构建MortarBench基准：利用金融数据合成与变异流水线生成覆盖广泛边界案例、匹配真实世界分布和问题的样本。2) 提出CRIT（置信度校准框架）：用于校准LLM输出，提升准确率并减少偏见。

Result: 1) 最先进LLM表现不佳，闭源模型最高仅达77.1%精确匹配准确率。2) LLM对非英语姓名存在系统性"外国性"感知偏见。3) CRIT框架将准确率提升至80.5%，同时改善了风险管理导向并减少了偏见。

Conclusion: 现有LLM在贷款发起任务中性能不足且存在偏见，而所提出的CRIT框架能有效提升性能、改善风险管理并降低偏见，为金融领域AI应用提供了有价值的改进方向。

Original Abstract: Loan origination is the process by which a lender creates a new loan, from application and underwriting through approval and funding. This process serves a critical role in evaluating the eligibility and level of risk posed by an applicant. Recently, firms have begun using mortgage loan agents to augment human loan officers, despite a lack of any public benchmark. To fill this gap, we present MortarBench, a loan origination agent benchmark. MortarBench uses a financial data synthesis and mutation pipeline to generate examples with broad edge case coverage that match real-world distributions and questions. We find that state-of-the-art large language models (LLMs) perform poorly, with closed-source models achieving at most 77.1\% exact match accuracy. We also discover systematic biases in LLM perception of foreignness related to non-English names. Noting these weaknesses, we introduce CRIT, a confidence calibration framework. Our method increases accuracy to 80.5\% while improving risk management steering and reducing bias.

中文摘要翻译: 贷款发起是贷方创建新贷款的过程，涵盖从申请、承保到审批和放款。该过程在评估申请人的资格和风险水平方面起着关键作用。近年来，尽管缺乏任何公开基准，企业已开始使用抵押贷款智能体来辅助人类信贷员。为填补这一空白，我们提出了MortarBench——一个贷款发起智能体基准测试。MortarBench利用金融数据合成与变异流水线生成样本，这些样本覆盖广泛的边界案例，并与真实世界的数据分布和问题相匹配。我们发现最先进的大语言模型（LLM）表现不佳，闭源模型最高仅达到77.1%的精确匹配准确率。我们还发现LLM对非英语姓名存在系统性的"外国性"感知偏见。注意到这些弱点，我们引入了CRIT——一个置信度校准框架。我们的方法将准确率提升至80.5%，同时改善了风险管理导向并减少了偏见。

</details>


### [141] [3D-DLP: Self-Supervised 3D Object-Centric Scene Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19451)
*Ellina Zhang,Madhaven Iyengar,Amir Zadeh,Chuan Li,Deepak Pathak,David Held,Tal Daniel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出3D-DLP，一种自监督的3D物体中心表示学习方法，将RGB-D或体素观测分解为3D潜粒子，每个粒子编码位置、边界框和外观等解耦属性，通过自监督重建学习可解释的分割图，并在机器人操作任务中提升性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有物体中心表示学习模型大多基于2D图像，缺乏显式的3D结构信息；而基于3D的方法要么内存开销大，要么缺少物体中心的结构化表示。希望将2D的Deep Latent Particles框架扩展到3D，以实现可解释、可操控的3D场景表示。

Method: 在Deep Latent Particles (DLP)框架基础上，将场景级RGB-D或体素观测分解为一组3D潜粒子。每个粒子编码解耦的属性：3D关键点位置、边界框尺寸和外观特征。通过端到端的自监督重建目标学习每个粒子的可解释分割图。

Result: 在模拟和真实数据集上，学习到的潜空间是可解释且可操控的——通过操纵粒子位置并解码可生成新的场景配置。在机器人下游操作任务中，基于3D潜粒子的方法优于缺乏显式3D信息的基线方法，也优于使用无物体中心结构的高内存消耗密集3D输入的方法。

Conclusion: 3D-DLP成功将物体中心表示从2D扩展到3D，提供了可解释、可操控的3D场景表示，并在机器人操作任务中展示了其实际应用价值。

Original Abstract: We introduce 3D-DLP, a self-supervised object-centric representation learning model that decomposes scene-level RGB-D or voxel observations into a set of 3D latent particles. Building on the Deep Latent Particles (DLP) framework, each particle encodes disentangled attributes, including 3D keypoint position, bounding box dimensions, and appearance features, and represents a distinct entity in the scene. The model learns interpretable per-particle segmentation maps through an end-to-end self-supervised reconstruction objective. We demonstrate on both simulated and real-world datasets that the learned latent space is interpretable and controllable: by manipulating particle positions and decoding, we can generate novel scene configurations. Furthermore, we show that leveraging these compact 3D latent particles for downstream robotic manipulation improves performance over baselines that either lack explicit 3D information or rely on memory-intensive dense 3D inputs without object-centric structure. Code and videos are available at https://eubooks3003.github.io/3d-dlp.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了3D-DLP，一种自监督的物体中心表示学习模型，该模型将场景级别的RGB-D或体素观测分解为一组3D潜粒子。基于Deep Latent Particles (DLP)框架，每个粒子编码解耦的属性，包括3D关键点位置、边界框尺寸和外观特征，并代表场景中的一个独立实体。该模型通过端到端的自监督重建目标学习可解释的逐粒子分割图。我们在模拟和真实数据集上证明了所学到的潜空间是可解释且可操控的：通过操控粒子位置并解码，我们可以生成新的场景配置。此外，我们展示了将这些紧凑的3D潜粒子用于下游机器人操作任务时，其性能优于那些缺乏显式3D信息或依赖高内存消耗的密集3D输入且无物体中心结构的基线方法。代码和视频可在https://eubooks3003.github.io/3d-dlp获取。

</details>


### [142] [Can In-Context Learning Support Intrinsic Curiosity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19476)
*Eric Elmoznino,Sangnie Bhardwaj,Johannes von Oswald,Rajai Nasser,Blaise Agüera y Arcas,João Sacramento,Rif A. Saurous,Guillaume Lajoie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究能否利用序列模型的内在学习能力（上下文学习）替代传统的梯度下降更新，作为即时、免更新的世界模型来驱动探索策略。理论上证明在通用马尔可夫决策过程中无法无偏实现，但在非时间性环境（如主动学习）中可收敛到真实学习进度，实验验证了方法的有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统基于学习进度的探索奖励需要在内循环中执行昂贵的梯度下降更新，计算成本过高，难以规模化。本文探索能否利用序列模型内在的上下文学习能力来消除这一瓶颈。

Method: 提出利用序列模型的上下文学习能力作为即时、免更新的世界模型，通过其预测误差和反事实上下文操作来估计学习进度，训练探索策略以最大化学习进度。

Result: 理论上证明：在通用MDP中，ICL导出的内在奖励无法无偏估计真实学习进度；但在非时间性环境（如主动学习、贝叶斯实验设计）中，ICL导出的奖励能上界真实学习进度并渐近收敛。连续和符号环境下的实验验证了该方法能成功训练出探索最优的好奇数据收集策略。

Conclusion: 序列模型的上下文学习能力可在特定环境中替代传统梯度下降实现高效的探索驱动，为好奇心驱动的数据收集提供了计算可扩展的新范式。

Original Abstract: Effective machine learning depends not only on how we model data, but also on what data we choose to collect. While large sequence models have revolutionized data modeling, the problem of automated data selection, or "intrinsic curiosity", remains a significant challenge. Classic approaches incentivize exploration by rewarding an agent based on its "learning progress", which measures how much a newly acquired observation improves a world model's predictive ability. However, evaluating these rewards traditionally requires expensive inner loops of gradient descent updates within each trajectory, rendering them computationally impractical at scale. In this work, we investigate whether the emergent in-context learning (ICL) capabilities of sequence models can eliminate this bottleneck by serving as immediate, update-free world models. Specifically, we evaluate whether an exploration policy can be trained to maximize learning progress, using solely the prediction errors and counterfactual context manipulations of an in-context learner. We first prove that in general Markov decision processes, this is in fact impossible in an unbiased way: the resulting intrinsic rewards either suffer from nuisance terms that bias their estimation of true learning progress, or they cannot be implemented using an in-context learner's prediction errors. Conversely, we prove a positive result for a broad subclass of non-temporal settings, encompassing active learning and Bayesian Experimental Design: here, ICL-derived rewards successfully bound and asymptotically converge to the true learning progress. We corroborate our theory with controlled experiments across continuous and symbolic environments, demonstrating that our ICL-driven framework successfully trains curious data-collection policies that explore optimally.

中文摘要翻译: 有效的机器学习不仅取决于我们如何对数据建模，还取决于我们选择收集哪些数据。虽然大型序列模型已经彻底改变了数据建模，但自动化数据选择（即"内在好奇心"）问题仍然是一个重大挑战。经典方法通过基于智能体的"学习进度"（即新获取的观察在多大程度上提升了世界模型的预测能力）给予奖励来激励探索。然而，传统上评估这些奖励需要在每个轨迹内执行昂贵的内循环梯度下降更新，使其在规模上计算不可行。在这项工作中，我们研究序列模型涌现出的上下文学习能力能否通过作为即时、免更新的世界模型来消除这一瓶颈。具体而言，我们评估能否训练一个探索策略以最大化学习进度，仅使用上下文学习器的预测误差和反事实上下文操作。我们首先证明，在一般马尔可夫决策过程中，这实际上无法以无偏的方式实现：由此产生的内在奖励要么包含干扰项从而偏置其对真实学习进度的估计，要么无法使用上下文学习器的预测误差来实现。相反，我们证明了一个关于广泛的非时间性环境子类（涵盖主动学习和贝叶斯实验设计）的正面结果：在此类环境中，基于上下文学习的奖励成功地上界并渐近收敛到真实学习进度。我们通过在连续和符号环境中的受控实验验证了我们的理论，证明我们基于上下文学习的框架能够成功训练出好奇的数据收集策略，实现最优探索。

</details>


### [143] [Insulin4RL: Real-Time Insulin Management in the Intensive Care Unit for Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19481)
*Thomas Frost,Steve Harris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Insulin4RL，一个基于MIMIC-IV构建的医疗离线强化学习数据集，包含来自12,209名ICU患者、超过37.5万条真实临床轨迹中的自然非均匀时序决策数据，用于评估ORL模型在真实临床采样假设下的表现。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前离线强化学习在医疗领域的训练与评估高度依赖将电子健康记录数据离散化为固定、规则的时间间隔，但这种离散化创建了复杂临床场景的虚构表示，损害了回顾性模型评估的泛化能力。因此需要一种保留临床轨迹自然非均匀特性的数据集。

Method: 从MIMIC-IV中提取需要胰岛素输注滴定的ICU患者数据，构建包含自然非均匀输入和动作的ORL数据集Insulin4RL；采用无模型离线强化学习算法计算基线性能指标，并使用拟合Q评估（Fitted Q-evaluation）提供标准化评估协议。

Result: 构建了包含12,209名患者、超过37.5万条标记决策的Insulin4RL数据集，并提供了基线性能指标和标准化评估协议。

Conclusion: Insulin4RL为医疗ORL研究提供了反映真实临床采样假设的数据资源，有助于提升模型在复杂临床场景中的泛化能力；论文最后指出了未来可利用该资源开展的研究方向。

Original Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (ORL) offers the potential to improve the quality of clinical decision-making using historical electronic health record (EHR) data. Current training and evaluative practices in this field rely heavily on EHR datasets that have been temporally discretised into fixed, regular time intervals. Discretisation creates fictional representations of complex clinical scenarios and compromises the generalisability of retrospective model evaluations. In this paper, we introduce Insulin4RL, a healthcare ORL dataset featuring naturally irregular inputs and actions from real clinical trajectories. Derived from MIMIC-IV, Insulin4RL comprises over 375,000 labelled decisions across 12,209 patients requiring insulin infusion titration in the Intensive Care Unit. The dataset can thus be used for research into ORL model performance under realistic clinical sampling assumptions. We provide a description of the dataset's structure and characteristics, baseline performance metrics using model-free offline reinforcement learning, and a standardised evaluation protocol using fitted Q-evaluation. We conclude with suggested areas for future research that could be addressed using this resource.

中文摘要翻译: 离线强化学习（ORL）有望利用历史电子健康记录（EHR）数据提升临床决策质量。当前该领域的训练与评估实践严重依赖于将EHR数据集按固定、规则的时间间隔进行时间离散化处理。离散化创造了复杂临床场景的虚构表征，并损害了回顾性模型评估的泛化能力。本文提出Insulin4RL，一个医疗ORL数据集，其数据来自真实临床轨迹中自然非均匀的输入和动作。该数据集基于MIMIC-IV构建，包含12,209名需要在重症监护室进行胰岛素输注滴定的患者的超过37.5万条标记决策。该数据集可用于研究ORL模型在真实临床采样假设下的表现。我们提供了数据集的结构与特征描述、基于无模型离线强化学习的基线性能指标，以及使用拟合Q评估的标准化评估协议。最后，我们提出了未来可利用该资源进行的研究方向。

</details>


### [144] [Concept Flow Models: Anchoring Concept-Based Reasoning with Hierarchical Bottlenecks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19489)
*Ya Wang,Adrian Paschke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出概念流模型（CFM），通过层级化概念决策树替代扁平瓶颈层，在保持预测性能的同时缓解信息泄漏，增强可解释性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有概念瓶颈模型（CBM）在概念数量接近嵌入维度时，信息泄漏加剧，模型会利用虚假或语义无关的相关性，削弱可解释性。

Method: 将扁平瓶颈替换为层级化概念驱动的决策树：从视觉嵌入构建决策层次，在各层级分布语义概念，通过概率树遍历训练可微分的概念权重。

Result: 在多个基准上，CFM匹配了扁平CBM的预测性能，同时通过减少有效概念使用显著缓解了信息泄漏，并提供了逐步决策流以实现可审计的推理过程。

Conclusion: 层级化概念决策树能够有效缓解信息泄漏问题，在保持性能的同时提升模型可解释性和推理透明度。

Original Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) enhance interpretability by projecting learned features into a human-understandable concept space. Recent approaches leverage vision-language models to generate concept embeddings, reducing the need for manual concept annotations. However, these models suffer from a critical limitation: as the number of concepts approaches the embedding dimension, information leakage increases, enabling the model to exploit spurious or semantically irrelevant correlations and undermining interpretability. In this work, we propose Concept Flow Models (CFMs), which replace the flat bottleneck with a hierarchical, concept-driven decision tree. Each internal node in the hierarchy focuses on a localized subset of discriminative concepts, progressively narrowing the prediction scope. Our framework constructs decision hierarchies from visual embeddings, distributes semantic concepts at each hierarchy level, and trains differentiable concept weights through probabilistic tree traversal. Extensive experiments on diverse benchmarks demonstrate that CFMs match the predictive performance of flat CBMs, while substantially mitigating information leakage by reducing effective concept usage. Furthermore, CFMs yield stepwise decision flows that enable transparent and auditable model reasoning with hierarchical class structures.

中文摘要翻译: 概念瓶颈模型（CBM）通过将学习到的特征映射到人类可理解的概念空间中，增强了模型的可解释性。近期的方法利用视觉-语言模型生成概念嵌入，减少了对人工概念标注的需求。然而，这些模型存在一个关键局限：当概念数量接近嵌入维度时，信息泄漏加剧，使得模型能够利用虚假或语义上无关的相关性，从而削弱可解释性。在本文中，我们提出了概念流模型（CFM），用层级化的概念驱动决策树替代扁平的瓶颈层。层级结构中的每个内部节点关注一个局部化的判别性子概念集，逐步缩小预测范围。我们的框架从视觉嵌入中构建决策层次，在各层级分配语义概念，并通过概率树遍历训练可微分的概念权重。在多个不同基准上的大量实验表明，CFM在匹配扁平CBM的预测性能的同时，通过减少有效概念使用显著缓解了信息泄漏。此外，CFM产生的逐步决策流使得模型推理过程透明且可审计，并具有层级化的类别结构。

</details>


### [145] [Algebraic Dead Directions in LayerNorm Transformers: A Forward-Pass-Only Diagnostic at LLM Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19491)
*Tejas Pradeep Shirodkar,P. J. Narayanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现LayerNorm变换器在损失函数奇异性极小值处存在一种“死亡方向”，该方向可由LayerNorm层的尺度参数直接解析计算得出，无需前向/反向传播或特征分解。该方向在9/9的LayerNorm模型初始化时与SVD底部奇异方向高度吻合，在训练后协方差特征值加深约10³倍。这一发现可用于仅从参数判断归一化类型，并揭示残差流的最小奇异值在模块间保持不变的规律。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 预训练Transformer处于损失函数的奇异性极小值附近，Fisher信息矩阵沿某些“死亡方向”退化。现有方法需要通过前向传播和激活的特征分解或基于采样的复杂度估计来定位这些方向，无法仅从网络参数直接计算。本文旨在寻找一种仅从参数本身即可计算的死亡方向。

Method: 证明了对于LayerNorm变换器，逆尺度方向 γ⁻¹/‖γ⁻¹‖ 是最终归一化后中心化激活协方差矩阵的精确代数核，该方向可从LayerNorm的尺度参数直接读取，无需任何前向/反向传播或特征分解。通过理论推导和14个预训练变换器（9个LayerNorm、5个RMSNorm，参数规模160M-35B）的实验验证了这一发现。

Result: 在9/9的LayerNorm模型初始化时，预测方向与实测SVD底部奇异方向吻合到小数点后四位；在5/5的RMSNorm模型中该方向正确消失。训练后该方向上的协方差特征值加深约10³倍，并出现更多死亡方向。该方向的存在可仅从参数判断归一化类型；残差流的最小奇异值在13/14个模型上保持跨模块不变，唯一例外（Gemma4-31B）可由该方向精确定位。
