The neural additive model (NAM) and its variant called the neural basis model (NBM) use neural networks (NNs) as nonlinear shape functions in generalized additive models (GAMs). Both models are highly interpretable and exhibit good performance and flexibility for NN training. NAM and NBM can provide and visualize the contribution of each feature to the prediction owing to GAM-based architectures. However, when using two-input NNs to consider feature interactions or when applying them to high-dimensional datasets, training NAM and NBM becomes intractable due to the increase in the computational resources required. This paper proposes incorporating the feature selection mechanism into NAM and NBM to resolve computational bottlenecks. We introduce the feature selection layer in both models and update the selection weights during training. Our method is simple and can reduce computational costs and model sizes compared to vanilla NAM and NBM. In addition, it enables us to use two-input NNs even in high-dimensional datasets and capture feature interactions. We demonstrate that the proposed models are computationally efficient compared to vanilla NAM and NBM, and they exhibit better or comparable performance with state-of-the-art GAMs.

中文摘要翻译: 深度神经网络（DNN）在各个领域展现出优异的性能，但通常可解释性较低。神经加性模型（NAM）及其变体神经基模型（NBM）在广义加性模型（GAM）中使用神经网络（NN）作为非线性形状函数。这两种模型具有高度可解释性，并且在NN训练中表现出良好的性能和灵活性。由于基于GAM的架构，NAM和NBM可以提供并可视化每个特征对预测的贡献。然而，当使用双输入NN来考虑特征交互或将其应用于高维数据集时，由于所需计算资源的增加，NAM和NBM的训练变得难以处理。本文提出将特征选择机制融入NAM和NBM以解决计算瓶颈。我们在两种模型中引入特征选择层，并在训练过程中更新选择权重。我们的方法简单，与原始NAM和NBM相比能够降低计算成本和模型规模。此外，它使我们即使在处理高维数据集时也能使用双输入NN并捕获特征交互。我们证明了所提出的模型相比原始NAM和NBM具有计算效率优势，并且在与最先进的GAM方法相比表现出更好或相当的性能。

</details>


### [177] [Physics-Informed Neural Network with Squeeze-Excitation-like Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19853)
*Yun-Fei Song,Long-Gang Pang,Fu-Peng Li,Jun-Jie Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SEA-PINN是一种将Squeeze-Excitation注意力机制引入物理信息神经网络的轻量化插件模块，通过动态重新校准神经元重要性实现准确定性初始化，在20个基准问题中的17个上具有近乎可忽略的方差，并显著提升模型收敛鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有物理信息神经网络（PINN）存在初始化不稳定、收敛困难以及对高频问题建模能力不足的问题。作者旨在设计一种轻量级插件模块，增强非线性表示能力并提升训练鲁棒性，而无需依赖傅里叶特征嵌入或周期激活函数等复杂技巧。

Method: 提出SEA-PINN架构，核心是将Squeeze-Excitation（SE）类注意力机制融入PINN，通过动态重新校准各层神经元的重要性权重，实现高度稳定的初始化，从而为优化提供一个准确定性的有利起点。

Result: 在20个基准问题中，17个问题上SEA-PINN表现出近乎可忽略的方差且初始损失显著降低；在高频案例（案例7）中，未使用傅里叶特征或周期激活函数即达到与TSA-PINN（专为高频问题设计）竞争的精度（相对FNN-PINN提升83%~90%）；将SEA-PINN集成到TSA-PINN中性能提升42.49%。

Conclusion: SEA-PINN作为一种轻量级即插即用模块，能够增强非线性表示能力、促进更鲁棒高效的收敛，并提升物理信息学习的整体可靠性。

Original Abstract: We introduce SEA-PINN, a novel architecture that incorporates a Squeeze-Excitation-like attention mechanism into physics-informed neural networks to dynamically recalibrate the importance of neurons across layers. A key feature of SEA-PINN is its highly stable initialization. On 17 out of 20 benchmark problems, SEA-PINN exhibit nearly negligible variance and significantly reduced initial loss, establishing a quasi-deterministic and favorable starting point for optimization. Notably, without employing Fourier feature embeddings or periodic activation functions, SEA-PINN attained competitive accuracy (83\% vs. 90\% improvement relative to FNN-PINN on the high-frequency case 7) as compared with TSA-PINN-a model specifically engineered for high-frequency problems via learnable frequencies in sinusoidal activations. Furthermore, integrating SEA-PINN into TSA-PINN boosted performance by 42.49\%. These results underscore SEA-PINN as a lightweight plug-in module that enhances nonlinear representation power, promotes more robust and efficient convergence, and strengthens the overall reliability of physics-informed learning.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出SEA-PINN，一种新颖的架构，它将Squeeze-Excitation类注意力机制引入物理信息神经网络，以动态重新校准各层神经元的重要性。SEA-PINN的一个关键特性是其高度稳定的初始化。在20个基准问题中的17个上，SEA-PINN表现出近乎可忽略的方差且初始损失显著降低，为优化建立了一个准确定性的有利起点。值得注意的是，在没有使用傅里叶特征嵌入或周期激活函数的情况下，SEA-PINN在高频案例（案例7）中达到了与TSA-PINN（一种通过正弦激活中的可学习频率专门针对高频问题设计的模型）竞争的精度（相对FNN-PINN提升83%~90%）。此外，将SEA-PINN集成到TSA-PINN中使性能提升了42.49%。这些结果凸显了SEA-PINN作为一种轻量级即插即用模块的潜力，它能够增强非线性表示能力，促进更鲁棒且高效的收敛，并提升物理信息学习的整体可靠性。

</details>


### [178] [Global Convergence of Gradient Descent for Score Matching in Gaussian Mixtures via Reverse Fisher Divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19876)
*Alexander Tyurin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究反向Fisher散度作为评分匹配的替代目标函数，证明在高斯混合模型拟合中，相对于前向Fisher散度，反向Fisher散度具有更优的优化景观，并建立了梯度下降的全局收敛性保证。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 前向Fisher散度作为评分匹配的标准目标函数，即使在高斯混合模型这种简单场景下也会导致不良的、依赖初始化的收敛行为，因此需要寻找优化性质更好的替代目标函数。

Method: 采用反向Fisher散度作为优化目标（期望对学生分布而非教师分布取），并利用基于Lyapunov函数的分析框架，研究梯度下降算法在高斯混合模型拟合中的动力学行为。

Result: 1) 当教师为单个高斯、学生为固定权重和单位协方差的高斯混合模型时，证明了梯度下降从任意初始化出发的全局收敛性；2) 当教师也为高斯混合模型时，在全局随机初始化方案和目标均值$\widetildeΩ(1)$-分离假设下，证明了各学生分量以高概率收敛到最近教师分量附近，并给出了总变差距离收敛的条件。

Conclusion: 反向Fisher散度相比前向Fisher散度具有更有利的优化景观，为改进生成模型、扩散模型等领域的训练目标提供了理论依据。

Original Abstract: The score matching problem is a central training objective in modern generative modeling, diffusion models, fitting unnormalized statistical models, and inverse problems. A standard approach is to minimize the forward Fisher divergence, where the expectation is taken with respect to the teacher distribution. However, recent results show that even in simple Gaussian mixture model settings, this objective can lead to undesirable and initialization-dependent convergence behavior. In this paper, we study an alternative objective: the reverse Fisher divergence, where the expectation is taken with respect to the student distribution. We analyze gradient descent (GD) for fitting Gaussian mixture models and show that this change in the objective leads to significantly better optimization properties. First, when the teacher distribution is a single Gaussian and the student is a Gaussian mixture model with fixed weights and identity covariances, we prove the global convergence of GD from arbitrary initializations. Second, we extend the analysis to the case where the teacher is also a Gaussian mixture model and prove global convergence guarantees under a global random initialization scheme and a $\widetildeΩ(1)$-separation assumption on the target means. In particular, with high probability, each student component converges near its closest teacher component, and we provide conditions under which the student distribution converges in total variation distance. Our proofs rely on a new Lyapunov-based analysis of the gradient descent dynamics, showing that the reverse Fisher divergence has a much more favorable optimization landscape than the forward Fisher divergence.

中文摘要翻译: 评分匹配问题是现代生成建模、扩散模型、非归一化统计模型拟合以及逆问题中的核心训练目标。标准的做法是最小化前向Fisher散度，其期望对教师分布取。然而，近期结果表明，即使在高斯混合模型这种简单场景下，该目标也会导致不良的、依赖初始化的收敛行为。本文研究了一个替代目标：反向Fisher散度，其期望对学生分布取。我们分析了拟合高斯混合模型的梯度下降方法，并表明目标函数的这一改变带来了显著更优的优化性质。首先，当教师分布为单个高斯分布而学生为固定权重和单位协方差的高斯混合模型时，我们证明了梯度下降从任意初始化出发的全局收敛性。其次，我们将分析推广到教师也是高斯混合模型的情形，并在全局随机初始化方案和目标均值满足$\widetildeΩ(1)$-分离假设下，证明了全局收敛保证。特别地，每个学生分量以高概率收敛到其最近的教师分量附近，我们给出了学生分布在总变差距离下收敛的条件。我们的证明依赖于对梯度下降动力学的一种新的基于Lyapunov函数的分析，表明反向Fisher散度比前向Fisher散度具有更有利的优化景观。

</details>


### [179] [On the Oracle Complexity of Interpolation-Based Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19878)
*Dongmin Lee,William Lu,Anuran Makur*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于分段多项式插值的梯度下降方法（PPI-GD），通过在数据域等距点查询一阶预言机来近似每个迭代中的全梯度，在损失函数充分光滑且数据空间维度的对数多项式有界时，其预言机复杂度优于多种GD变体。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 利用经验风险最小化（ERM）损失函数在训练数据上的光滑性（而非优化参数上的光滑性）来改进梯度下降方法的预言机复杂度。

Method: 提出一种不精确梯度方法PPI-GD，在每次迭代中，在数据域的等距点处查询一阶预言机，构建梯度样本的多项式插值函数，在适当大小的数据域块上近似全梯度。

Result: 在强凸和非凸损失函数下，当数据空间维度是训练样本数量的对数多项式有界函数时，PPI-GD的预言机复杂度在关键场景下优于多种GD变体；同时将双三次样条插值的误差分析技术推广到了d变量张量积多项式插值设置中。

Conclusion: PPI-GD通过利用数据域光滑性有效降低了GD方法的预言机复杂度，且所提出的插值误差分析技术具有独立的理论价值。

Original Abstract: Recent work on first-order optimizers for empirical risk minimization (ERM) has suggested that smoothness of ERM loss functions in the training data, rather than in the optimization parameters, can be leveraged to improve the oracle complexity of gradient descent (GD) methods. In this paper, we propose an inexact gradient method, piecewise polynomial interpolation-based gradient descent (PPI-GD), which approximates the full gradient in each iteration by querying the first-order oracle at equidistant points in the data domain to construct polynomial interpolants of the resulting gradient samples over appropriately sized patches of the data domain. We analyze the oracle complexity of PPI-GD for strongly convex and non-convex loss functions when the data space dimension is bounded by a polylogarithmic function of the number of training samples, and find it to outperform several GD variants in key regimes when the loss function is sufficiently smooth. Furthermore, our analysis extends several techniques from the error analysis of bicubic spline interpolants to the setting of $d$-variate tensor product polynomial interpolants which may be of independent interest in interpolation analysis.

中文摘要翻译: 近期关于经验风险最小化（ERM）一阶优化器的工作表明，可以利用ERM损失函数在训练数据上的光滑性（而非优化参数上的光滑性）来改进梯度下降（GD）方法的预言机复杂度。本文提出了一种不精确梯度方法——基于分段多项式插值的梯度下降（PPI-GD），该方法在每次迭代中通过在数据域的等距点处查询一阶预言机，构造梯度样本的多项式插值函数，以在数据域的适当大小块上近似全梯度。我们分析了PPI-GD在强凸和非凸损失函数下的预言机复杂度，其中数据空间维度被限制为训练样本数量的对数多项式函数，并在损失函数充分光滑的关键场景下发现其优于多种GD变体。此外，我们的分析将双三次样条插值的误差分析技术推广到了d变量张量积多项式插值设置中，这一成果在插值分析领域可能具有独立的研究价值。

</details>


### [180] [Matching Markets meet Cumulative Prospect Theory: Towards Optimal and Adversarially Robust Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19883)
*Ananya Kunisetty,Avishek Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于累积前景理论（CPT）的人类偏好建模下的多智能体多臂老虎机问题，在双边匹配市场的竞争设置中提出了改进的遗憾界，同时扩展到对抗性市场中的鲁棒算法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有多智能体多臂老虎机研究大多假设理性行为，而实际人类决策常受非线性偏好影响。本文旨在引入行为经济学中的CPT模型来刻画人类偏好，并解决该设置下的学习与匹配问题。

Method: 1) 使用CPT权重函数扭曲奖励，并基于此分析现有学习算法的后悔界；2) 通过优化探索阶段的活动臂选择策略，消除主导项中对臂数K的依赖，获得改进的遗憾界；3) 针对对抗性市场（奖励可能被破坏），分别针对已知和未知总破坏预算的情况设计鲁棒算法。

Result: 1) 在CPT权重扭曲奖励下得到玩家最优遗憾界 O(K log T (1/Δ)^{2/α})；2) 通过主动选择探索臂集，在K >> N时消除了K的依赖，获得最优遗憾界；3) 在对抗性市场中，对于已知和未知破坏预算两种情况，均建立了对数级的玩家最优遗憾保证。

Conclusion: 本文成功将CPT模型引入多智能体多臂老虎机匹配市场问题，理论证明了所提算法在常规和对抗性市场下均能达到最优或近最优的遗憾界，为人类偏好导向的决策系统提供了理论支撑。

Original Abstract: We study a multi-agent multi-armed bandit problem in the competitive setup with two-sided matching markets under a human centric decision making model. To capture human preferences, we use cumulative prospect theory (CPT) that weighs the actions of the agent in a nonlinear fashion using a ($α$-Hölder continuous) weight function. CPT has been widely used in behavioral economics and risk sensitive machine learning to emulate human preferences. We analyze the state-of-the-art learning algorithm with CPT weight distorted rewards and obtain a player optimal regret of $\mathcal{O}(K\log T \left(\frac{1}Δ\right)^{2/α})$, where $K$ denotes the number of arms, $T$ is the learning horizon, and $Δ$ represents (suitably defined) players' minimum preference gap. Noticing the dependence on $Δ$ to be sub-optimal, we further improve this regret by judiciously selecting the active set of arms during exploration, which removes the dependence on $K$ in the dominant term and achieves an improved (optimal) regret guarantees in the setting where the number of arms $K$ is significantly larger than the number of players $N$. In addition, we consider adversarial markets where the observed rewards of the agents may be corrupted. We propose and analyze algorithms for robust markets with CPT as risk sensitive measure in both settings where the total corruption budget is known and where it is unknown, and establish logarithmic player-optimal regret guarantees in both cases.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究了一种在以人为中心的决策模型下的竞争性双边匹配市场中的多智能体多臂老虎机问题。为刻画人类偏好，我们使用了累积前景理论（CPT），该理论通过一个(α-赫尔德连续的)权重函数以非线性方式对智能体的行为进行加权。CPT已被广泛应用于行为经济学和风险敏感的机器学习中以模拟人类偏好。我们分析了使用CPT权重扭曲奖励的最先进学习算法，并获得了玩家最优遗憾界 O(K log T (1/Δ)^{2/α})，其中K表示臂数，T是学习周期，Δ表示（适当定义的）玩家的最小偏好差距。注意到该界对Δ的依赖是次优的，我们进一步通过审慎选择探索过程中活跃的臂集来改进这一遗憾界，这消除了主导项中对K的依赖，并在臂数K显著大于玩家数N的设置中实现了改进的（最优）遗憾界。此外，我们考虑了对抗性市场，其中智能体观测到的奖励可能被破坏。我们提出并分析了以CPT作为风险敏感度量的鲁棒市场算法，分别针对总破坏预算已知和未知两种情况，并在两种情况下均建立了对数级的玩家最优遗憾保证。

</details>


### [181] [SL-S4Wave: Self-Supervised Learning of Physiological Waveforms with Structured State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19888)
*Feng Wu,Harsh Deep,Eric Lehman,Sanyam Kapoor,Guoshuai Zhao,Rahul Krishnan,Gari Clifford,Li-wei H Lehman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SL-S4Wave，一种结合对比学习与结构化状态空间模型的自监督学习框架，用于多通道生理波形（如ECG、EEG）的长序列建模，在心律失常检测等任务上优于现有方法，并展现出强标签效率和跨域泛化能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有自监督学习方法（如基于CNN的编码器）难以捕获长程依赖和噪声不变特征，而结构化状态空间模型虽擅长长序列建模，但现有S4架构无法捕获多通道生理波形的独特特征。亟需一种既能处理长序列又能适应多通道噪声生理信号的自监督框架。

Method: 提出SL-S4Wave框架，核心包括：(1) 基于结构化状态空间模型（S4）的编码器，融入多层全局卷积与多尺度子核，同时捕获细粒度局部模式和长程时间依赖；(2) 结合对比学习的自监督预训练范式，充分利用无标签数据学习鲁棒表示。

Result: (1) 在心律失常检测任务上一致优于现有监督和自监督基线；(2) 使用显著更少的标注样本即可达到高性能，标签效率强；(3) 在长波形片段上保持鲁棒性能，能建模大多数现有方法无法有效处理的复杂时序动态；(4) 有效迁移到未见的心律失常类型，展现强跨域泛化能力；(5) 在多个EEG任务上也取得优于强基线的性能，证明方法超越心电波形的通用性。

Conclusion: SL-S4Wave通过结合结构化状态空间模型与对比学习，有效解决了多通道生理波形长序列建模中的关键挑战，在多种生理信号任务上均展现出优越性能、标签效率和泛化能力，为生理时间序列的自监督学习提供了新的有效范式。

Original Abstract: Modeling long-sequence medical time series data, such as electrocardiograms (ECG), poses significant challenges due to high sampling rates, multichannel signal complexity, inherent noise, and limited labeled data. While recent self-supervised learning (SSL) methods, based on various encoder architectures such as convolutional neural networks, have been proposed to learn representations from unlabeled data, they often fall short in capturing long-range dependencies and noise-invariant features. Structured state space models (S4) excel at long-sequence modeling, but existing S4 architectures fail to capture the unique characteristics of multichannel physiological waveforms. In this work, we propose SL-S4Wave, a self-supervised learning framework that combines contrastive learning with a tailored encoder built on structured state space models. The encoder incorporates multi-layer global convolution using multiscale subkernels, enabling the capture of both fine-grained local patterns and long-range temporal dependencies in noisy, high-resolution multichannel waveforms. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that SL-S4Wave (1) consistently outperforms state-of-the-art supervised and self-supervised baselines in a challenging arrhythmia detection task, (2) achieves high performance with significantly fewer labeled examples, showcasing strong label efficiency, and (3) maintains robust performance on long waveform segments, highlighting its capacity to model complex temporal dynamics in long sequences that most existing approaches fail to efficiently model, and (4) transfers effectively to unseen arrhythmia types, underscoring its robust cross-domain generalization. We additionally evaluate SL-S4Wave on multiple EEG tasks, achieving superior performance over strong baselines, demonstrating generalizability of our approach beyond cardiac waveforms.

中文摘要翻译: 建模长序列医学时间序列数据（如心电图ECG）面临着高采样率、多通道信号复杂性、固有噪声以及有限标注数据等重大挑战。尽管近期基于各种编码器架构（如卷积神经网络）的自监督学习方法被提出用于从无标签数据中学习表示，但它们往往在捕获长程依赖和噪声不变特征方面存在不足。结构化状态空间模型（S4）擅长长序列建模，但现有S4架构未能捕获多通道生理波形的独特特征。本文提出SL-S4Wave，一种结合对比学习与基于结构化状态空间模型定制化编码器的自监督学习框架。该编码器采用多尺度子核的多层全局卷积，能够在含噪声的高分辨率多通道波形中同时捕获细粒度局部模式和长程时间依赖。在真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，SL-S4Wave（1）在挑战性心律失常检测任务上持续优于最先进的监督和自监督基线；（2）使用显著更少的标注样本即可达到高性能，展现出强大的标签效率；（3）在长波形片段上保持鲁棒性能，突显其建模大多数现有方法无法有效处理的长序列复杂时序动态的能力；（4）有效迁移到未见的心律失常类型，强调了其稳健的跨域泛化能力。我们还在多个EEG任务上评估了SL-S4Wave，取得了优于强基线的性能，证明了该方法超越心电波形的泛化能力。

</details>


### [182] [Adversarial Bandit Optimization with Globally Bounded Perturbations to Convex Losses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19891)
*Zhuoyu Cheng,Kohei Hatano,Eiji Takimoto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究非凸非光滑损失函数的对抗性bandit优化，将全局预算的后动作扰动模型从线性损失推广到一般凸且β-光滑损失，并给出期望遗憾界。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有对抗性bandit优化主要关注线性损失函数，而实际场景中损失函数往往是非凸非光滑的，且可能受到对抗性扰动。本文旨在将后动作扰动模型从线性损失推广到更一般的凸且β-光滑损失函数，以覆盖更广泛的实际应用。

Method: 修改标准的bandit优化算法，并设计新的分析方法来控制扰动带来的额外遗憾。具体地，将损失分解为底层凸且β-光滑部分和满足全局预算的对抗性扰动部分，在算法分析中显式刻画扰动预算对遗憾的影响。

Result: 建立了期望遗憾的保证，显式刻画了扰动预算的影响；当无扰动时，结果退化为标准bandit凸优化（β-光滑损失）的遗憾界。

Conclusion: 成功将全局预算后动作扰动模型从线性损失扩展到一般凸且β-光滑损失，证明了算法在该设置下的理论保证，为更复杂的非凸非光滑对抗性bandit优化问题提供了理论分析框架。

Original Abstract: We study adversarial bandit optimization in which the loss functions may be non-convex and non-smooth. In each round, the learner selects an action and observes only the loss incurred at that action. The loss consists of an underlying convex and $β$-smooth component and an adversarial perturbation that may be chosen after observing the learner's action. The perturbations are subject to a global budget controlling their cumulative magnitude over time.
  This framework extends the globally budgeted, post-action perturbation model from underlying linear losses to general convex and $β$-smooth losses. For this broader class, we establish expected regret guarantees that explicitly characterize the effect of the perturbation budget.
  To establish these guarantees, we modify a standard bandit optimization algorithm and develop an analysis that controls the additional regret caused by the perturbations. In the absence of perturbations, our results reduce to regret guarantees for the standard bandit convex optimization setting with $β$-smooth losses.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究损失函数可能非凸且非光滑的对抗性bandit优化问题。在每一轮中，学习者选择一个动作并仅观察该动作产生的损失。损失由一个底层的凸且β-光滑分量和一个对抗性扰动组成，该扰动可能在观察到学习者的动作后被选择。扰动受到一个全局预算的约束，该预算控制其随时间累积的幅度。该框架将全局预算的后动作扰动模型从底层线性损失扩展到一般的凸且β-光滑损失。针对这一更广泛的类别，我们建立了期望遗憾保证，显式刻画了扰动预算的影响。为建立这些保证，我们修改了一个标准的bandit优化算法，并开发了一种分析方法来控制扰动引起的额外遗憾。在无扰动的情况下，我们的结果退化为标准bandit凸优化设置中β-光滑损失的遗憾保证。

</details>


### [183] [Score Approximation for Diffusion Models on Arbitrary Low-Dimensional Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19894)
*Xinhe Mu,Zaijiu Shang,Zhaoqi Zhou,Chuan Zhou,Qi Meng,Guiying Yan,Zhiming Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通用得分近似定理，证明对于任何支撑在任意上闵可夫斯基维度为d的紧集上的分布，其得分函数可以用复杂度仅关于d呈指数增长的ReLU网络近似，从而打破了环境维度的指数灾难，解释了扩散模型在非光滑、不规则的现实数据上表现优异的原因。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有扩散模型的理论复杂度界限依赖过于严苛的假设（如Lipschitz连续密度或光滑流形支撑），而真实感知数据普遍存在奇异性、尖锐边界和不连通簇，这些假设经常被违反，因此需要建立更通用的理论框架来解释扩散模型的实际有效性。

Method: 采用离散-混合（discrete-mixture）公式化方法，证明对于任何支撑在任意上闵可夫斯基维度为d的紧集上的分布，其得分函数可以用ReLU网络进行近似，网络复杂度仅关于内在维度d呈指数增长，而非环境维度。

Result: 得分函数近似所需的ReLU网络复杂度仅与数据的内在维度d呈指数关系，打破了环境维度的指数诅咒；结合已有的反向扩散SDE精确求解理论，证明扩散模型能够自适应地处理不规则、非光滑的数据结构。

Conclusion: 扩散模型能够有效适应现实世界中不规则、非光滑的数据结构，这为扩散模型在真实生成任务中的卓越表现提供了理论依据。

Original Abstract: The remarkable success of score-based diffusion models has spurred significant efforts to establish their theoretical foundations. However, existing complexity bounds for score approximation rely heavily on restrictive assumptions like Lipschitz continuous densities or smooth manifold supports, which are routinely violated by the singularities, sharp boundaries, and disjoint clusters inherent to real-world perceptual data. This work establishes a universal score approximation theorem that works for any distribution supported on any compact set of upper Minkowski dimension $d$. Using a novel discrete-mixture formulation, we prove that the score function can be approximated with a ReLU network whose complexity grows exponentially only with $d$, thus breaking the exponential curse of ambient dimensionality. Combined with existing theories on accurately solving the backward diffusion SDE for arbitrary compact distributions, our work shows that diffusion models readily adapt to irregular, non-smooth data structures, explaining their competence in real-world generative tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 基于得分的扩散模型取得的显著成功，激发了建立其理论基础的重大努力。然而，现有的得分近似复杂度界限严重依赖于Lipschitz连续密度或光滑流形支撑等限制性假设，而这些假设被现实感知数据中固有的奇异性、尖锐边界和不连通簇所频繁违反。本文建立了一个通用的得分近似定理，适用于任何支撑在任意上闵可夫斯基维度为$d$的紧集上的分布。通过一种新颖的离散-混合公式化方法，我们证明了得分函数可以用一个复杂度仅关于$d$呈指数增长的ReLU网络来近似，从而打破了环境维度的指数诅咒。结合已有的关于精确求解任意紧分布上反向扩散随机微分方程的现有理论，我们的工作表明，扩散模型能够自适应地适应不规则、非光滑的数据结构，这解释了它们在现实世界生成任务中的卓越表现力。

</details>


### [184] [ADaPT: Token-Level Decoupling for Efficient Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19919)
*Tingyun Li,Zishang Jiang,Jinyi Han,Xinyi Wang,Sihang Jiang,Han Xia,Zhaoqian Dai,Shuguang Ma,Fei Yu,Jiaqing Liang,Yanghua Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ADaPT通过令牌级双过程框架解耦效率与正确性信号，在训练中仅对模式选择令牌施加效率奖励，从而在保证推理性能的同时显著降低计算成本，并支持推理时连续调节效率-性能权衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有大推理模型的长链思维虽性能强但计算成本高，而效率导向的方法（如缩短或混合推理策略）往往损害推理能力，其根本原因在于效率激励与正确性优化在序列级别耦合，导致长但正确的推理轨迹被隐性惩罚。

Method: 提出自适应双过程思维框架（ADaPT），引入模式选择令牌来控制快/慢推理；训练时将效率相关奖励仅施加于该令牌，避免惩罚正确的长推理；推理时通过调整该令牌的生成概率，实现单模型沿效率-性能帕累托前沿的连续控制。

Result: 大量实验表明，ADaPT在多个基准测试上显著降低推理成本的同时，保持了强大的推理性能。

Conclusion: ADaPT通过令牌级解耦效率与正确性信号，有效解决了推理效率与性能之间的根本矛盾，实现了对效率-性能权衡的精确连续控制。

Original Abstract: Large reasoning models rely on long chain-of-thought to achieve strong performance, but applying such reasoning uniformly incurs high computational cost. Existing efficiency-oriented methods attempt to shorten or mix reasoning strategies, yet often degrade reasoning capability. We identify the root cause as sequence-level coupling between efficiency incentives and correctness optimization, which implicitly penalizes long but correct reasoning trajectories. To address this issue, we propose Adaptive Dual-Process Thinking (ADaPT), a token-level dual-process framework that explicitly decouples efficiency and correctness signals during training. ADaPT introduces a mode-selection token to control fast and slow reasoning, applying efficiency-related rewards exclusively to this token to avoid penalizing correct long reasoning while encouraging efficiency when appropriate. Moreover, ADaPT enables precise and continuous control over the efficiency-performance trade-off at inference time: by adjusting the generation probability of the mode-selection token, a single trained model can smoothly move along the efficiency-performance Pareto frontier. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ADaPT significantly reduces inference cost while maintaining strong reasoning performance across multiple benchmarks.

中文摘要翻译: 大型推理模型依赖长链思维来获得强性能，但统一应用这种推理会导致高昂的计算成本。现有的效率导向方法试图缩短或混合推理策略，但往往会降低推理能力。我们将根本原因归结为效率激励与正确性优化在序列级别的耦合，这隐性地惩罚了长但正确的推理轨迹。为解决此问题，我们提出自适应双过程思维（ADaPT），一种令牌级双过程框架，在训练过程中显式解耦效率与正确性信号。ADaPT引入一个模式选择令牌来控制快慢推理，仅对该令牌施加与效率相关的奖励，从而在鼓励适当效率的同时避免惩罚正确的长推理。此外，ADaPT能够在推理时对效率-性能权衡进行精确且连续的控制：通过调整模式选择令牌的生成概率，单个训练好的模型可以沿效率-性能帕累托前沿平滑移动。大量实验表明，ADaPT在多个基准测试上显著降低推理成本，同时保持了强大的推理性能。

</details>


### [185] [Compositionality Emerges in a Narrow Depth-Connectivity Regime: Architecture Constraints and Solution Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19941)
*Dat H. Do,Rushi Shah,Duc V. Le,Dianbo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现组合性（compositionality）仅出现在特定的稀疏连接与深度的狭窄区域：连接稀疏性必须结构性地保留某些连接而非仅权重稀疏，深度需处于与目标任务相关的特定区间。超出该范围，梯度下降会收敛到碎裂解。作者提出相似性剪枝（SP）和深度预测器来恢复和预测组合性，并给出理论支撑。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 解释组合性内部结构如何以及为何在标准梯度优化中形成，揭示其出现的条件。

Method: (1) 通过系统性实验探究网络连接稀疏性（哪些连接保留 vs. 仅权重稀疏）和深度对组合性涌现的影响；(2) 提出相似性剪枝（Similarity-based Pruning, SP）恢复组合性连接结构；(3) 提出启发式深度预测器估计最可能出现组合性的深度；(4) 基于组合稀疏性、体积比论证和特征干扰界构建理论框架。

Result: (1) 组合性仅在特定的稀疏网络中出现，且关键取决于保留哪些连接而非仅权重稀疏度；(2) 组合性仅在特定目标任务依赖的深度区间出现，过浅或过深均不涌现；(3) 条件不满足时梯度下降收敛到碎裂解而非组合解；(4) SP方法可有效恢复组合性连接，深度预测器可估计最佳深度。

Conclusion: 组合性的涌现受限于连接结构与深度的狭窄交汇区域，超出该区域模型无法习得组合解；所提出的SP剪枝和深度预测器可有效利用该发现，理论框架解释了为何组合解仅在特定深度-连通性区域内可达。

Original Abstract: Compositionality is believed to be the foundation for generalization, enabling models to reuse meaningful primitives in novel combinations. Yet, models trained with standard gradient-based optimization rarely, and often only weakly, exhibit compositional internal structure, and it remains unclear how or why such compositionality forms. In this work, we show that compositionality emerges in a narrow connectivity-depth sweet spot. Along the connectivity axis, compositionality only appears in some specifically sparse networks, heavily depends on which connections remain rather than on weights' sparsity alone. Along the depth axis, compositionality emerges within a narrow, target-dependent regime, peaking at specific depths, while both shallower and deeper networks fail. When either the depth or connectivity condition is violated, gradient descent silently converges to fractured solutions rather than compositional ones. To discover and exploit this emergence, we introduce (i) similarity-based pruning (SP) to recover compositional connectivity and (ii) a heuristic depth predictor to estimate where compositionality is most likely to appear. Finally, we support these empirical findings with a theoretical framework based on compositional sparsity, volume-ratio arguments, and feature-interference bounds, explaining why compositional solutions are reachable only in a narrow depth-connectivity regime.

中文摘要翻译: 组合性被认为是泛化的基础，它使模型能够在新的组合中重用有意义的基元。然而，通过标准梯度优化训练的模型很少（且通常很弱地）表现出组合性内部结构，目前尚不清楚这种组合性如何或为何形成。在本工作中，我们展示了组合性出现在一个狭窄的连通性-深度最佳区间内。沿连通性轴，组合性仅出现在某些特定稀疏网络中，且严重依赖于保留哪些连接而非仅权重的稀疏度。沿深度轴，组合性在狭窄的、目标依赖的区间内涌现，在特定深度达到峰值，而更浅和更深的网络都无法实现。当深度或连通性条件不满足时，梯度下降静默地收敛到碎裂解而非组合解。为了发现并利用这种涌现，我们引入了（i）基于相似性的剪枝（SP）来恢复组合性连接，以及（ii）启发式深度预测器来估计组合性最可能出现的位置。最后，我们基于组合稀疏性、体积比论证和特征干扰界为这些实证发现提供了理论框架，解释了为什么组合解仅在狭窄的深度-连通性区域内可达。

</details>


### [186] [Towards Graph-Based Deep Learning for Map Generalization: Insights from Building Footprints Simplification and Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19956)
*Yanning Wang,Zhiyong Zhou,Zhouyu Liu,Mengni Yu,Yu Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次探索将图深度学习应用于地图综合中的建筑足迹简化与聚合任务，将简化建模为节点移动预测、聚合建模为链接预测。实验表明GraphSAGE在链接预测上表现较好，但节点移动预测仍具挑战，且聚合任务比简化更复杂。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 地图综合是制图学的基本任务，建筑足迹的简化与聚合尤为关键。现有深度学习方法在这两个任务上的探索不足，本文旨在首次系统地应用图深度学习来同时处理简化与聚合问题。

Method: 将简化重构为节点移动预测任务，将聚合重构为链接预测任务，并在统一的图学习框架下进行。评估了GCN、GAT和GraphSAGE三种代表性图神经网络架构在多尺度建筑数据集上的性能。

Result: GraphSAGE在链接预测准确性上表现相对较好；节点移动预测仍存在持续挑战；聚合任务比简化任务复杂度更高、更具挑战性；存在数据不平衡和需要后处理等局限性。

Conclusion: 尽管存在局限性，本研究为利用深度学习方法推进自动化地图综合提供了有价值的见解和方法论方向，指出聚合任务对当前深度学习方法捕捉高层空间关系的能力提出了更大挑战。

Original Abstract: Map generalization remains one of the fundamental tasks in cartography, especially for the simplification and aggregation of complex building footprints. This study presents the first exploratory application of graph-based deep learning to both tasks, reformulating simplification as node movement prediction and aggregation as link prediction within a unified graph learning framework. We evaluate representative graph neural network architectures (GCN, GAT, and GraphSAGE) on multi-scale building datasets, showing that GraphSAGE demonstrates relative strengths in link prediction accuracy, while also revealing persistent challenges in precise node movement prediction. Beyond quantitative performance, the results highlight that aggregation poses greater complexity and challenges than simplification, underscoring the difficulty of capturing higher-level spatial relationships in map generalization with current deep learning approaches. Although limitations such as data imbalance and the need for post-processing remain, the study provides valuable insights and methodological directions for advancing automated map generalization with deep learning approaches.

中文摘要翻译: 地图综合仍然是制图学中的基本任务之一，尤其是复杂建筑足迹的简化与聚合。本研究首次探索性地将基于图的深度学习应用于这两项任务，将简化重构为节点移动预测、将聚合重构为链接预测，并在统一的图学习框架中进行。我们在多尺度建筑数据集上评估了具有代表性的图神经网络架构（GCN、GAT和GraphSAGE），结果表明GraphSAGE在链接预测准确性上展现出相对优势，同时也揭示了节点移动预测方面存在的持续挑战。除了定量性能外，结果还表明聚合任务比简化任务具有更高的复杂性和挑战性，突显了当前深度学习方法在地图综合中捕捉高层空间关系的困难。尽管存在数据不平衡和需要后处理等局限性，本研究为利用深度学习方法推进自动化地图综合提供了有价值的见解和方法论方向。

</details>


### [187] [Low-Energy Reduced RISC-V Instruction Subset Processor for Tsetlin Machine Inference at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19964)
*Chanda Gupta,Sanidhya Bhatia,Shaurya Priyadarshi,Himani Panwar,Rishad Shafik,Sudip Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于RISC-V的专用微处理器架构和设计流程，用于Tsetlin Machine（TM）推理，通过精简指令集和优化数据路径，在保持可编程性的同时实现了高达98%的执行时间减少和平均29.7倍的能耗降低，适用于边缘AI部署。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有TM加速器设计虽然性能高，但通常依赖紧耦合接口、微码风格编程和外部主机处理器，灵活性和可编程性受限。因此需要一种既高效又可编程的TM推理解决方案。

Method: 利用RISC-V的模块化结构，设计了一个精简指令子集处理器：通过指令性能分析（Instruction Profiling）指导指令精简，随后针对TM推理简化数据路径和控制路径，最终实现专用于TM推理的RISC-V变体架构。

Result: 在多个数据集上，TM相比二值化神经网络（BNN）达到相当或更高的准确率（如CIFAR-2上88.18% vs 60.0%），执行时间减少高达98%，平均能耗降低29.7倍。

Conclusion: 所提出的精简RISC-V核心在保持可编程性的同时，显著提升了TM推理的性能和能效，证明了其作为可编程、高效边缘AI系统的有效方案。

Original Abstract: Tsetlin Machine (TM) is a logic-based machine learning approach that relies on simple bitwise operations and finite-state automata, which makes it attractive for edge AI deployments. Recent work has focused on co-processor and accelerator designs based on Tsetlin Machines (TMs). Although these designs achieve high performance, they typically depend on tightly coupled interfaces, microcode-style programming, and external host processors, limiting flexibility and ease of programming. In this work, we present a domain-specific RISC-V microprocessor architecture and design flow tailored for TM inference. Leveraging the modular structure of RISC-V, we design a reduced instruction subset processor that retains programmability while targeting improved performance and lower energy consumption for TM workloads. Instruction profiling is employed to guide instruction reduction, followed by datapath and control path simplifications tailored to TM inference. Both the baseline RV32IM core and the proposed reduced core are evaluated across multiple datasets and compared with Binarized Neural Networks (BNNs), which serve as a hardware-efficient baseline due to their reliance on bitwise operations during inference. Results show that TM achieves comparable or higher accuracy (e.g., up to 88.18% on CIFAR-2 compared to 60.0% for BNN) while reducing execution time by up to 98% across multiple datasets. Furthermore, the proposed design achieves an average $29.7\times$ reduction in energy consumption, demonstrating its effectiveness for programmable and efficient edge AI systems.

中文摘要翻译: Tsetlin Machine（TM）是一种基于逻辑的机器学习方法，依赖简单的位运算和有限状态自动机，因此非常适合边缘AI部署。近期工作聚焦于基于Tsetlin Machine（TM）的协处理器和加速器设计。尽管这些设计实现了高性能，但它们通常依赖紧耦合接口、微码风格编程以及外部主机处理器，限制了灵活性和编程便利性。本文提出了一种面向TM推理的领域专用RISC-V微处理器架构及设计流程。利用RISC-V的模块化结构，我们设计了一个精简指令子集处理器，在保持可编程性的同时，针对TM工作负载提升性能并降低能耗。通过指令性能分析指导指令精简，随后针对TM推理对数据路径和控制路径进行简化。我们在多个数据集上评估了基线RV32IM核心和所提出的精简核心，并与二值化神经网络（BNN）进行比较——后者因推理时依赖位运算而作为硬件高效的基线。结果表明，TM在多个数据集上实现了与BNN相当或更高的准确率（例如，在CIFAR-2上达到88.18%，而BNN为60.0%），同时执行时间减少高达98%。此外，所提出的设计平均实现了29.7倍的能耗降低，证明了其在可编程且高效边缘AI系统中的有效性。

</details>


### [188] [Kolmogorov-Arnold Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19984)
*Juntian Huang,Jurgen Kurths,Ying Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Kolmogorov-Arnold储层计算（KARC），用显式基函数展开替代传统储层，实现轻量级、可闭式训练的高保真动力系统预测，并桥接了储层计算与KAN网络。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统储层计算存在表示能力有限、超参数敏感或特征维度爆炸等问题，难以捕获长程依赖，亟需一种既轻量又具强表达能力的方法。

Method: 受Kolmogorov-Arnold表示定理启发，用显式基函数展开替代传统储层，设计KARC框架。该框架保留了KAN的潜在表达能力，同时支持高效的闭式训练，无需循环结构。

Result: 在偏微分方程等挑战性基准上，KARC以相近计算成本超越现有储层计算方法；还可与生成扩散模型集成用于文生图任务。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了储层计算与KAN之间的原则性桥梁，为实现高效、高保真的动力系统预测提供了新途径。

Original Abstract: Reservoir computing offers a lightweight framework for forecasting dynamical systems but may struggle to capture long-range dependencies due to limited representational capacity. Conventional reservoir computing recurrently uses trainable reservoirs with hyperparameter sensitivity, while the next-generation reservoir computing removes recurrence at the cost of rapidly growing feature dimensions. Here, we develop Kolmogorov-Arnold Reservoir Computing (KARC), which replaces reservoirs with explicit basis-function expansions inspired by the Kolmogorov-Arnold representation theorem. We rigorously show that KARC is a lightweight design of Kolmogorov-Arnold networks (KANs), preserving the potential expressive capacity of KANs while admitting efficient closed-form training of reservoir computing. At comparable cost, KARC outperforms existing reservoir computing methods on challenging benchmarks including partial differential equations. It can also be integrated with generative diffusion models for text-to-image generation. This work thus establishes a principled bridge between reservoir computing and KANs, enabling efficient and high-fidelity dynamical system forecasting.

中文摘要翻译: 储层计算为预测动力系统提供了一种轻量级框架，但由于表示能力有限，难以捕获长程依赖。传统储层计算循环使用可训练储层，存在超参数敏感性问题；而下一代储层计算去除了循环结构，但代价是特征维度迅速增长。本文提出了Kolmogorov-Arnold储层计算（KARC），受Kolmogorov-Arnold表示定理启发，用显式基函数展开替代储层。我们严格证明了KARC是Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（KAN）的一种轻量级实现，既保留了KAN的潜在表达能力，又支持储层计算的高效闭式训练。在包括偏微分方程在内的挑战性基准上，KARC以相近的计算成本超越了现有储层计算方法。它还可以与生成扩散模型集成，用于文本到图像生成。本工作从而在储层计算与KAN之间建立了原则性的桥梁，实现了高效且高保真的动力系统预测。

</details>


### [189] [Activation- and Influence-Aware Ranks (AIR): Function-Preserving SVD Compression for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19993)
*Nico Harder,Daniel Becking,Karsten Mueller,Wojciech Samek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AIR 是一种基于 SVD 的 LLM 压缩框架，引入反向信号影响度量来指导低秩近似，通过单次闭式交替最小二乘扫描实现单调下降保证，在低参数保留率下显著提升困惑度并减少校准数据需求。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有 SVD 类压缩方法（如 SVD-LLM(W)）仅考虑激活感知的最优性，未充分利用反向传播信号反映的权重重要性信息，导致压缩质量受限。

Method: 提出 AIR 框架：在 SVD-LLM(W) 的激活感知最优起点上，执行单次闭式交替最小二乘（ALS）扫描，逐元素集成反向信号影响度量，并保证单调下降。

Result: 在 ≤60% 参数保留率下，AIR 相比 SVD-LLM(W) 困惑度提升 >18%；达到相同质量所需校准数据减少约 90%；AIR 单独优于 ACIP，AIR+LoRA 进一步超越；参数节省转化为 FLOP、峰值内存和每 token 延迟的实际收益。

Conclusion: AIR 是一种层局部、与端到端方法正交兼容的高效 LLM 压缩框架，通过融合激活与影响信息在压缩率与模型质量之间取得更优平衡。

Original Abstract: We present Activation- and Influence-Aware Ranks (AIR), an SVD-based LLM compression framework that guides each weight matrix's low-rank approximation with a backward-signal influence metric. Starting from the activation-aware optimum of SVD-LLM(W), AIR runs a single closed-form alternating least squares (ALS) sweep that integrates influence element-wise under a monotone-descent guarantee. AIR is layer-local and composes orthogonally with end-to-end methods: alone it exceeds ACIP, and AIR+LoRA outperforms it further. AIR improves perplexity over SVD-LLM(W) by >18% at <=60% parameter retention, matches its quality with ~90% less calibration data, and turns parameter savings into FLOP, peak-memory, and per-token latency gains.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了激活感知与影响感知排名（AIR），一种基于 SVD 的大语言模型压缩框架，该框架利用反向信号影响度量来指导每个权重矩阵的低秩近似。从 SVD-LLM(W) 的激活感知最优起点出发，AIR 执行单次闭式交替最小二乘（ALS）扫描，在单调下降保证下逐元素集成影响信息。AIR 是层局部的，并与端到端方法正交组合：单独使用时超越 ACIP，而 AIR+LoRA 表现更优。在 ≤60% 参数保留率下，AIR 相比 SVD-LLM(W) 将困惑度提升超过 18%；达到相同质量所需校准数据减少约 90%；并将参数节省转化为 FLOP、峰值内存和每 token 延迟的实际收益。

</details>


### [190] [Connect the Dots: Training LLMs for Long-Lifecycle Agents with Cross-Domain Generalization Via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20002)
*Yanxi Chen,Weijie Shi,Yuexiang Xie,Boyi Hu,Yaliang Li,Bolin Ding,Jingren Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoD（Connect the Dots）框架，用于训练大语言模型具备长期自主智能体的元能力——在环境中持续探索、学习、更新上下文并提升后续任务表现。框架包括端到端强化学习算法和专用任务环境，实验验证了训练有效性和跨域泛化能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的LLM智能体缺乏长期自主演进能力：它们在部署后不能持续从自身经验中学习并迭代更新环境上下文以提升未来任务表现。本文旨在赋予LLM这一元能力。

Method: (1) 设计端到端强化学习算法与基础设施，支持长序列rollout，交替执行"解决任务"和"更新上下文"两种episode；(2) 构建专用任务和环境，激励并评估LLM在训练中习得该元能力；采用GRPO风格的RL算法，结合细粒度信用分配。

Result: 实验验证了CoD设置下端到端RL训练的有效性，展示出在训练域内、跨域以及从CoD到Ralph-loop设置中的分布外泛化潜力。

Conclusion: CoD框架连接了多条前人工作线索，为推进LLM和AI智能体的长期自主能力开辟了新方向，并开源实现以促进后续研究。

Original Abstract: This work presents a general framework for training large language models (LLMs) to "Connect the Dots" (CoD), a meta-capability required by long-lifecycle agents: as an LLM-based AI agent gets deployed in an environment, it solves a long sequence of tasks while continuously exploring the environment, learning from its own experiences, and iteratively self-updating its context about the environment, thereby achieving progressively better performance on future tasks conditioned on the updated context. Major components of the CoD framework include: (1) algorithm design and infrastructure for end-to-end reinforcement learning (RL) with long rollout sequences interleaving solve-task and update-context episodes; (2) tasks and environments for incentivizing and eliciting the targeted meta-capability in LLMs during training, as well as for faithfully measuring progress during evaluation. We present proof-of-concept implementations of the CoD framework, including a GRPO-style RL algorithm with fine-grained credit assignment, as well as tasks and environments tailored to the targeted meta-capability (rather than domain-specific LLM capabilities or standard task-by-task RL). Empirical results validate the efficacy of end-to-end RL training in the CoD setting, and demonstrate the potential for out-of-distribution generalization -- within the training domains, across different domains, and from CoD to Ralph-loop settings -- of the elicited meta-capability. Our investigation of CoD connects several lines of prior works, and opens up new opportunities for advancing LLMs and AI agents. To facilitate further research and applications, we release our implementations at \url{https://github.com/agentscope-ai/Trinity-RFT/tree/research/cod/examples/research_cod}.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出一个通用框架，用于训练大语言模型（LLMs）掌握"串联点"（Connect the Dots, CoD）能力——这是长期生命周期智能体所需的一种元能力：基于LLM的AI智能体在环境中部署时，需要解决一长串任务，同时持续探索环境、从自身经验中学习，并迭代地自我更新关于环境的上下文，从而在更新后的上下文条件下，在未来的任务上逐步取得更好的表现。CoD框架的主要组成部分包括：(1) 端到端强化学习（RL）的算法设计与基础设施，支持长序列rollout，其中交替执行"解决任务"和"更新上下文"两种episode；(2) 用于在训练过程中激励和引导LLM产生目标元能力的任务与环境，以及用于在评估中忠实衡量进展的任务与环境。我们提供了CoD框架的概念验证实现，包括一种采用细粒度信用分配的GRPO风格RL算法，以及针对该目标元能力（而非领域特定的LLM能力或标准逐任务RL）量身定制的任务与环境。实验结果验证了CoD设置下端到端RL训练的有效性，并展示了所习得元能力在分布外泛化方面的潜力——包括在训练域内、跨不同域、以及从CoD到Ralph-loop设置中的泛化。我们对CoD的研究连接了多条前人工作线索，为推进LLM和AI智能体开辟了新机会。为促进进一步的研究与应用，我们在 \url{https://github.com/agentscope-ai/Trinity-RFT/tree/research/cod/examples/research_cod} 开源了实现代码。

</details>


### [191] [StreamKL: Fast and Memory-Efficient KL Divergence for Boosting Attention Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20005)
*Guangda Liu,Yiquan Wang,Chengwei Li,Wenhao Chen,Jing Lin,Yiwu Yao,Danning Ke,Wenchao Ding,Jieru Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StreamKL 提出了一种融合的 GPU 原语，用于高效计算注意力分布的 KL 散度，将显存占用从 O(N_Q N_K) 降至 O(1)，并实现单次前向/反向传播，显著加速长序列上的注意力蒸馏。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有注意力蒸馏方法需要物化两个注意力分布矩阵后再计算 KL 散度，导致 O(N_Q N_K) 的显存和 IO 开销，在长上下文场景下变得不可接受。

Method: 提出 StreamKL 融合 GPU 原语：前向传播中设计新颖的在线双分布 KL 约简公式，通过流式处理查询-键分块，在片上 SRAM 中完成计算，避免物化完整注意力矩阵；反向传播中逐块重计算注意力概率，避免存储二次中间结果。同时实现了专用的 GPU 内核优化。

Result: 前向传播相比基线方法加速最高达 43 倍，反向传播加速最高达 14 倍；将注意力蒸馏的额外 HBM 占用从 O(N_Q N_K) 降至 O(1)，使得单 GPU 上即可进行长上下文蒸馏。

Conclusion: StreamKL 通过在线计算和逐块重计算策略，从根本上解决了注意力 KL 散度计算中的二次显存开销问题，为长上下文场景下的注意力蒸馏提供了高效可行的方案。

Original Abstract: Attention distillation, which trains one attention distribution to match another by minimizing their Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence, is widely used in knowledge distillation, model compression, continual learning, and sparse-attention LLM training. However, existing approaches materialize both attention distributions before computing the KL reduction, incurring $O(N_QN_K)$ memory and IO costs that become prohibitive at long context lengths. We present StreamKL, the first fused GPU primitive for attention KL divergence that eliminates this quadratic materialization. StreamKL derives a novel online formulation for the coupled two-distribution KL reduction, enabling a single one-pass forward kernel that streams query-key tiles through on-chip SRAM. For the backward pass, StreamKL recomputes attention probabilities tile-by-tile, avoiding storage of quadratic intermediates. We further design and implement efficient GPU kernels with dedicated optimizations. Experiments show StreamKL delivers up to $43\times$ and $14\times$ speedups over baseline methods in the forward and backward passes, respectively. Most importantly, StreamKL reduces the extra HBM footprint of attention distillation from $O(N_QN_K)$ to $O(1)$, enabling long-context distillation on a single GPU.

中文摘要翻译: 注意力蒸馏通过最小化两个注意力分布之间的 Kullback-Leibler (KL) 散度来训练一个分布匹配另一个分布，广泛应用于知识蒸馏、模型压缩、持续学习和稀疏注意力大语言模型训练中。然而，现有方法在计算 KL 约简之前需要物化两个注意力分布，导致 O(N_Q N_K) 的显存和 IO 开销，在长上下文场景下变得难以承受。我们提出 StreamKL，这是首个用于注意力 KL 散度计算的融合 GPU 原语，消除了这种二次物化开销。StreamKL 推导了一种新颖的在线双分布 KL 约简公式，使得单个一次前向内核能够通过片上 SRAM 流式处理查询-键分块。对于反向传播，StreamKL 逐块重计算注意力概率，避免存储二次中间结果。我们进一步设计并实现了带有专门优化的高效 GPU 内核。实验表明，StreamKL 在前向和反向传播中分别比基线方法加速高达 43 倍和 14 倍。最重要的是，StreamKL 将注意力蒸馏的额外 HBM 占用从 O(N_Q N_K) 降至 O(1)，使得单 GPU 上即可进行长上下文蒸馏。

</details>


### [192] [VIMPO: Value-Implicit Policy Optimization for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20008)
*Zhewei Kang,Aosong Feng,Sergey Levine,Dawn Song,Xuandong Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VIMPO提出了一种无需评论家的策略优化方法，通过KL正则化强化学习的最优性条件推导出策略隐含的价值函数，在数学推理任务上优于GRPO，特别是在竞争性数学基准上提升显著。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有RLVR方法面临简单性与信用分配之间的权衡：基于组的相对方法（如GRPO）虽无需评论家，但只能为每个轨迹分配整体优势；而演员-评论家方法能提供更密集的学习信号，但需要学习价值函数，存在训练不稳定的问题。作者希望在不引入额外评论家网络的前提下，实现更精细的信用分配。

Method: VIMPO从KL正则化强化学习的最优性条件出发，推导出策略隐含的价值函数（policy-implied value function）。对于自回归生成，该价值递归可用策略-参考对数比率表示，并以轨迹末端无未来奖励作为终止条件。由此得到简单的价值损失（无需评论家），同时推导出无需评论家的演员优势，将奖励融入（通过价值损失）与策略改进（通过PPO风格的演员更新）分离开来。

Result: 在数学RLVR基准上，VIMPO在MATH-500、AIME 2024、AIME 2025和OlympiadBench上均优于GRPO，在竞争性数学评估中提升尤为显著。在含噪声奖励场景下，VIMPO仍保持对GRPO的持续优势。

Conclusion: 策略隐含价值优化（VIMPO）能够在保持无评论家训练实际简单性的同时，实现更精细的信用分配，是一种有效且实用的策略优化方法。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards has become a central tool for improving the reasoning ability of large language models, but current methods face a trade-off between simplicity and credit assignment. Group-relative methods such as GRPO avoid training a critic, but typically assign a trajectory-level advantage to every token. Actor-critic methods provide denser learning signals, but require a learned value function with its own training instability. We introduce VIMPO, a critic-free policy optimization method that derives a policy-implied value function from the optimality conditions of KL-regularized reinforcement learning. For autoregressive generation, the resulting value recurrence can be written in terms of policy-reference log-ratios and anchored by the terminal condition that no future reward remains at the end of a trajectory. This gives a simple value loss that incorporates outcome-level verifiable rewards without training a critic. The same derivation also yields a critic-free actor advantage, allowing VIMPO to separate reward incorporation through the value loss from policy improvement through a PPO-style actor update. On mathematical RLVR benchmarks, VIMPO improves over GRPO across MATH-500, AIME 2024, AIME 2025, and OlympiadBench, with especially larger gains on competition-style evaluations. Under noisy rewards, VIMPO retains a consistent advantage over GRPO, suggesting that policy-implied value optimization can provide finer credit assignment while preserving the practical simplicity of critic-free training.

中文摘要翻译: 基于可验证奖励的强化学习已成为提升大语言模型推理能力的核心工具，但现有方法在简单性与信用分配之间存在权衡。基于组的相对方法（如GRPO）无需训练评论家，但通常为每个轨迹中的所有令牌分配相同的轨迹级优势。演员-评论家方法能提供更密集的学习信号，但需要学习价值函数，且存在自身的训练不稳定性问题。我们提出VIMPO，一种无需评论家的策略优化方法，它从KL正则化强化学习的最优性条件出发，推导出策略隐含的价值函数。对于自回归生成，所得的价值递归可以用策略-参考对数比率表示，并以轨迹末端无未来奖励的终止条件作为锚点。这产生了一个简单的价值损失，无需训练评论家即可融入结果级可验证奖励。相同的推导还得到了无需评论家的演员优势，使VIMPO能够将奖励融入（通过价值损失）与策略改进（通过PPO风格的演员更新）分离开来。在数学RLVR基准上，VIMPO在MATH-500、AIME 2024、AIME 2025和OlympiadBench上均优于GRPO，在竞争性数学评估中提升尤为显著。在含噪声奖励场景下，VIMPO仍保持对GRPO的持续优势，表明策略隐含价值优化能够在保持无评论家训练实际简单性的同时，实现更精细的信用分配。

</details>


### [193] [Self-Adaptive Scale Handling for Forecasting Time Series with Scale Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20010)
*Xu Zhang,Zhengang Huang,Yunzhi Wu,Xun Lu,Erpeng Qi,Yunkai Chen,Zhongya Xue,Peng Wang,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对时间序列预测中因尺度异质性（不同序列数值量级差异大）导致现有归一化方法失效的问题，提出自适应尺度处理模块（AS），通过学习自适应尺度因子保留语义判别性并减少逆缩放误差，在真实基金销售数据集上验证了其有效性和通用性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有TSF研究多聚焦于尺度同质数据，但真实工业场景中不同时间序列常存在数量级差异（尺度异质性）。现有方法如全局归一化会压缩低尺度信号，基于窗口的缩放则破坏语义判别性并放大逆缩放误差，因此需要一种能自适应处理尺度异质性的方法。

Method: 提出自适应尺度处理模块（AS），包含两个子模块：尺度校准（SC）通过神经网络学习校准先验均值尺度因子；尺度选择（SS）决定是否应用校准或保留原始因子，以避免过度校准。AS模块可无缝集成到现有TSF模型中。

Result: 在蚂蚁财富和支付宝的真实基金销售数据集上进行的实验表明，AS模块可无缝集成到多种主流TSF模型中，并一致地提升其性能。

Conclusion: AS模块能有效处理时间序列预测中的尺度异质性，保留语义判别性的同时减少逆缩放误差，具有通用性和有效性。

Original Abstract: Current time series forecasting (TSF) research predominantly focuses on scale-homogeneous data, where different time series share similar numerical magnitude ranges. However, in real-world industrial scenarios such as financial product sales, different time series often differ by orders of magnitude (scale heterogeneity). Since these series share similar temporal patterns, joint modeling is desirable for better data utilization, yet existing scaling methods either compress low-scale signals (global normalization) or destroy semantic discriminability and amplify inverse-scaling errors (window-based scaling). This paper proposes a self-Adaptive Scale-handling (AS) module that learns adaptive scale factors tailored to each input, preserving semantic discriminability while reducing inverse-scaling errors. AS consists of Scale Calibrating (SC), which calibrates prior mean scaling factors through neural networks, and Scaling Selection (SS), which decides whether to apply calibration or retain the original factor, avoiding over-calibration. Experiments on real-world fund sales datasets from Ant Fortune and Alipay show that AS seamlessly integrates into popular TSF models and consistently improves their performance. The code and dataset are available at the link https://github.com/Meteor-Stars/ASTSF.

中文摘要翻译: 当前时间序列预测（TSF）研究主要关注尺度同质数据，即不同时间序列具有相近的数值量级范围。然而，在金融产品销售等真实工业场景中，不同时间序列往往存在数量级上的差异（尺度异质性）。由于这些序列共享相似的时间模式，联合建模有助于更好地利用数据，但现有的缩放方法要么压缩低尺度信号（全局归一化），要么破坏语义判别性并放大逆缩放误差（基于窗口的缩放）。本文提出了一种自适应尺度处理（AS）模块，该模块学习适应每个输入的自适应尺度因子，在保留语义判别性的同时减少逆缩放误差。AS由两个部分组成：尺度校准（SC），通过神经网络校准先验均值尺度因子；以及尺度选择（SS），决定是否应用校准或保留原始因子，避免过度校准。在蚂蚁财富和支付宝的真实基金销售数据集上的实验表明，AS模块可无缝集成到主流TSF模型中，并一致地提升其性能。代码和数据集可在链接 https://github.com/Meteor-Stars/ASTSF 获取。

</details>


### [194] [Hierarchical Control in Multi-Agent Games: LLM-based Planning and RL Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20014)
*Jannik Hösch,Alessandro Sestini,Florian Fuchs,Amir Baghi,Joakim Bergdahl,Konrad Tollmar,Jean-Philippe Barrette-LaPierre,Linus Gisslén*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种分层架构，将预训练大语言模型(LLM)作为集中式策略控制器，协调多个专用RL技能策略，在2v2对抗环境中实现与手工行为树相当的性能，并显著提升人类对智能体行为的感知真实感。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 强化学习在复杂多智能体环境中面临稀疏奖励、巨大的状态-动作空间以及学习协调策略的困难，亟需一种能够有效协调多智能体协作的新方法。

Method: 提出分层架构：预训练LLM作为中央战略控制器，负责选择团队中各智能体的专用RL技能策略；RL策略则负责反应式的底层执行。在2v2 King of the Hill对抗环境中进行评测。

Result: 1) LLM+RL系统与手工行为树胜率相当(46.4% vs 51.5%, p=0.103)，两者均显著优于无技能分解的端到端Flat RL；2) 用户研究(n=15)中60%参与者认为LLM+RL智能体最具人类行为特征(p=0.027)，认可其行为适应性和战术多样性。

Conclusion: 预训练LLM的推理能力可以有效协调预训练的RL技能，在无需手工规则工程的前提下实现具有竞争力的多智能体协调和更优的感知可信度。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has achieved strong performance in sequential decision-making, yet scaling to complex multi-agent environments remains challenging due to sparse rewards, large state-action spaces, and the difficulty of learning coordinated strategies. We propose a hierarchical architecture where a pretrained large language model (LLM) acts as a centralized strategic controller that selects among specialized RL skill policies for a team of agents, while RL policies handle reactive low-level execution. We evaluate this hybrid system in a competitive 2v2 King of the Hill environment against behavior tree (BT) and \emph{``Flat''} RL (end-to-end training without skill decomposition) baselines. The LLM+RL system achieves task performance statistically equivalent to hand-crafted BT (46.4\% vs 51.5\% win rate, $p=0.103$) while both significantly outperform Flat RL trained without skill decomposition. A user study ($n=15$) reveals that 60\% of participants perceive LLM+RL agents as the most human-like ($p=0.027$), citing behavioral adaptability and tactical variability. These results demonstrate that pretrained LLM reasoning can effectively orchestrate pretrained RL skills, achieving competitive multi-agent coordination and superior perceived believability without manual rule engineering.

中文摘要翻译: 强化学习（RL）在序列决策中取得了强劲性能，但扩展到复杂的多智能体环境仍面临挑战，原因在于稀疏奖励、巨大的状态-动作空间以及学习协调策略的困难。我们提出一种分层架构，其中预训练的大语言模型（LLM）作为集中式战略控制器，为智能体团队选择专门的RL技能策略，而RL策略则负责反应式的底层执行。我们在竞技性2v2 King of the Hill环境中评估了这一混合系统，与行为树（BT）和"扁平化"RL（无技能分解的端到端训练）基线进行比较。LLM+RL系统达到了与手工设计BT统计上相当的任务性能（胜率46.4% vs 51.5%，p=0.103），而两者均显著优于未经技能分解训练的Flat RL。一项用户研究（n=15）显示，60%的参与者认为LLM+RL智能体最具类人特征（p=0.027），并提及行为适应性和战术多样性。这些结果表明，预训练LLM的推理能力可以有效协调预训练的RL技能，在无需手工规则工程的情况下实现具有竞争力的多智能体协调和更优的感知可信度。

</details>


### [195] [Adaptive Distance-Aware Trunk Deep Operator Learning for Long-Span Roadway Bridges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20015)
*Bilal Ahmed,Diab W. Abueidda,Waleed El-Sekelly,Tarek Abdoun,Mostafa E. Mobasher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自适应主干深度算子网络(AD-DeepONet)，用于大跨度桥梁局部响应预测，通过KNN构建负载依赖学习域、距离感知特征和刚度信息Schur补全场重建，在保持有限元精度(误差<5%)的同时实现约60倍计算加速。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 大跨度桥梁在车辆荷载下表现出高度局部化的结构响应，传统有限元分析计算成本高昂，现有科学机器学习方法难以准确捕捉这种局部化响应模式。

Method: 自适应主干DeepONet框架：1) 利用KNN策略动态构建负载依赖学习域；2) 通过距离感知特征编码荷载与结构节点的几何关系增强主干网络；3) 采用刚度信息Schur补公式实现物理信息全场重建；4) 使用降阶等效壳模型生成训练数据。

Result: 在基准桥梁模型和真实Mussafah大桥上验证，相对误差低于5%达到有限元精度；总响应评估时间减少约60倍；排除后处理重建步骤，推理速度比有限元快四个数量级；支持快速生成任意车辆荷载配置下的全场响应、影响线和影响面。

Conclusion: 所提AD-DeepONet框架能高效准确地预测大跨度桥梁局部响应，显著降低计算成本，适用于大规模桥梁分析和数字孪生应用。

Original Abstract: Long-span roadway bridges exhibit highly localized structural responses under vehicular loading, making repeated FE analysis computationally expensive for applications such as influence surface generation and structural digital twins. Existing SciML approaches struggle to accurately capture these localized responses. To address this challenge, this study proposes an adaptive-trunk DeepONet for localized structural response prediction in large-scale bridge systems. The framework dynamically constructs a load-dependent learning domain using a KNN strategy, allowing the network to focus on structural influence zones. The trunk network is further enhanced using distance-aware features that encode the geometric relationship between the load and structural nodes. A physics-based full-field reconstruction is incorporated through a stiffness-informed Schur complement formulation, enabling predictions at adaptive nodes to be extended to the entire structural domain. To enable scalable training, response data are generated using a reduced-order equivalent shell model that preserves the dominant global behavior while significantly reducing computational cost. The proposed framework is validated on both a benchmark bridge model and the real-world Mussafah Bridge. Results show that the method achieves FEM-level accuracy with relative errors below 5%, while reducing the total response evaluation time (including full-field reconstruction) by approximately 60x; excluding the post-processing reconstruction step, the AD-DeepONet inference is up to four orders of magnitude faster than FEM. In addition, the framework enables rapid generation of full-field responses, influence lines, and influence surfaces under arbitrary vehicular loading configurations, demonstrating strong potential for large-scale bridge analysis and digital twin applications.

中文摘要翻译: 大跨度桥梁在车辆荷载作用下呈现高度局部化的结构响应，这使得重复有限元分析在影响面生成和结构数字孪生等应用中计算成本高昂。现有科学机器学习方法难以准确捕捉这些局部化响应。为解决这一挑战，本研究提出了一种自适应主干深度算子网络(AD-DeepONet)，用于大规模桥梁系统中的局部结构响应预测。该框架利用KNN策略动态构建荷载依赖的学习域，使网络能够聚焦于结构影响区域。主干网络通过距离感知特征进一步增强，这些特征编码了荷载与结构节点之间的几何关系。通过刚度信息Schur补公式实现基于物理场的全场重建，使得自适应节点上的预测可以扩展到整个结构域。为实现可扩展训练，采用降阶等效壳模型生成响应数据，该模型在保留主要全局行为的同时显著降低计算成本。所提框架在基准桥梁模型和真实Mussafah大桥上进行了验证。结果表明，该方法在相对误差低于5%的情况下达到有限元级别的精度，同时将总响应评估时间（包括全场重建）减少约60倍；排除后处理重建步骤，AD-DeepONet推理速度比有限元快四个数量级。此外，该框架能够在任意车辆荷载配置下快速生成全场响应、影响线和影响面，展现出在大规模桥梁分析和数字孪生应用中的巨大潜力。

</details>


### [196] [Exploring the potential of AlphaEarth and TESSERA embeddings for Fine-scale Local Climate Zone Mapping: A case study across five cities in Switzerland](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20034)
*Htet Yamin Ko Ko,Clement Atzberger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究比较了TESSERA和AlphaEarth等基础模型嵌入与传统Sentinel-1/2数据，在瑞士五个城市中使用注意力U-Net将粗分辨率（~100米）LCZ地图升尺度至10米分辨率。TESSERA表现最优，证明了EO基础模型嵌入在减少预处理和特征工程方面的潜力，但跨年迁移仍具挑战。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 许多城市仍使用约100米粗分辨率的局部气候区（LCZ）记录，难以满足精细尺度城市研究需求，亟需高效的升尺度方法。

Method: 使用基于注意力的U-Net架构，比较TESSERA和AlphaEarth的预计算嵌入与传统Sentinel-1/2复合数据，将粗分辨率LCZ地图升尺度至10米分辨率。设置三项实验评估多城市迁移性、更高分辨率参考数据的影响以及年际物候变化的时序鲁棒性。

Result: 所有数据集在实验一和二中IoU分别达到0.59-0.69和0.77-0.82。TESSERA在两个实验中均一致优于S1S2和AlphaEarth。基于嵌入的模型跨年迁移仍具挑战。

Conclusion: EO基础模型嵌入在减少耗时预处理和手动特征工程方面具有潜力，结合简单的位置感知注意力U-Net可增强区域迁移性和可扩展性，有助于开发全球城市气候应用的精细尺度LCZ地图。提升参考数据质量仍是进一步提高精度的最强手段。

Original Abstract: Understanding urban spatial morphology is critical for climate modeling, risk assessment, and sustainable urban design, and Local Climate Zone (LCZ) mapping provides the basic framework for this. However, many cities still use coarse ~100-m resolution LCZ records, which are unsuitable for fine-scale urban research. In this study, precomputed embeddings from TESSERA (Feng et al., 2025) and AlphaEarth (Brown et al., 2025) are compared to traditional Sentinel-1/2 (S1S2) composites in five Swiss cities to see if they can upscale coarse LCZ maps to 10-m resolution using an attention-based U-Net. Three experiments assess multi-city transferability, the impact of higher-resolution reference data, and temporal robustness to year-to-year phenology changes. We find that all datasets achieve strong performance with test data Intersection-over-Union (IoU) ranging from 0.59-0.69 and 0.77-0.82 in the first two experiments. TESSERA consistently outperforms both S1S2 and AlphaEarth across both settings As expected, we find that the transfer of embedding-based models from one year to another remains an open challenge. Overall, however, our results demonstrate the promising potential of embeddings derived from EO foundation models to reduce time consuming preprocessing, respectively, manual feature engineering tasks and to guide a universal deep learning-based LCZ mapping workflow. When combined with a simple location-aware attention U-Net architecture, the embeddings enhance regional transferability and scalability, supporting the development of comprehensive and reproducible fine-scale LCZ maps for global urban climate applications Improving reference data quality remains the strongest lever for further accuracy gains.

中文摘要翻译: 理解城市空间形态对于气候建模、风险评估和可持续城市设计至关重要，而局部气候区（LCZ）制图为此提供了基础框架。然而，许多城市仍在使用约100米粗分辨率的LCZ记录，这不适合精细尺度的城市研究。在本研究中，我们将TESSERA（Feng等人，2025）和AlphaEarth（Brown等人，2025）的预计算嵌入与传统的Sentinel-1/2（S1S2）复合数据在瑞士五个城市中进行比较，以探究它们能否使用基于注意力的U-Net将粗分辨率LCZ地图升尺度至10米分辨率。我们设计了三项实验来评估多城市迁移性、更高分辨率参考数据的影响以及年际物候变化的时间鲁棒性。我们发现，所有数据集在实验一和实验二中均表现强劲，测试数据的交并比（IoU）分别达到0.59-0.69和0.77-0.82。TESSERA在这两种设置下均一致优于S1S2和AlphaEarth。正如预期，我们发现基于嵌入的模型在不同年份之间的迁移仍然是一个有待解决的挑战。然而，总体而言，我们的结果表明，来自地球观测（EO）基础模型的嵌入在减少耗时预处理和手动特征工程任务方面具有巨大潜力，并可引导基于深度学习的通用LCZ制图流程。当与简单的位置感知注意力U-Net架构相结合时，这些嵌入增强了区域迁移性和可扩展性，有助于为全球城市气候应用开发全面且可复现的精细尺度LCZ地图。改善参考数据质量仍然是进一步提高精度的最强手段。

</details>


### [197] [Alzheimer's Disease Diagnosis using a Multimodal Approach with 3D MRI and PET](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20037)
*Loukas Ilias,Anthi-Maria Vozinaki,Christos Ntanos,Dimitris Askounis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将3D卷积特征提取器与三种融合策略（拼接、门控多模态单元、门控自注意力）及稀疏门控混合专家（MoE）分类器结合，通过输入自适应路由实现MRI与PET的多模态融合，用于阿尔茨海默病早期诊断。实验表明，门控自注意力在MCI vs. AD任务上达到82.08%准确率，且消融实验证实MoE对性能至关重要。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有多模态模型大多采用静态拼接方式融合MRI和PET，并对所有受试者应用相同的计算，这限制了模型对患者/中心异质性的鲁棒性，且浪费计算资源。

Method: 1) 使用3D卷积神经网络提取MRI和PET特征；2) 对比三种融合策略：静态拼接、门控多模态单元（GMU）、门控自注意力；3) 采用稀疏门控混合专家（MoE）分类器实现输入自适应路由，仅激活每个样本最相关的专家；4) 利用Grad-CAM可视化疾病相关区域，增强模型可解释性。

Result: 在NC vs. MCI、MCI vs. AD、NC vs. AD三个二分类任务上：GMU在NC vs. MCI达80.46%，NC vs. AD达95.47%；门控自注意力在MCI vs. AD达82.08%。消融实验表明，移除MoE会导致所有任务的准确率一致下降。

Conclusion: 输入自适应的多模态建模能够有效利用MRI和PET的互补特性，提升AD诊断性能；稀疏门控MoE分类器通过动态专家路由增强模型鲁棒性与计算效率。

Original Abstract: Alzheimer's disease (AD) is an irreversible neurodegenerative disorder and a leading cause of death worldwide. Early diagnosis plays an important part especially at the Mild Cognitive Impairment stage, where timely intervention can help slow its progression before it advances to AD. Neuroimaging data, like Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI) and Positron Emission Tomography (PET) scans, can help detect brain changes early by providing structural and functional brain changes related to the disease. Yet, many multimodal models still fuse MRI and PET with static concatenation and apply identical computation to all subjects, which limits robustness to patient/site heterogeneity and can waste computation. To address these limitations, we present the first study of combining 3D convolutional feature extractors with three fusion strategies - concatenation, Gated Multimodal Unit (GMU), and gated self-attention - and a sparsely gated Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) classifier that performs input-adaptive routing, activating only the most informative experts per case. Finally, we utilize Grad-CAM to visualize disease-related regions, ensuring model interpretability. Experiments are performed across three binary classification tasks (NC vs. MCI, MCI vs. AD, and NC vs. AD). Results show that GMU achieves accuracies of 80.46 % (NC vs. MCI) and 95.47 % (NC vs. AD), while gated self-attention attains 82.08 % on MCI vs. AD. Ablations show that removing the MoE consistently degrades accuracy across all tasks. These findings underscore the value of input-adaptive, multimodal modeling for AD diagnosis by leveraging the complementary nature of MRI and PET.

中文摘要翻译: 阿尔茨海默病（AD）是一种不可逆的神经退行性疾病，也是全球范围内导致死亡的主要原因之一。早期诊断尤为重要，尤其是在轻度认知障碍（MCI）阶段，及时的干预可以帮助延缓其向AD的进展。神经影像学数据，如磁共振成像（MRI）和正电子发射断层扫描（PET），能够通过提供与疾病相关的脑部结构和功能变化，帮助早期检测脑部改变。然而，许多多模态模型仍然采用静态拼接的方式融合MRI和PET，并对所有受试者应用相同的计算，这限制了模型对患者/中心异质性的鲁棒性，并可能造成计算浪费。为解决这些局限，本文首次提出了将3D卷积特征提取器与三种融合策略——拼接（concatenation）、门控多模态单元（GMU）和门控自注意力（gated self-attention）——以及稀疏门控混合专家（MoE）分类器相结合的方法，该分类器执行输入自适应路由，仅激活每个病例中最具信息量的专家。最后，我们利用Grad-CAM对疾病相关区域进行可视化，确保模型的可解释性。实验在三个二分类任务（NC vs. MCI、MCI vs. AD、NC vs. AD）上进行。结果表明，GMU在NC vs. MCI和NC vs. AD上分别达到80.46%和95.47%的准确率，而门控自注意力在MCI vs. AD上达到82.08%。消融实验显示，移除MoE会导致所有任务的准确率一致下降。这些发现凸显了通过利用MRI和PET的互补特性，采用输入自适应的多模态建模方法在AD诊断中的价值。

</details>


### [198] [Comparative Study of Neural Surrogate Architectures for Autoregressive Prediction of Internal Battery States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20053)
*Gihyun Lee,Thorben Menne,Simon Olma,Jakob Hilgert,Sangyoung Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了四种神经网络架构（MLP、ResNet、U-Net、FNO）作为自回归状态转移算子，用于替代DFN模型实时预测锂离子电池内部电化学状态。结果表明，U-Net凭借其多尺度空间归纳偏置在300步自回归推理中达到3%的nRMSE，并实现5.38倍加速，为下一代电池管理系统和数字孪生的发展提供了关键支撑。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: DFN模型虽然能高保真地解析锂离子电池内部电化学状态，但其数值求解计算成本极高，难以在实时场景中部署，且从单体扩展到电池包和车队规模时面临严重瓶颈。现有的机器学习代理模型大多学习特定工况下的解近似，缺乏泛化能力，无法学习可迁移的状态演化动力学。

Method: 在统一框架下，将四种神经网络架构（MLP、ResNet、U-Net、FNO）构建为自回归状态转移算子，采用多步展开（multi-step unrolling）和电流条件化（current-conditioning）进行训练，以隔离空间归纳偏置对模型性能的影响，系统比较各架构在全工况范围内预测DFN完整内部状态的能力。

Result: U-Net凭借其多尺度特征层次结构，在300步自回归展开后，所有内部状态变量的平均最终步nRMSE达到3%，同时相比数值求解器实现了5.38倍的加速。

Conclusion: 空间归纳偏置是影响代理模型性能的关键决定因素，U-Net的优异表现推动了用于内部状态可观测性的代理模型发展，为下一代电池管理系统和数字孪生技术奠定了基础。

Original Abstract: The Doyle-Fuller-Newman (DFN) model resolves internal electrochemical states in lithium-ion batteries with high fidelity. However, the numerical solution of its governing equations is computationally prohibitive for real-time deployment, limiting scalability from individual cells to pack and fleet-scale applications. While machine learning surrogates can substantially reduce inference latency through GPU acceleration, most existing approaches learn solution approximations tied to specific operating conditions rather than learning generalizable state-evolution dynamics. This work presents a systematic comparison of four neural network architectures (MLP, ResNet, U-Net, FNO) formulated as autoregressive state-transition operators that predict full DFN internal states across a wide range of operating conditions. To ensure a controlled architectural comparison, all models are trained under a unified framework using multi-step unrolling and current-conditioning, isolating the impact of spatial inductive bias. Results demonstrate that the U-Net's multi-scale feature hierarchy achieves a mean final-step nRMSE of 3% averaged across all internal state variables after 300-step autoregressive rollouts, while providing a 5.38x speed-up over the numerical solver. These findings highlight spatial inductive bias as a critical determinant of surrogate performance, advancing the development of surrogates for internal state observability for next-generation battery management systems and digital twins.

中文摘要翻译: Doyle-Fuller-Newman (DFN) 模型能以高保真度解析锂离子电池的内部电化学状态。然而，其控制方程的数值求解在实时部署中计算成本过高，限制了从单体到电池包及车队级应用的可扩展性。虽然机器学习代理模型可以通过GPU加速显著降低推理延迟，但现有方法大多学习特定工况下的解近似，而非学习可泛化的状态演化动力学。本文系统比较了四种神经网络架构（MLP、ResNet、U-Net、FNO），将其构建为自回归状态转移算子，能够在广泛的工况范围内预测完整的DFN内部状态。为确保可控的架构比较，所有模型均在统一框架下采用多步展开和电流条件化进行训练，从而隔离空间归纳偏置的影响。结果表明，U-Net凭借其多尺度特征层次结构，在300步自回归展开后，所有内部状态变量的平均最终步归一化均方根误差（nRMSE）达到3%，同时相比数值求解器实现了5.38倍的加速。这些发现突显了空间归纳偏置是决定代理模型性能的关键因素，为下一代电池管理系统和数字孪生中内部状态可观测性的代理模型开发提供了重要推进。

</details>


### [199] [PaAno+: Multiscale Encoding and Cross-Variable Attention for Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20055)
*Youji Zhu,Hongbing Wang,Wenchao Liu,Xiaodong Liu,Xiangguang Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级高效的时间序列异常检测模型PaAno，基于补丁导向的表示学习范式，通过多尺度特征提取、跨变量融合注意力及时间补丁窗口排序 pretext 任务，在TSB-AD基准上实现了SOTA检测精度，同时具备优异的计算效率，适合资源受限终端部署。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于Transformer和大模型的异常检测方法计算开销过大，而轻量级方案存在特征提取不足、多变量依赖建模不充分的问题，亟需一种既轻量又高效的检测模型。

Method: (1) 编码器模块：使用差异化感受野的卷积核构建多尺度特征提取骨干，捕获层次化时序特征；结合跨尺度自适应注意力聚合与残差连接优化，稳定特征表示学习。(2) 嵌入交叉变量融合注意力模块，显式建模变量间相关性。(3) 设计基于时间补丁窗口排序的新型前置任务，揭示时间序列内在结构特性；利用三元组损失优化补丁嵌入空间，提升特征判别能力。

Result: 在TSB-AD基准上，PaAno在单变量和多变量异常检测任务中均取得了最先进的检测精度，在VUS-PR等评估指标上相比原始PaAno有显著性能提升，同时计算效率优越，可在资源受限终端上实现实时推理。

Conclusion: PaAno通过紧凑的网络设计和创新的补丁级表示学习，在保持轻量高效的同时实现了顶尖的异常检测性能，验证了其在实际工业与医疗监控场景中的部署潜力。

Original Abstract: Time-series anomaly detection has significant practical value for industrial and medical monitoring, as well as other critical domains. Current Transformer- and large-model-based detection approaches incur excessive computational overhead, while existing lightweight alternatives are constrained by insufficient feature extraction and inadequate modeling of dependencies across multivariate variables. To mitigate the above drawbacks, this study develops a lightweight, efficient anomaly detection model, dubbed PaAno, within the patch-oriented representation learning paradigm. In the encoder module, a multiscale feature-extraction backbone is constructed using convolutional kernels with differentiated receptive fields to capture hierarchical temporal characteristics; subsequent cross-scale adaptive attention aggregation, combined with residual connection optimization, further stabilizes feature representation learning. A cross-variable fusion attention module is embedded to explicitly characterize inter-variable correlations, empowering the model to identify anomalous patterns amid intricate operational conditions. Moreover, a novel pretext task based on temporal patch-window sorting is customized to uncover intrinsic structural properties of time series, and triplet loss is leveraged to optimize the patch embedding space for enhanced feature discrimination. Extensive experiments on the TSB-AD benchmark demonstrate that the proposed PaAno achieves state-of-the-art detection accuracy on both univariate and multivariate tasks, yielding significant performance gains across evaluation metrics, including VUS-PR, relative to the original PaAno. Leveraging a compact network design, the presented model achieves favorable computational efficiency, enabling deployment on resource-limited terminals for real-time anomaly inference.

中文摘要翻译: 时间序列异常检测在工业和医疗监测以及其他关键领域中具有重要的实际价值。当前基于Transformer和大模型的检测方法计算开销过大，而现有的轻量级替代方案受限于特征提取不充分以及对多变量间依赖关系建模不足。为缓解上述问题，本研究在补丁导向的表示学习范式下，开发了一种轻量、高效的异常检测模型，命名为PaAno。在编码器模块中，采用具有差异化感受野的卷积核构建多尺度特征提取骨干，以捕获层次化的时序特征；随后结合跨尺度自适应注意力聚合与残差连接优化，进一步稳定特征表示学习。嵌入交叉变量融合注意力模块，显式刻画变量间相关性，使模型能够在复杂运行条件下识别异常模式。此外，定制了一种基于时间补丁窗口排序的新型前置任务，以揭示时间序列的内在结构特性，并利用三元组损失优化补丁嵌入空间以增强特征判别能力。在TSB-AD基准上的大量实验表明，所提出的PaAno在单变量和多变量任务上均达到了最先进的检测精度，在VUS-PR等评估指标上相比原始PaAno取得了显著的性能提升。凭借紧凑的网络设计，该模型实现了良好的计算效率，能够在资源受限的终端上部署以进行实时异常推断。

</details>


### [200] [What Makes Effective Supervision in Latent Chain-of-Thought: An Information-Theoretic Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20075)
*Xinghao Chen,Chak Tou Leong,Wenjin Guo,Jian Wang,Wenjie Li,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从信息论角度分析潜在思维链（Latent CoT），指出其失败源于双重崩溃（梯度衰减与表征漂移），将过程监督分解为轨迹监督和空间监督两个维度，提出统一潜在探针（ULP）量化潜在轨迹与显式推理步骤之间的互信息，实验表明推理准确率与信息保真度之间存在信息-性能绑定关系。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 潜在CoT虽然通过连续隐状态内化推理过程，避免了冗长的离散推理痕迹，但结果监督提供的学习信号微弱，导致潜在轨迹容易发生语义漂移，鲁棒的潜在推理仍然困难。本文旨在从理论上分析该失败原因并寻找有效的监督策略。

Method: (1) 从信息论视角将潜在CoT的失败归因为双重崩溃：优化路径上的梯度衰减和潜在空间中的表征漂移；(2) 将过程监督分解为两个互补维度：轨迹监督（注入密集的逐步推理信号）和空间监督（保持潜在流形的语义结构）；(3) 提出统一潜在探针（ULP），用于量化潜在轨迹与显式推理步骤之间的互信息。

Result: (1) 刚性的几何压缩会导致推理空间坍塌，而生成式重建提供了更灵活的语义锚点，能更好地保持信息容量；(2) 推理准确率依赖于潜在链中保留的信息保真度，即存在信息-性能绑定关系。

Conclusion: 这些发现为潜在推理监督提供了原则性框架，建议从几何模仿转向互信息最大化。

Original Abstract: Latent Chain-of-Thought (CoT) internalizes reasoning within continuous hidden states, offering a promising alternative to verbose discrete reasoning traces. However, robust latent reasoning remains difficult because outcome supervision provides weak learning signals and leaves latent trajectories prone to semantic drift. In this work, we analyze Latent CoT from an information-theoretic perspective and identify this failure as a dual collapse: gradient attenuation along the optimization path and representational drift in the latent space. We further decompose process supervision into two complementary dimensions: Trajectory Supervision, which injects dense stepwise reasoning signals, and Space Supervision, which preserves the semantic structure of the latent manifold. Our analysis shows that rigid geometric compression can collapse the reasoning space, whereas generative reconstruction provides a more flexible semantic anchor that better preserves information capacity. To measure these effects, we introduce the Unified Latent Probe (ULP), which quantifies the mutual information between latent trajectories and explicit reasoning steps. Experiments reveal a clear Information-Performance Binding: reasoning accuracy depends on the information fidelity preserved in the latent chain. These findings provide a principled framework for latent reasoning supervision and suggest shifting from geometric imitation toward mutual information maximization. Our code is available at \href{https://github.com/EIT-NLP/Supervision-in-Latent-CoT}{this repository}.

中文摘要翻译: 潜在思维链（Latent Chain-of-Thought, Latent CoT）将推理过程内化到连续隐状态中，为冗长的离散推理痕迹提供了一种有前景的替代方案。然而，鲁棒的潜在推理仍然困难，因为结果监督提供的学习信号微弱，导致潜在轨迹容易发生语义漂移。在本文中，我们从信息论角度分析潜在思维链，并将这种失败归因为双重崩溃：优化路径上的梯度衰减和潜在空间中的表征漂移。我们进一步将过程监督分解为两个互补的维度：轨迹监督（注入密集的逐步推理信号）和空间监督（保持潜在流形的语义结构）。我们的分析表明，刚性的几何压缩会导致推理空间坍塌，而生成式重建则提供了更灵活的语义锚点，能够更好地保持信息容量。为了量化这些影响，我们提出了统一潜在探针（Unified Latent Probe, ULP），用于量化潜在轨迹与显式推理步骤之间的互信息。实验揭示了清晰的信息-性能绑定关系：推理准确率依赖于潜在链中保留的信息保真度。这些发现为潜在推理监督提供了原则性框架，并建议从几何模仿转向互信息最大化。我们的代码可在\href{https://github.com/EIT-NLP/Supervision-in-Latent-CoT}{此仓库}获取。

</details>


### [201] [Sensorimotor World Models: Perception for Action via Inverse Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20104)
*Petr Ivashkov,Randall Balestriero,Bernhard Schölkopf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出传感器运动世界模型（SMWM），通过逆向动力学正则化端到端训练潜在世界模型，防止表征坍塌并诱导行动对齐的表征，在离线无奖励轨迹上实现稳定的潜在世界模型学习，并在2D/3D控制任务中取得竞争性规划性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有JEPA风格的潜在世界模型在端到端训练时容易因仅追求潜在状态的可预测性而导致表征坍塌；同时，感知应服务于行动，但现有方法未能有效将表征与行动对齐。需要一种既能防止表征坍塌又能诱导行动对齐表征的机制。

Method: 提出传感器运动世界模型（SMWM），在潜在世界模型的端到端训练中加入逆向动力学正则化项。该正则化强制潜在状态保留转移背后行动的信息，使模型偏向环境中可控的自由度，同时丢弃不可控的干扰因素，从而防止表征坍塌并诱导行动对齐的表征。无需冻结编码器、指数移动平均或复杂的潜在正则化器。

Result: 实验表明，SMWM能够学习紧凑、可解释的潜在空间，并在简单的2D和3D控制任务中实现具有竞争力的规划性能。训练过程稳定，仅需离线、无奖励的轨迹数据。

Conclusion: 逆向动力学正则化是训练潜在世界模型的有效单一机制，同时解决了表征坍塌和行动对齐两个关键问题，为从离线无奖励数据中学习可用于规划的紧凑世界模型提供了一种简洁实用的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Perception for action suggests that representations of the world should be shaped not by visual fidelity alone, but by their relevance for actions. At the same time, latent JEPA-style world models advocate learning compact predictive states from high-dimensional observations to facilitate the prediction of future states, but end-to-end training of these models is nontrivial because representations may collapse if our only goal is to construct a latent state that is easy to predict. We introduce a sensorimotor world model (SMWM): a latent world model trained end-to-end with inverse dynamics regularization. This single regularizer addresses both issues: it prevents representation collapse and induces action-aligned representations. By forcing latent states to preserve information about the action underlying a transition, it biases the model toward the controllable degrees of freedom of the environment while discarding uncontrollable distractors. This yields stable latent world models trained from offline, reward-free trajectories, without frozen encoders, exponential moving averages, or complex latent regularizers. Empirically, SMWM learns compact, interpretable latent spaces and enables competitive planning performance across simple 2D and 3D control tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 行动导向的感知表明，世界表征不应仅由视觉保真度塑造，而应取决于它们与行动的相关性。与此同时，潜在JEPA风格的世界模型主张从高维观测中学习紧凑的预测状态，以促进未来状态的预测，但这些模型的端到端训练并非易事，因为如果我们唯一的目标是构建一个易于预测的潜在状态，表征可能会坍塌。我们引入了传感器运动世界模型（SMWM）：一种通过逆向动力学正则化进行端到端训练的潜在世界模型。这一单一正则化器同时解决了两个问题：它防止了表征坍塌并诱导了行动对齐的表征。通过强制潜在状态保留转移背后行动的信息，该模型偏向于环境中可控的自由度，同时丢弃不可控的干扰因素。这使得我们能够在离线、无奖励的轨迹上训练出稳定的潜在世界模型，无需冻结编码器、指数移动平均或复杂的潜在正则化器。实验表明，SMWM学习了紧凑、可解释的潜在空间，并在简单的2D和3D控制任务中实现了具有竞争力的规划性能。

</details>


### [202] [Quantile of Means: A Bonus-Free Ensemble Method for Minimax Optimal Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20107)
*Asaf Cassel,Aviv Rosenberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分位数的集成方法，用于有限时域马尔可夫决策过程，在不使用计数的情况下实现了最优方差相关的遗憾界，为基于集成的探索在强化学习中提供了理论依据。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的最优强化学习算法通常依赖基于计数的不确定性估计来驱动探索，虽然理论上合理，但在实际场景中难以计算；而集成方法虽在实践中有效，却缺乏理论支撑。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 基于最近针对多臂老虎机的集成方法，提出了一种面向有限时域MDPs的基于分位数的集成方法。该方法无需计数，通过分位数估计来驱动探索。

Result: 该方法实现了最优方差相关的遗憾界，为基于集成的探索在强化学习中提供了理论依据。

Conclusion: 本文提出的无计数、基于分位数的集成方法在理论上达到了最优性能，证明了集成探索在强化学习中的有效性。

Original Abstract: Optimal Reinforcement Learning (RL) algorithms typically rely on carefully constructed count-based uncertainty estimates to drive exploration. Although theoretically sound, such estimates are hard to compute in practical settings and therefore offer limited insight for designing exploration heuristics. Meanwhile, ensembling has emerged as a practical approach, but remains without theoretical justification. Building on a recent ensemble-based method for Multi-Armed Bandits, we propose a quantile-based ensemble method for finite-horizon Markov Decision Processes (MDPs). Our simple count-free approach achieves optimal variance-dependent regret bounds, providing theoretical grounding for ensemble-based exploration in RL.

中文摘要翻译: 最优强化学习算法通常依赖精心构建的基于计数的不确定性估计来驱动探索。尽管在理论上合理，但这些估计在实际设置中难以计算，因此对设计探索启发式方法的指导有限。与此同时，集成方法已成为一种实用方法，但一直缺乏理论依据。基于最近一种针对多臂老虎机的集成方法，我们提出了一种面向有限时域马尔可夫决策过程（MDPs）的基于分位数的集成方法。这种简单的无计数方法实现了最优方差相关的遗憾界，为强化学习中的基于集成的探索提供了理论基础。

</details>


### [203] [When Calibration Fails the Vulnerable Hospital: Federated Conformal Risk Control via Risk-Curve Shrinkage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20115)
*Nafis Fuad Shahid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于收缩的联邦保形风险控制协议，在联邦医学图像分割场景中，解决了朴素池化CRC导致部分机构覆盖违规、以及本地CRC导致预测集膨胀83倍的问题。通过仅传输经验风险曲线，由服务器计算收缩正则化阈值，单个超参数n0平滑权衡最坏情况覆盖与预测集效率，在FeTS-2022数据集上达到2.7/20违规、2.0倍膨胀。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 联邦部署中标准保形风险控制(CRC)在所有站点池化校准分数设定单一阈值，但实验表明这种做法虽保护平均医院，却导致40%个体机构覆盖违规（最差站点假阴性率超出目标7.8个百分点）；而本地CRC虽恢复覆盖但预测集膨胀83倍，临床不可用。因此需要一种既保护个体机构覆盖又维持预测集效率的联邦CRC方案。

Method: 提出基于收缩的联邦CRC协议：(1) 每个站点仅上传其经验风险曲线（G个标量）至服务器；(2) 服务器计算收缩正则化阈值，通过单个超参数n0平滑权衡最坏情况覆盖与预测集效率；(3) 通过留一站点敏感性分析确定n0=19。该方法保留了边际CRC保证（在站点混合假设下），且无需传输患者级图像、掩膜或每体素评分。

Result: 在真实多机构脑肿瘤数据(FeTS-2022, 1251名受试者, 20个机构)上：(1) 朴素池化CRC在40%个体机构违规，最差站点假阴性率超目标7.8个百分点；(2) 本地CRC膨胀83倍；(3) 所提方法实现2.7/20违规，仅2.0倍膨胀；(4) 直接拉格朗日优化覆盖预算失败，集中风险于脆弱医院；(5) 有限样本校正项不可或缺，移除后违规数增加三倍。

Conclusion: 所提联邦CRC协议在保持边际CRC保证的前提下，有效平衡了个体机构覆盖与预测集效率，为联邦医学图像分割提供了一种实用且保护隐私的保形风险控制方案。有限样本校正项和超参数选择是关键组件。

Original Abstract: Conformal risk control (CRC) provides distribution-free guarantees on segmentation quality by calibrating a prediction-set threshold on held-out data. In federated deployments, the standard approach pools calibration scores across sites into a single threshold. We provide the first quantification, on real multi-institutional brain tumor data (FeTS-2022, 1,251 subjects, 20 institutions), showing that this naive pooled CRC protects the average hospital but violates coverage at 40% of individual institutions, with the worst site exceeding the target false-negative rate by 7.8 percentage points. The naive alternative, per-site local CRC, largely restores coverage but inflates prediction sets by 83x, rendering them clinically useless. We propose a shrinkage-based federated CRC protocol: each site transmits only its empirical risk curve (G scalars) to a server, which computes a shrinkage-regularized threshold per site. A single hyperparameter n0 smoothly trades worst-case coverage for prediction-set efficiency; leave-one-site-out sensitivity analysis identifies n0=19, achieving 2.7/20 violations at 2.0x stretch. We further show that direct Lagrangian optimization of coverage budgets fails, concentrating risk on vulnerable hospitals, and that the finite-sample correction term is essential: removing it triples violations. The marginal CRC guarantee is preserved by construction under the stated site-mixture assumption; per-site coverage is validated across four targets with three seeds. No patient-level images, masks, or per-volume scores leave any site.

中文摘要翻译: 保形风险控制通过在校准数据上设定预测集阈值，为分割质量提供无分布假设的保证。在联邦部署中，标准做法是将各站点的校准分数合并为单一阈值。我们首次在真实多机构脑肿瘤数据（FeTS-2022，1251名受试者，20个机构）上进行量化分析，表明这种朴素的池化CRC虽然保护了平均医院，但导致40%的个体机构覆盖违规，最差站点假阴性率超出目标7.8个百分点。朴素替代方案——每站点本地CRC——虽大幅恢复覆盖，但将预测集膨胀83倍，使其临床无用。我们提出基于收缩的联邦CRC协议：每个站点仅向服务器传输其经验风险曲线（G个标量），服务器据此计算每个站点的收缩正则化阈值。单个超参数n0平滑权衡最坏情况覆盖与预测集效率；留一站点敏感性分析确定n0=19，实现2.7/20违规，2.0倍膨胀。我们进一步表明，直接拉格朗日优化覆盖预算会失败，将风险集中于脆弱医院，且有限样本校正项至关重要：移除它会使违规数增加三倍。在所述站点混合假设下，边际CRC保证通过构造得以保留；我们使用三组随机种子在四个目标上验证了每站点覆盖。没有患者级图像、掩膜或每体素评分离开任何站点。

</details>


### [204] [Multi-Modal Contrastive Learning for Implicit Earth Embeddings via Location Tying](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20167)
*Jonathan Hecht,Lukas Arzoumanidis,Ziyue Li,Youness Dehbi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了两种多模态对比学习架构（MELT和SALT），利用未配对的地理空间数据将对比学习从双模态扩展到多模态，但发现定位编码器是主要瓶颈——无论模态多样性或预训练量如何，对比目标都会提前达到峰值。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 空间预测任务常因缺乏高质量标注地面真值观测数据而受限，自监督预训练（尤其是对比学习）是解决方案，但现有方法通常仅将地理坐标与单一额外模态对齐，缺乏多模态融合的研究。

Method: 提出两种多模态对比学习架构：MELT（通过位置绑定进行多模态嵌入）和SALT（序列交替位置训练），利用未配对的多种地理空间数据将对比学习框架从双模态扩展到多模态。

Result: 两种方法在四个下游任务上均达到与最强双模态基线（SATCLIP）相当的性能；但增加模态数量并不能持续提升性能，表明定位编码器是主要限制因素——对比目标在训练早期便达到峰值。

Conclusion: MELT比SALT提供更稳定的训练，并具备更好的未来扩展潜力；当前多模态对比学习的瓶颈在于定位编码器本身，而非模态数量或数据量。

Original Abstract: Spatial prediction tasks are often limited by a lack of high-quality labelled ground-truth observations. To overcome this challenge, self-supervised pre-training is a possible solution, with contrastive learning dominant for location encoders. Those approaches usually align geographic coordinates with just one additional modality. We propose two multimodal contrastive learning architectures: Multimodal Embedding via Location Tying (MELT) and Sequential Alternating Location Training (SALT). These architectures expand this framework beyond two modalities by utilising unpaired geospatial data. Both methods are technically viable and match the performance of the strongest two-modality baseline (SATCLIP) across four downstream tasks. However, increasing the number of modalities does not consistently improve performance, suggesting that the chosen location encoder is the main limitation - the contrastive objective reaches its peak early, regardless of modality diversity or pre-training volume. MELT provides more stable training than SALT and presents a stronger foundation for future scaling.

中文摘要翻译: 空间预测任务常因缺乏高质量标注地面真值观测数据而受限。为克服这一挑战，自监督预训练是一种可能的解决方案，其中对比学习在位置编码器中占据主导地位。这些方法通常仅将地理坐标与一种额外模态对齐。我们提出两种多模态对比学习架构：通过位置绑定进行多模态嵌入（MELT）和序列交替位置训练（SALT）。这些架构利用未配对的地理空间数据，将对比学习框架从双模态扩展到多模态。两种方法在技术上均可行，并在四个下游任务上与最强双模态基线（SATCLIP）性能相当。然而，增加模态数量并不能持续提升性能，这表明所选的位置编码器是主要限制——无论模态多样性或预训练数据量如何，对比目标在训练早期便达到峰值。MELT比SALT提供更稳定的训练，并为未来的扩展提供了更坚实的基础。

</details>


### [205] [Predicting gestational age at birth in the context of preterm birth from multi-modal fetal MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20172)
*Diego Fajardo-Rojas,Megan Hall,Daniel Cromb,Mary A. Rutherford,Lisa Story,Emma C. Robinson,Jana Hutter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文开发并评估了一个结合定制机器学习方法（数据插补、特征选择、回归模型）的流水线，利用多模态胎儿MRI数据预测出生时的胎龄（GA），将预测结果分类为足月和早产，并在333例对照和93例早产数据上取得了R²=0.13、MAE=2.74周、准确率0.77、灵敏度0.59、特异性0.82的结果。关键特征包括宫颈长度和胎盘T2*值。这是首次将早产预测作为回归问题处理。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 早产与高死亡率及终身发病风险相关，但其复杂的多因素病因导致预测困难，从而难以实现最优护理。现有文献仅将早产作为分类问题处理，缺乏回归预测方法，因此需要开发能够预测具体出生胎龄的机器学习方法。

Method: 构建了一个包含定制机器学习方法的流水线：数据插补（处理缺失值）、特征选择（筛选重要特征）和回归模型（预测出生时胎龄）。使用333例对照和93例早产的多模态形态和功能胎儿MRI数据，将GA预测结果分类为足月和早产，采用分层10折交叉验证评估，并进行了消融研究验证流水线设计。

Result: 流水线在GA回归预测上达到R²=0.13、MAE=2.74周；分类性能上准确率0.77、灵敏度0.59、特异性0.82。最关键的特征是宫颈长度和基于胎盘T2*值的统计量。

Conclusion: 快速、运动鲁棒的多模态胎儿MRI技术与机器学习预测相结合，能够预测出生时的胎龄。该工作首次将早产预测作为回归问题而非分类问题处理，提供了概念验证。未来将扩大队列规模以实现早产亚组内的更精细分层。

Original Abstract: Preterm birth is associated with significant mortality and a risk for lifelong morbidity. The complex multifactorial aetiology hampers accurate prediction and thus optimal care. A pipeline consisting of bespoke machine learning methods for data imputation, feature selection, and regression models to predict gestational age (GA) at birth was developed and evaluated from comprehensive multi-modal morphological and functional fetal MRI data from 333 control cases and 93 preterm birth cases. The GA at birth predictions were classified into term and preterm categories and their accuracy, sensitivity, and specificity were reported. An ablation study was performed to further validate the design of the pipeline. Performance was evaluated using stratified 10-fold cross-validation. The pipeline achieves an R2 score of 0.13 and a mean absolute error of 2.74 weeks. It also achieves a 0.77 accuracy, 0.59 sensitivity, and 0.82 specificity across folds. The predominant features selected by the pipeline include cervical length and statistics derived from placental T2* values. The confluence of fast, motion-robust and multi-modal fetal MRI techniques and machine learning prediction allowed the prediction of the gestation at birth. This information is essential for any pregnancy. To the best of our knowledge, preterm birth had only been addressed as a classification problem in the literature. Therefore, this work provides a proof of concept. Future work will increase the cohort size to allow for finer stratification within the preterm birth cohort. Our code is available at https://github.com/dfajardorojas/ml-for-preterm-birth-.

中文摘要翻译: 早产与显著的死亡率和终身发病风险相关。其复杂的多因素病因学阻碍了准确的预测，进而影响了最优护理。本研究开发并评估了一个包含定制机器学习方法（用于数据插补、特征选择和回归模型）的流水线，用于预测出生时的胎龄（GA）。该流水线基于来自333例对照和93例早产病例的综合性多模态形态和功能胎儿MRI数据。将出生时胎龄的预测结果分类为足月和早产类别，并报告了其准确率、灵敏度和特异性。进行了消融研究以进一步验证流水线的设计。使用分层10折交叉验证评估性能。该流水线实现了R²分数为0.13，平均绝对误差为2.74周。在交叉验证中，准确率达到0.77，灵敏度为0.59，特异性为0.82。流水线选择的主要特征包括宫颈长度和基于胎盘T2*值的统计量。快速、运动鲁棒的多模态胎儿MRI技术与机器学习预测的结合，使得预测出生时的胎龄成为可能。这一信息对任何妊娠都至关重要。据我们所知，文献中早产仅被作为分类问题处理。因此，本研究提供了概念验证。未来工作将扩大队列规模，以便在早产群体中进行更精细的分层。我们的代码可在https://github.com/dfajardorojas/ml-for-preterm-birth-获取。

</details>


### [206] [Computational Methods and Challenges in Cell-Free DNA Analysis for Multi-Cancer Early Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20174)
*Nicko Starkey,Marcin W. Wojewodzic,Krzysztof Rzecki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 综述了2022-2025年间基于cfDNA片段组学和表观遗传特征进行多癌种早期检测（MCED）的计算方法，涵盖经典统计/机器学习与深度学习框架，指出多模态集成方法最具临床整合潜力，但评估标准化至关重要。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: cfDNA有望实现无创多癌种早期检测，尤其适用于目前缺乏有效筛查方案的癌症，但相关计算方法进展迅速，亟需系统梳理和比较，以推动临床转化。
